'use client'

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import { isSupabaseConfigured } from '@/lib/supabase/config'

export type SyncDomain =
  | 'holidays'
  | 'schedule'
  | 'schedules'
  | 'incidents'
  | 'users'
  | 'infrastructure'
  | 'logs'
  | 'settings'
  | 'workstations'
  | 'assistance'

const BROADCAST_CHANNEL_NAME = 'labsync-realtime-bus'

// Broadcast a sync event across all tabs and connected clients
export function broadcastSync(domain: SyncDomain, payload?: any) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return

  const timestamp = Date.now()
  const eventKey = `labsync_event_${domain}`

  // 1. BroadcastChannel API for instantaneous modern cross-tab messaging
  try {
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined') {
      const bc = new BroadcastChannel(BROADCAST_CHANNEL_NAME)
      bc.postMessage({ domain, payload, timestamp })
      bc.close()
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // 2. Local storage event (triggers 'storage' in all other browser tabs as fallback)
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(
      eventKey,
      JSON.stringify({ domain, payload, timestamp, rand: Math.random() })
    )
  } catch (e) {}

  // 3. Local window event (triggers listeners in the current tab immediately)
  window.dispatchEvent(
    new CustomEvent(`labsync:${domain}`, {
      detail: { domain, payload, timestamp },
    })
  )

  // 3. Supabase Realtime Broadcast (syncs to other active browsers/devices over the web)
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = createClient()
      const channel = supabase.channel(BROADCAST_CHANNEL_NAME)
      if (typeof (channel as any).httpSend === 'function') {
        ;(channel as any)
          .httpSend(domain, { domain, payload, timestamp })
          .catch(() => {})
      } else {
        channel
          .send({
            type: 'broadcast',
            event: domain,
            payload: { domain, payload, timestamp },
          })
          .catch(() => {})
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Realtime broadcast warning:', err)
    }
  }
}

// React hook / listener for auto-updating components when changes occur anywhere
export function subscribeToSync(
  domain: SyncDomain | 'all',
  callback: (data: { domain: string; payload?: any; timestamp: number }) => void
) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {}

  // Handle local window events
  const handleLocal = (e: Event) => {
    const custom = e as CustomEvent
    callback(custom.detail)
  }

  // Handle cross-tab storage events
  const handleStorage = (e: StorageEvent) => {
    if (e.key && e.key.startsWith('labsync_event_')) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(e.newValue || '{}')
        if (domain === 'all' || parsed.domain === domain) {
          callback(parsed)
        }
      } catch (err) {}
    }
  }

  const eventName = domain === 'all' ? 'labsync:all' : `labsync:${domain}`
  window.addEventListener(eventName, handleLocal)
  window.addEventListener('storage', handleStorage)

  // Handle BroadcastChannel cross-tab events
  let bc: BroadcastChannel | null = null
  try {
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined') {
      bc = new BroadcastChannel(BROADCAST_CHANNEL_NAME)
      bc.onmessage = (ev) => {
        if (ev.data && (domain === 'all' || ev.data.domain === domain)) {
          callback(ev.data)
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // Supabase Realtime subscription
  let supabaseChannel: any = null
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = createClient()
      supabaseChannel = supabase
        .channel(BROADCAST_CHANNEL_NAME)
        .on('broadcast', { event: domain === 'all' ? '*' : domain }, (payload) => {
          if (payload?.payload) {
            callback(payload.payload)
          }
        })
        .subscribe()
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Realtime subscribe warning:', err)
    }
  }

  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener(eventName, handleLocal)
    window.removeEventListener('storage', handleStorage)
    if (bc) {
      try {
        bc.close()
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (supabaseChannel) {
      try {
        const supabase = createClient()
        supabase.removeChannel(supabaseChannel)
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\time-simulator.ts ===
'use client'

import { broadcastSync } from '@/lib/sync-bus'

/**
 * ⏱️ LabSync Time Simulator & Dev Time Travel Engine
 * Enables seamless simulation of academic routine periods, weeks, days,
 * and custom dates without snapping back to current real-world week.
 */

const STORAGE_KEY = 'lmr_simulated_time'
export const TIME_TRAVEL_EVENT = 'lmr-time-travel-change'

interface StoredSimTime {
  baseTime: number // Timestamp in ms
  setAt: number    // Real Date.now() when baseTime was set
}

/**
 * Retrieves the currently effective Date.
 * If time simulation is active, returns the simulated timestamp plus real elapsed milliseconds
 * so clocks and interval countdowns tick naturally.
 */
export function getEffectiveDate(): Date {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return new Date()
  }

  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
    if (!raw) {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.cookie.includes('lmr_simulated_time=')) {
        document.cookie = 'lmr_simulated_time=; path=/; max-age=0; SameSite=Lax'
      }
      return new Date()
    }

    const parsed: StoredSimTime = JSON.parse(raw)
    if (!parsed || !parsed.baseTime || !parsed.setAt) {
      return new Date()
    }

    // Keep cookie synchronized with localStorage
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && !document.cookie.includes('lmr_simulated_time=')) {
      document.cookie = `lmr_simulated_time=${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(parsed))}; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`
    }

    const elapsed = Date.now() - parsed.setAt
    return new Date(parsed.baseTime + elapsed)
  } catch {
    return new Date()
  }
}

/**
 * Checks if the system is currently in simulated time mode.
 */
export function isSimulatingTime(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) !== null
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

/**
 * Sets a new simulated target Date.
 * Pass `null` to reset back to live real-world system clock.
 */
export function setSimulatedTime(targetDate: Date | null): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return

  try {
    if (!targetDate) {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY)
      document.cookie = 'lmr_simulated_time=; path=/; max-age=0; SameSite=Lax'
    } else {
      const payload: StoredSimTime = {
        baseTime: targetDate.getTime(),
        setAt: Date.now(),
      }
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload))
      document.cookie = `lmr_simulated_time=${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(payload))}; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`
    }

    // Broadcast across all open browser windows and tabs
    broadcastSync('schedules', { simulatedTime: targetDate ? targetDate.getTime() : null })
    broadcastSync('workstations', { reason: 'time-travel' })
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(TIME_TRAVEL_EVENT))
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to set simulated time:', e)
  }
}

/**
 * Step simulated time by minutes.
 */
export function stepSimulatedMinutes(minutes: number): void {
  const current = getEffectiveDate()
  const updated = new Date(current.getTime() + minutes * 60 * 1000)
  setSimulatedTime(updated)
}

/**
 * Step simulated time by days (preserving current hour & minute).
 */
export function stepSimulatedDays(days: number): void {
  const current = getEffectiveDate()
  const updated = new Date(current)
  updated.setDate(updated.getDate() + days)
  setSimulatedTime(updated)
}

/**
 * Step simulated time by weeks (preserving day of week, hour & minute).
 */
export function stepSimulatedWeeks(weeks: number): void {
  stepSimulatedDays(weeks * 7)
}

/**
 * Jumps to a specific day of the week (0 = Sun .. 6 = Sat)
 * WITHIN THE CURRENTLY ACTIVE SIMULATED WEEK (does not snap back to real system date).
 */
export function jumpToDayInActiveWeek(dayOfWeek: number, hours?: number, minutes?: number): void {
  const current = getEffectiveDate()
  const currentDay = current.getDay()
  const diff = dayOfWeek - currentDay
  const target = new Date(current)
  target.setDate(target.getDate() + diff)
  if (hours !== undefined) target.setHours(hours)
  if (minutes !== undefined) target.setMinutes(minutes)
  setSimulatedTime(target)
}

/**
 * Sets a specific time of day (hours, minutes) while PRESERVING the currently simulated date & week.
 */
export function jumpToTimeOfDay(hours: number, minutes: number): void {
  const current = getEffectiveDate()
  const target = new Date(current)
  target.setHours(hours, minutes, 0, 0)
  setSimulatedTime(target)
}

/**
 * Sets an exact custom date & time string from inputs (e.g. date: "2026-09-24", time: "14:15").
 */
export function setExactDateTime(dateStr: string, timeStr?: string): void {
  if (!dateStr) return
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number)
  const current = getEffectiveDate()
  let h = current.getHours()
  let min = current.getMinutes()

  if (timeStr) {
    const [hPart, mPart] = timeStr.split(':').map(Number)
    if (!isNaN(hPart)) h = hPart
    if (!isNaN(mPart)) min = mPart
  }

  const target = new Date(y, m - 1, d, h, min, 0, 0)
  setSimulatedTime(target)
}

/**
 * Clears any active simulation and restores the live system clock.
 */
export function resetToLiveTime(): void {
  setSimulatedTime(null)
}

import { type ClassValue, clsx } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

export interface ParsedTimeRange {
  startTime: string
  endTime: string
  startMin: number
  endMin: number
  durationMin: number
  isBreak: boolean
}

/**
 * Parses any time slot string (e.g. "10:10 - 11:00", "Period 1 (10:10 - 11:00)", "10:10")
 * into normalized start time, end time, and 24h minute numbers.
 */
export function parseSlotTimeRange(labelOrSlot: string): ParsedTimeRange {
  if (!labelOrSlot) {
    return {
      startTime: '10:10',
      endTime: '11:00',
      startMin: 610,
      endMin: 660,
      durationMin: 50,
      isBreak: false,
    }
  }

  let timeSegment = labelOrSlot
  const parenMatch = labelOrSlot.match(/\(([^)]+)\)/)
  if (parenMatch) {
    timeSegment = parenMatch[1]
  }

  const isBreak = /break|tiffin|lunch|recess/i.test(labelOrSlot)

  const parts = timeSegment.split('-').map((s) => s.trim())
  const startTime = parts[0] || '10:10'
  const endTime = parts[1] || parts[0] || '11:00'

  const toMinutes = (t: string): number => {
    const raw = t.trim()
    const isExplicitPm = /pm/i.test(raw)
    const isExplicitAm = /am/i.test(raw)

    const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^\d:]/g, '')
    const [hStr, mStr] = cleaned.split(':')
    let h = parseInt(hStr, 10)
    const m = parseInt(mStr, 10) || 0

    if (isNaN(h)) return 0

    if (isExplicitPm) {
      if (h < 12) h += 12
    } else if (isExplicitAm) {
      if (h === 12) h = 0
    } else {
      // In academic day-school schedules (starts ~09:00 AM, concludes ~05:00 PM):
      // Afternoon hours 1..7 (e.g. 01:15, 01:45, 02:30, 03:15, 04:05, 04:50) are PM (13:00 to 17:00).
      // Morning hours 8..11 (e.g. 09:15, 10:10, 11:00, 11:45) are AM.
      // Hour 12 (12:30) is Midday (12:00 PM).
      if (h >= 1 && h <= 7) {
        h += 12
      }
    }

    return h * 60 + m
  }

  const startMin = toMinutes(startTime)
  const endMin = toMinutes(endTime)
  const durationMin = Math.max(0, endMin - startMin)

  return {
    startTime,
    endTime,
    startMin,
    endMin,
    durationMin,
    isBreak,
  }
}

/**
 * Validates whether a given string is a standard RFC4122 v1-v5 UUID.
 */
export function isValidUuid(id?: string | null): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(id)
}

/**
 * Parses any time string into 24-hour minutes from midnight using academic timetable semantics.
 */
export function parseTimeToMinutes(timeStr: string): number {
  if (!timeStr) return 0
  const parsed = parseSlotTimeRange(timeStr)
  return parsed.startMin
}

/**
 * Universal Chronological Session Sorter
 * Correctly orders academic timetable slots by slot sequence and 24-hour start time,
 * eliminating 12-hour string sorting bugs where afternoon slots (e.g. 01:45 PM) appear before morning slots (10:10 AM).
 */
export function sortSessionsChronologically<T>(items: T[]): T[] {
  const slotOrder: Record<string, number> = {
    t1: 1, // Pre-Period (09:15 - 10:00)
    t2: 2, // Period 1 (10:10 - 11:00)
    t3: 3, // Period 2 (11:00 - 11:45)
    t4: 4, // Period 3 (11:45 - 12:30)
    t5: 5, // Period 4 (12:30 - 01:15)
    t6: 6, // Break / Tiffin (01:15 - 01:45)
    t7: 7, // Period 5 (01:45 - 02:30)
    t8: 8, // Period 6 (02:30 - 03:15)
    t9: 9, // Period 7 (03:15 - 04:05)
    t10: 10, // Period 8 (04:05 - 04:50)
  }

  return [...items].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
    const slotA = a.slot_id || a.slotId || ''
    const slotB = b.slot_id || b.slotId || ''
    const orderA = slotOrder[slotA] || 0
    const orderB = slotOrder[slotB] || 0

    if (orderA && orderB && orderA !== orderB) {
      return orderA - orderB
    }

    const timeA = parseTimeToMinutes(a.start_time || a.startTime || a.timeSlot || '')
    const timeB = parseTimeToMinutes(b.start_time || b.startTime || b.timeSlot || '')

    if (timeA !== timeB) {
      return timeA - timeB
    }

    // Tie-breaker: lab name / code (stable natural ordering)
    const labA = a.lab || a.lab_id || ''
    const labB = b.lab || b.lab_id || ''
    if (labA !== labB) {
      return labA.localeCompare(labB)
    }

    const subA = a.subject_name || a.subjectCode || ''
    const subB = b.subject_name || b.subjectCode || ''
    return subA.localeCompare(subB)
  })
}

import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'
import { recordAuditEvent } from '@/lib/audit'
import type {
  AuditAction,
  AuditEntityType,
  AuditLogItem,
  AuditLogFilterParams,
  AuditLogResponse,
  LogAuditEventParams,
} from '@/types/audit'

export type {
  AuditAction,
  AuditEntityType,
  AuditLogItem,
  AuditLogFilterParams,
  AuditLogResponse,
  LogAuditEventParams,

/**
 * 1. Record Attendance Override / Roll-Call Flip
 */
export async function recordAttendanceOverrideAudit(params: {
  practicalLogId: string
  studentName: string
  rollNumber: number

  afterPresent: boolean
  reason?: string
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; id?: string }> {

    action: 'OVERRIDE',
    entityType: 'attendance',
    entityId: params.practicalLogId,

/**
 * 5. Query Audit Ledger with Optional Filtering & Pagination
 */
export async function queryAuditLedger(
  filters: AuditLogFilterParams = {}
): Promise<AuditLogResponse> {
  const { entityType, action, search, page = 1, limit = 25 } = filters
  const offset = Math.max(0, (page - 1) * limit)

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const whereClauses: string[] = ['1=1']
  const queryParams: any[] = []
  let paramIndex = 1

  if (entityType && entityType !== 'all') {
    whereClauses.push(`entity_type = $${paramIndex++}`)
    queryParams.push(entityType)
  }

  if (action && action !== 'all') {
    whereClauses.push(`action = $${paramIndex++}`)
    queryParams.push(action)
  }

  if (search && search.trim()) {
    const searchPattern = `%${search.trim()}%`
    whereClauses.push(
      `(entity_label ILIKE $${paramIndex} OR actor_name ILIKE $${paramIndex} OR COALESCE(entity_id, '') ILIKE $${paramIndex})`
    )
    queryParams.push(searchPattern)
    paramIndex++
  }

  const whereSql = whereClauses.join(' AND ')

  const countRes = await pool.query(
    `SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS total FROM public.audit_logs WHERE ${whereSql}`,
    queryParams
  )
  const totalCount = countRes.rows[0]?.total || 0

  const dataParams = [...queryParams, limit, offset]
  const dataRes = await pool.query(
    `
    SELECT 
      id,
      created_at,
      actor_id,
      actor_name,
      actor_role,
      action,
      entity_type,
      entity_id,
      entity_label,
      changes,
      metadata
    FROM public.audit_logs
    WHERE ${whereSql}
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    LIMIT $${paramIndex++} OFFSET $${paramIndex++};
    `,
    dataParams
  )

  const logs: AuditLogItem[] = dataRes.rows.map((row) => ({
    id: row.id,
    created_at: row.created_at ? new Date(row.created_at).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
    actor_id: row.actor_id,
    actor_name: row.actor_name,
    actor_role: row.actor_role,
    action: row.action as AuditAction,
    entity_type: row.entity_type as AuditEntityType,
    entity_id: row.entity_id,
    entity_label: row.entity_label,
    changes: row.changes,
    metadata: row.metadata || {},
  }))

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalCount / limit))

  return {
    logs,
    totalCount,
    page,
    totalPages,
    hasMore: page < totalPages,
  }
}

/**
 * 6. Get Audit History for a Specific Entity ID
 */
export async function getAuditHistoryForEntity(
  entityType: string,
  entityId: string
): Promise<AuditLogItem[]> {
  const pool = getPgPool()
  const res = await pool.query(
    `
    SELECT 
      id,
      created_at,
      actor_id,
      actor_name,
      actor_role,
      action,
      entity_type,
      entity_id,
      entity_label,
      changes,
      metadata
    FROM public.audit_logs
    WHERE entity_type = $1 AND entity_id = $2
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    LIMIT 50;
    `,
    [entityType, entityId]
  )

  return res.rows.map((row) => ({
    id: row.id,
    created_at: row.created_at ? new Date(row.created_at).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
    actor_id: row.actor_id,
    actor_name: row.actor_name,
    actor_role: row.actor_role,
    action: row.action as AuditAction,
    entity_type: row.entity_type as AuditEntityType,
    entity_id: row.entity_id,
    entity_label: row.entity_label,
    changes: row.changes,
    metadata: row.metadata || {},
  }))
}

'use client'

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'
import { getAcademicYears } from '@/app/actions/academic-years'
import type { AcademicYear } from '@/lib/types/academic-years'
import { AlertTriangle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'

interface AcademicYearContextType {
  systemActiveYear: string
  systemActiveYearName: string
  userViewingYear: string
  setUserViewingYear: (yearId: string) => void
  isViewingArchived: boolean
  academicYears: AcademicYear[]
  resetToActiveYear: () => void
  isLoading: boolean
}

const AcademicYearContext = createContext<AcademicYearContextType>({
  systemActiveYear: '2083',
  systemActiveYearName: '2083 B.S.',
  userViewingYear: '2083',
  setUserViewingYear: () => {},
  isViewingArchived: false,
  academicYears: [],
  resetToActiveYear: () => {},
  isLoading: true,
})

export function AcademicYearProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [academicYears, setAcademicYears] = useState<AcademicYear[]>([])
  const [systemActiveYear, setSystemActiveYear] = useState<string>('2083')
  const [systemActiveYearName, setSystemActiveYearName] = useState<string>('2083 B.S.')
  const [userViewingYear, setUserViewingYearState] = useState<string>('2083')
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(true)

  // Load active and configured academic years on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadYears() {
      try {
        const res = await getAcademicYears()
        if (res.success && res.data && res.data.length > 0) {
          setAcademicYears(res.data)
          const current = res.data.find((y) => y.is_current)
          if (current) {
            setSystemActiveYear(current.id)
            setSystemActiveYearName(current.name)
            setUserViewingYearState(current.id)
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load academic cycles in provider:', err)
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false)
      }
    }
    loadYears()
  }, [])

  const setUserViewingYear = useCallback((yearId: string) => {
    setUserViewingYearState(yearId)
  }, [])

  const resetToActiveYear = useCallback(() => {
    setUserViewingYearState(systemActiveYear)
  }, [systemActiveYear])

  const isViewingArchived = useMemo(() => {
    return userViewingYear !== systemActiveYear
  }, [userViewingYear, systemActiveYear])

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      systemActiveYear,
      systemActiveYearName,
      userViewingYear,
      setUserViewingYear,
      isViewingArchived,
      academicYears,
      resetToActiveYear,
      isLoading,
    }),
    [
      systemActiveYear,
      systemActiveYearName,
      userViewingYear,
      setUserViewingYear,
      isViewingArchived,
      academicYears,
      resetToActiveYear,
      isLoading,
    ]
  )

  return (
    <AcademicYearContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AcademicYearContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useAcademicYear() {
  return useContext(AcademicYearContext)
}

/**
 * Ambient banner alerting the user when viewing an archived historical year
 */
export function HistoricalArchiveShield() {
  const { isViewingArchived, userViewingYear, academicYears, systemActiveYearName, resetToActiveYear } = useAcademicYear()

  if (!isViewingArchived) return null

  const viewingYearObj = academicYears.find((y) => y.id === userViewingYear)
  const viewingName = viewingYearObj ? viewingYearObj.name : `${userViewingYear} B.S.`

  return (
    <div className="bg-amber-500/10 border-b border-amber-500/20 px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between text-amber-400 text-xs font-medium animate-in slide-in-from-top-1 duration-150 select-none">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-400" />
        <span>
          Viewing Historical Archive: <strong className="font-bold text-amber-300">{viewingName}</strong> (Historical Read-Only Mode)
        </span>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={resetToActiveYear}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-200 text-[11px] font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer"
      >
        <RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" />
        <span>Switch to Active Cycle ({systemActiveYearName})</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\context\cascading-filters.ts ===
import { LAB_ROOMS, DEFAULT_CLASSES, DEFAULT_SUBJECTS } from '@/lib/master-data'
import {
  INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS,
  getTeacherAssignments,
  matchesGrade,
} from '@/lib/context/institutional-relationships'
import { UserScopeContext } from '@/lib/context/user-scope'

export interface CascadingSelections {
  labId?: string
  classBatch?: string
  subjectCode?: string
  teacherId?: string
}

export interface CascadingOptions {
  validLabs: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>
  validClasses: Array<{ id: string; name: string; fullName: string }>
  validSubjects: Array<{ code: string; title: string; grade: string; labId: string; labName: string }>
  validTeachers: Array<{ id: string; name: string; email: string }>
}

export interface CascadingCustomData {
  classes?: Array<{ id: string; name: string; stream?: string; capacity?: number }>
  subjects?: Array<{ code: string; title: string; grade: string; labId?: string; lab?: string; teacherId?: string; teacherName?: string }>
  labs?: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>
  teachers?: Array<{ id: string; name?: string; full_name?: string; email?: string }>
}

/**
 * Pure calculation of dynamic cascading options based on current user scope and active selections.
 */
export function getCascadingOptions(
  scope: UserScopeContext,
  selections: CascadingSelections = {},
  customData?: CascadingCustomData
): CascadingOptions {
  const { labId, classBatch, subjectCode, teacherId } = selections

  // 1. Initial base set based on user role and customData
  let baseTeachers = customData?.teachers && customData.teachers.length > 0
    ? customData.teachers.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, name: t.name || t.full_name || 'Faculty', email: t.email || '' }))
    : scope.isPrivileged
    ? INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS.map((t) => ({ id: t.teacherId, name: t.name, email: t.email }))
    : [{ id: scope.userId || 'self', name: scope.fullName, email: scope.email }]

  let rawLabs = (customData?.labs && customData.labs.length > 0) ? customData.labs : LAB_ROOMS
  let baseLabs = rawLabs.filter((l) => scope.isPrivileged || scope.assignedLabIds.includes(l.id))

  let rawClasses = (customData?.classes && customData.classes.length > 0)
    ? customData.classes.map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        name: c.name,
        fullName: c.stream ? `${c.name} (${c.stream})` : c.name,
      }))
    : DEFAULT_CLASSES.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name, fullName: c.fullName }))
  let baseClasses = rawClasses.filter((c) => scope.isPrivileged || scope.assignedClasses.includes(c.name))

  let rawSubjects = (customData?.subjects && customData.subjects.length > 0)
    ? customData.subjects.map((s) => ({
        code: s.code,
        title: s.title,
        grade: s.grade,
        labId: s.labId || 'comp',
        labName: s.lab || 'Laboratory',
      }))
    : DEFAULT_SUBJECTS.map((s) => ({
        code: s.code,
        title: s.title,
        grade: s.grade,
        labId: s.labId,
        labName: s.lab,
      }))
  let baseSubjects = scope.isPrivileged ? rawSubjects : scope.assignedSubjects

  // 2. Cascade by teacher selection
  const activeTeacherId = (!scope.isPrivileged ? scope.fullName : teacherId) || ''
  if (activeTeacherId && activeTeacherId !== 'all') {
    const teacherData = getTeacherAssignments(activeTeacherId)
    if (teacherData) {
      baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) =>
        teacherData.assignedClasses.some((ac) => matchesGrade(ac, c.name))
      )
      baseSubjects = baseSubjects.filter((s) =>
        teacherData.assignedSubjects.some((ts) => ts.code.toLowerCase() === s.code.toLowerCase())
      )
      baseLabs = baseLabs.filter((l) => teacherData.assignedLabIds.includes(l.id))
    }
  }

  // 3. Cascade by Lab selection
  if (labId && labId !== 'all') {
    const labFilteredSubs = baseSubjects.filter((s) => s.labId === labId)
    if (labFilteredSubs.length > 0) {
      baseSubjects = labFilteredSubs
      baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) =>
        labFilteredSubs.some((s) => matchesGrade(s.grade, c.name))
      )
    }
  }

  // 4. Cascade by Class/Grade selection
  if (classBatch && classBatch !== 'all') {
    const classFilteredSubs = baseSubjects.filter((s) => matchesGrade(s.grade, classBatch))
    if (classFilteredSubs.length > 0) {
      baseSubjects = classFilteredSubs
      const validLabsInClass = new Set(classFilteredSubs.map((s) => s.labId))
      baseLabs = baseLabs.filter((l) => validLabsInClass.has(l.id))
    }
  }

  // 5. Cascade by Subject selection
  if (subjectCode && subjectCode !== 'all') {
    const cleanSubCode = subjectCode.split(/[\s-—]/)[0].toUpperCase()
    const targetSub = baseSubjects.find((s) => s.code.toUpperCase().startsWith(cleanSubCode))
    if (targetSub) {
      baseLabs = baseLabs.filter((l) => l.id === targetSub.labId)
      baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) => matchesGrade(c.name, targetSub.grade))
    }
  }

  return {
    validLabs: baseLabs.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name })),
    validClasses: baseClasses,
    validSubjects: baseSubjects,
    validTeachers: baseTeachers,
  }
}

// Known UUID to Teacher mapping for Supabase Auth profiles
export const PROFILE_TEACHER_MAPPING: Record<string, string> = {
  'f3ad9570-a56c-4727-82b7-b3edd942896c': 't4', // Er. Anish Karki
  'a0110dea-ef2a-4fc1-b60a-6415fcc36bd1': 't2', // Dr. Prakash Adhikari
  '0a8ee514-a58a-41a1-a6a5-c444447b1db9': 't5', // Dr. Nirmala Poudel
  'e094a954-b7ec-4e42-ae36-b41019c0ce81': 't1', // Dr. Rajesh Sharma
}

/**
 * Resolve teacher assignment by any identifier (UUID, internal code, email, or full name)
 */
export function getTeacherAssignments(identifier?: string | null): TeacherAssignment | null {
  if (!identifier) return null
  const cleanId = identifier.trim().toLowerCase()

  // Check known UUID mapping
  if (PROFILE_TEACHER_MAPPING[identifier]) {
    const targetCode = PROFILE_TEACHER_MAPPING[identifier]
    const found = INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS.find((t) => t.teacherId === targetCode)
    if (found) return found
  }

  // Check direct matches
  const match = INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS.find((t) => {
    return (
      t.teacherId.toLowerCase() === cleanId ||
      t.email.toLowerCase() === cleanId ||
      t.name.toLowerCase() === cleanId ||
      cleanId.includes(t.name.toLowerCase()) ||
      t.name.toLowerCase().includes(cleanId)
    )
  })

  if (match) return match

  // CRITICAL SECURITY FIX: Unmatched / unassigned teacher receives null.
  // NEVER fall back to DEFAULT_CLASSES, DEFAULT_SUBJECTS, or LAB_ROOMS.
  return null
}

/**
 * Fuzzy check if two class/grade identifiers belong to the same level / cohort
 * e.g. "12 Eng" matches "12", "Class 12", "12 Eng", "12C", "12 Sc"
 */
export function matchesGrade(classOrGradeA?: string | null, classOrGradeB?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!classOrGradeA || !classOrGradeB) return true
  const normA = classOrGradeA.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^class\s+/i, '')
  const normB = classOrGradeB.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^class\s+/i, '')
  
  if (normA === normB) return true
  if (normA.startsWith(normB) || normB.startsWith(normA)) return true

  // Extract leading digits if present: e.g. "12 Eng" -> "12", "12 Sc" -> "12", "Class 12" -> "12"
  const numA = normA.match(/\d+/)?.[0]
  const numB = normB.match(/\d+/)?.[0]
  if (numA && numB && numA === numB) {
    const isMgtA = normA.includes('mgt') || normA.includes('management')
    const isMgtB = normB.includes('mgt') || normB.includes('management')
    const isEngA = normA.includes('eng') || normA.includes('comp')
    const isEngB = normB.includes('eng') || normB.includes('comp')
    const isScA = normA.includes('sc') || normA.includes('science')
    const isScB = normB.includes('sc') || normB.includes('science')

    // If one is explicitly management and the other is engineering/science, separate them
    if ((isMgtA && (isEngB || isScB)) || (isMgtB && (isEngA || isScA))) {
      return false
    }
    return true
  }

  return false
}

/**
 * Get all subjects for a class/grade
 */
export function getSubjectsForClass(className?: string | null, customSubjects?: any[]) {
  const pool = customSubjects && customSubjects.length > 0 ? customSubjects : DEFAULT_SUBJECTS
  if (!className) return pool
  const matched = pool.filter((s: any) => matchesGrade(s.grade, className))
  return matched.length > 0 ? matched : pool
}

/**
 * Get Lab for a subject code
 */
export function getLabForSubject(subjectCode?: string | null) {
  if (!subjectCode) return null
  const cleanCode = subjectCode.split(/[\s-—]/)[0].toUpperCase()
  const subject = DEFAULT_SUBJECTS.find((s) => s.code.toUpperCase().startsWith(cleanCode))
  if (subject) {
    const room = LAB_ROOMS.find((l) => l.id === subject.labId)
    return {
      labId: subject.labId,
      labName: subject.lab,
      room,
    }
  }
  return null
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\context\server-user-scope.ts ===
import { createClient } from '../supabase/server'
import { isSupabaseConfigured } from '../supabase/config'
import { getCurrentUserProfile } from '@/app/actions/auth'
import { resolveUserScope, type UserScopeContext } from './user-scope'

export type { UserScopeContext }

/**
 * Server-side helper to get the authenticated user's scope context,
 * augmented with real-time class_subjects database assignments.
 * 
 * STRICT APPARATUS / APP ROUTER ISOLATION:
 * This function imports createClient from @/lib/supabase/server which uses 'next/headers'.
 * It must remain strictly in this server-only file and NEVER be bundled into Client Components.
 */
export async function getServerUserScope(): Promise<UserScopeContext> {
  const profile = await getCurrentUserProfile()
  const baseScope = resolveUserScope(profile)

  // For teachers, query live class_subjects database records directly from PostgreSQL
  // Deprecating hardcoded INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS fallback when database is configured
  if (profile && profile.role === 'teacher') {
    try {
      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
        const supabase = await createClient()
        const { data: dbAssignments, error: dbErr } = await supabase
          .from('class_subjects')
          .select(`
            class_id,
            subject_id,
            default_lab_id,
            classes:class_id(name),
            subjects:subject_id(code, name)
          `)
          .eq('teacher_id', profile.id)

        if (!dbErr) {
          const assignedClasses = new Set<string>()
          const assignedLabs = new Set<string>()
          const assignedSubjects: UserScopeContext['assignedSubjects'] = []

          for (const row of (dbAssignments || []) as any[]) {
            const className = row.classes?.name || row.class_id
            if (className) assignedClasses.add(className)
            if (row.default_lab_id) assignedLabs.add(row.default_lab_id)
            if (row.subjects) {
              const code = row.subjects.code || row.subject_id
              if (!assignedSubjects.some((s) => s.code === code && s.grade === className)) {
                assignedSubjects.push({
                  code,
                  title: row.subjects.name || code,
                  grade: className,
                  labId: row.default_lab_id || 'comp',
                  labName: 'Laboratory',
                })
              }
            }
          }

          return {
            ...baseScope,
            assignedClasses: Array.from(assignedClasses),
            assignedLabIds: Array.from(assignedLabs),
            assignedSubjects,
            teacherProfile: null, // Deprecated INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS in favor of canonical PostgreSQL truth
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Error querying teacher scope from class_subjects:', err)
    }
  }

  return baseScope
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\context\user-scope.ts ===
import type { UserProfile, UserRole } from '../permissions'
import {
  getTeacherAssignments,
  type TeacherAssignment,
  INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS,
} from './institutional-relationships'
import { LAB_ROOMS, DEFAULT_CLASSES, DEFAULT_SUBJECTS } from '../master-data'

export interface UserScopeContext {
  userId: string
  fullName: string
  email: string
  role: UserRole
  department: string
  isPrivileged: boolean // super_admin, hod, lab_incharge
  isTeacher: boolean
  
  // Scoped Responsibilities:
  assignedLabIds: string[]
  assignedClasses: string[]
  assignedSubjects: Array<{
    code: string
    title: string
    grade: string
    labId: string
    labName: string
    defaultTopic?: string
  }>
  
  // Default and active scoping
  defaultViewMode: 'my_data' | 'institutional'
  teacherProfile: TeacherAssignment | null
}

/**
 * Pure function to calculate user scope from a profile
 */
export function resolveUserScope(profile: UserProfile | null): UserScopeContext {
  if (!profile) {
    // Default guest / unauthenticated scope
    return {
      userId: '',
      fullName: 'Guest User',
      email: '',
      role: 'teacher',
      department: 'Science & Technology',
      isPrivileged: false,
      isTeacher: true,
      assignedLabIds: [],
      assignedClasses: [],
      assignedSubjects: [],
      defaultViewMode: 'my_data',
      teacherProfile: null,
    }
  }

  const role = profile.role
  const isPrivileged = role === 'super_admin' || role === 'hod' || role === 'lab_incharge'
  const isTeacher = role === 'teacher'

  // If user is a teacher or specifically assigned, resolve their curriculum responsibilities
  const teacherAssign = getTeacherAssignments(profile.id) ||
    getTeacherAssignments(profile.email) ||
    getTeacherAssignments(profile.full_name)

  if (isPrivileged) {
    // Privileged users have institutional access across all labs, classes, and subjects
    return {
      userId: profile.id,
      fullName: profile.full_name,
      email: profile.email || '',
      role,
      department: profile.department || 'Science & Technology Laboratories',
      isPrivileged: true,
      isTeacher: false,
      assignedLabIds: LAB_ROOMS.map((l) => l.id),
      assignedClasses: DEFAULT_CLASSES.map((c) => c.name),
      assignedSubjects: DEFAULT_SUBJECTS.map((s) => ({
        code: s.code,
        title: s.title,
        grade: s.grade,
        labId: s.labId,
        labName: s.lab,
        defaultTopic: s.defaultTopic,
      })),
      defaultViewMode: 'institutional',
      teacherProfile: teacherAssign,
    }
  }

  // Regular teacher scope: strictly scoped to assigned curriculum
  // CRITICAL FIX: Unmatched / unassigned teachers receive empty scope, NEVER institutional broad defaults
  const assignedClasses = teacherAssign?.assignedClasses || []
  const assignedSubjects = teacherAssign?.assignedSubjects || []
  const assignedLabIds = teacherAssign?.assignedLabIds || []

  return {
    userId: profile.id,
    fullName: profile.full_name,
    email: profile.email || '',
    role: 'teacher',
    department: profile.department || teacherAssign?.department || 'Science Department',
    isPrivileged: false,
    isTeacher: true,
    assignedLabIds,
    assignedClasses,
    assignedSubjects,
    defaultViewMode: 'my_data',
    teacherProfile: teacherAssign,
  }
}

import type { PracticalGroupedSection, IncidentRecordItem, MaintenanceRecordItem } from '@/types/records'
import { INSTITUTION_CONFIG } from '../institution'
import { formatClassCode, sanitizeSubjectTitle } from '@/lib/report-spec/practical-spec'

function escapeCsvCell(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return '""'
  const str = String(val).replace(/"/g, '""')
  return `"${str}"`
}

interface CsvColDef<T> {
  id: string
  header: string
  getValue: (r: T) => any
}

const ALL_PRACTICAL_CSV_COLS: CsvColDef<any>[] = [
  { id: 'date', header: 'Date', getValue: (r) => r.date },
  { id: 'period', header: 'Period / Slot', getValue: (r) => r.period_label || '—' },
  { id: 'facility', header: 'Laboratory', getValue: (r) => r.labs?.name || r.lab_id || '—' },
  { id: 'class_batch', header: 'Class', getValue: (r) => formatClassCode(r.batch_group) },
  {
    id: 'subject',
    header: 'Subject',
    getValue: (r) => sanitizeSubjectTitle(r.subject_name) + (r.subject_code ? ` (${r.subject_code})` : ''),
  },
  { id: 'experiment', header: 'Practical Title', getValue: (r) => r.practical_title || '—' },
  { id: 'teacher', header: 'Subject Teacher', getValue: (r) => r.profiles?.full_name || 'Assigned Subject Teacher' },
  {
    id: 'attendance',
    header: 'Attendance',
    getValue: (r) => {
      const attPct = r.total_students > 0 ? (r.present_students / r.total_students) * 100 : 0
      return r.status === 'skipped' ? 'N/A' : `${r.present_students}/${r.total_students} (${attPct.toFixed(1)}%)`
    },
  },
  { id: 'status', header: 'Status', getValue: (r) => (r.status === 'skipped' ? 'SKIPPED' : 'CONDUCTED') },
  {
    id: 'remarks',
    header: 'Remarks / Skip Reason',
    getValue: (r) => (r.status === 'skipped' ? r.skip_reason || 'Skipped' : r.remarks || ''),
  },
]

/**
 * Generate RFC 4180 compliant CSV for Practical Records (with optional grouping subtotals and WYSIWYG columns)
 */
export function generatePracticalRecordsCsv(
  sections: PracticalGroupedSection[],
  filtersMeta?: {
    labName?: string
    dateRange?: string
    academicYear?: string
    traceabilityNote?: string
    visibleColumns?: string[]
  }
): string {
  const lines: string[] = []

  // UTF-8 Byte Order Mark for Excel compatibility
  const BOM = '\uFEFF'

  // Institutional Header Banner
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name))
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(INSTITUTION_CONFIG.department))
  lines.push(
    escapeCsvCell(
      `Academic Year: ${filtersMeta?.academicYear || INSTITUTION_CONFIG.academicYear} | Facility: ${
        filtersMeta?.labName || 'All Laboratories'
      } | Date Range: ${filtersMeta?.dateRange || 'All Time'}`
    )
  )

  // Audit Traceability metadata
  if (filtersMeta?.traceabilityNote) {
    lines.push(escapeCsvCell(`Audit Traceability: ${filtersMeta.traceabilityNote}`))
  }
  lines.push('') // Empty row

  // Resolve active columns based on WYSIWYG selection
  const visibleSet =
    filtersMeta?.visibleColumns && filtersMeta.visibleColumns.length > 0
      ? new Set(filtersMeta.visibleColumns)
      : null
  const activeCols = visibleSet
    ? ALL_PRACTICAL_CSV_COLS.filter((c) => visibleSet.has(c.id))
    : ALL_PRACTICAL_CSV_COLS
  const resolvedCols = activeCols.length > 0 ? activeCols : ALL_PRACTICAL_CSV_COLS

  // Table Column Headers
  lines.push(resolvedCols.map((c) => escapeCsvCell(c.header)).join(','))

  for (const sec of sections) {
    if (sec.groupKey !== 'all') {
      // Group Header Row
      const groupRow = [
        escapeCsvCell(`--- GROUP: ${sec.groupTitle} ---`),
        ...Array(Math.max(0, resolvedCols.length - 1)).fill('""'),
      ]
      lines.push(groupRow.join(','))
    }

    for (const row of sec.records) {
      lines.push(resolvedCols.map((c) => escapeCsvCell(c.getValue(row))).join(','))
    }

    if (sec.groupKey !== 'all') {
      // Subtotal Summary Row
      const subtotalLabel = `Subtotal [${sec.groupTitle}]: ${sec.subtotal.totalSessions} Sessions (${sec.subtotal.conductedSessions} Conducted, ${sec.subtotal.skippedSessions} Skipped)`
      const subtotalAttendance = `${sec.subtotal.totalPresent}/${sec.subtotal.totalEnrolled} (${sec.subtotal.averageAttendancePct.toFixed(1)}%)`

      const subRow: string[] = resolvedCols.map((col, idx) => {
        if (idx === 0) return escapeCsvCell(subtotalLabel)
        if (col.id === 'attendance') return escapeCsvCell(subtotalAttendance)
        return '""'
      })
      lines.push(subRow.join(','))
      lines.push('') // Empty row between groups
    }
  }

  // Footer Signature Notice
  lines.push('')
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(`Generated via ${INSTITUTION_CONFIG.systemTitle} on ${new Date().toISOString()}`))
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell('Certified Verification: Lab In-Charge & Head of Department'))

  return BOM + lines.join('\r\n')
}

const ALL_INCIDENT_CSV_COLS: CsvColDef<IncidentRecordItem>[] = [
  { id: 'date_case', header: 'Date & Case #', getValue: (inc) => `${inc.date || inc.created_at?.split('T')[0]} (${inc.id})` },
  { id: 'facility', header: 'Laboratory Facility', getValue: (inc) => inc.labs?.name || inc.lab_id || '—' },
  {
    id: 'equipment',
    header: 'Lab Equipment / Workstation',
    getValue: (inc) => `${inc.equipment_name || '—'}${inc.quantity && inc.quantity > 1 ? ` (${inc.quantity} units)` : ''}`,
  },
  { id: 'title_circ', header: 'Circumstances & Description', getValue: (inc) => inc.title || '—' },
  { id: 'severity', header: 'Severity', getValue: (inc) => (inc.severity || '').toUpperCase() },
  {
    id: 'teacher_batch',
    header: 'Faculty & Class',
    getValue: (inc) => `${inc.subject_teacher_name || inc.reported_by || '—'} [${formatClassCode(inc.batch_name)}]`,
  },
  { id: 'status', header: 'Current Status', getValue: (inc) => (inc.status || '').toUpperCase() },
  {
    id: 'resolution',
    header: 'Action / Resolution Notes',
    getValue: (inc) => inc.resolution_notes || 'Pending Triage / Inspection',
  },
]

/**

export function generateIncidentRecordsCsv(

    labName?: string
    dateRange?: string
    traceabilityNote?: string
    visibleColumns?: string[]
  }
): string {

  // Header Banner
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name))
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell('OFFICIAL LABORATORY INCIDENT & EQUIPMENT DAMAGE REGISTER'))
  lines.push(
    escapeCsvCell(
      `Facility: ${filtersMeta?.labName || 'All Facilities'} | Period: ${
        filtersMeta?.dateRange || 'All Time'
      } | Generated: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`
    )
  )

  // Audit Traceability metadata
  if (filtersMeta?.traceabilityNote) {
    lines.push(escapeCsvCell(`Audit Traceability: ${filtersMeta.traceabilityNote}`))
  }
  lines.push('')

  // Resolve active columns based on WYSIWYG selection
  const visibleSet =
    filtersMeta?.visibleColumns && filtersMeta.visibleColumns.length > 0
      ? new Set(filtersMeta.visibleColumns)
      : null
  const activeCols = visibleSet
    ? ALL_INCIDENT_CSV_COLS.filter((c) => visibleSet.has(c.id))
    : ALL_INCIDENT_CSV_COLS
  const resolvedCols = activeCols.length > 0 ? activeCols : ALL_INCIDENT_CSV_COLS

  lines.push(resolvedCols.map((c) => escapeCsvCell(c.header)).join(','))

    lines.push(resolvedCols.map((c) => escapeCsvCell(c.getValue(inc))).join(','))

  lines.push(escapeCsvCell('Certified Verification: Lab In-Charge & Head of Department'))

  return BOM + lines.join('\r\n')

/**
 * Generate RFC 4180 compliant CSV for Lab Maintenance & Servicing Records
 */
export function generateMaintenanceRecordsCsv(
  records: MaintenanceRecordItem[],
  filtersMeta?: { labName?: string; dateRange?: string }
): string {
  const lines: string[] = []
  const BOM = '\uFEFF'

  // Header Banner
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name))
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell('OFFICIAL LABORATORY PREVENTIVE MAINTENANCE & SERVICING AUDIT REGISTER'))
  lines.push(
    escapeCsvCell(
      `Facility: ${filtersMeta?.labName || 'All Facilities'} | Period: ${
        filtersMeta?.dateRange || 'All Time'
      } | Generated: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`
    )
  )
  lines.push('')

  // Two-phase Flattened Headers matching XLSX
  const headers = [
    'Target Asset / Hardware',
    'Category',
    'Routine Plan Title',
    'Cadence',
    'Target Role',
    'Scheduled Due Date',
    'SOP Checklist Items',
    'Work Performed / Action Taken',
    'Checklist Completed %',
    'Parts Replaced',
    'Service Date',
    'Certified Technician',
    'Next Recommended Due',
    'Remarks / Observations',
  ]
  lines.push(headers.map(escapeCsvCell).join(','))

  for (const rec of records) {
    const planChecklist = Array.isArray(rec.plan?.checklist) ? rec.plan.checklist : []
    const completedChecklist = Array.isArray(rec.checklist_completed) ? (rec.checklist_completed as string[]) : []
    const totalSteps = planChecklist.length > 0 ? planChecklist.length : completedChecklist.length
    const checklistSummary = totalSteps > 0
      ? `${completedChecklist.length}/${totalSteps} (${rec.checklist_completed_pct}%)`
      : '—'

    const intervalLabel = rec.plan?.interval_days
      ? `Every ${rec.plan.interval_days} Days`
      : 'As Needed'

    const checklistItemsStr = planChecklist.length > 0
      ? planChecklist.map((step, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${step}`).join('; ')
      : (completedChecklist.length > 0 ? completedChecklist.join('; ') : 'Routine Technical Checklist')

    lines.push(
      [
        escapeCsvCell(rec.asset_identifier),
        escapeCsvCell((rec.plan?.category || 'general').toUpperCase()),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.plan_title || rec.plan?.title || 'Preventive Maintenance'),
        escapeCsvCell(intervalLabel),
        escapeCsvCell((rec.plan?.target_role || 'lab_incharge').replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase()),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.service_date),
        escapeCsvCell(checklistItemsStr),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.work_performed),
        escapeCsvCell(checklistSummary),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.parts_replaced || 'None (Serviced / Cleaned)'),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.service_date),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.performed_by_name || 'Laboratory Staff'),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.next_service_due || '—'),
        escapeCsvCell(rec.remarks || 'Routine maintenance verified.'),
      ].join(',')
    )
  }

  // Footer Certification
  lines.push('')
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell(`Generated via ${INSTITUTION_CONFIG.systemTitle} on ${new Date().toISOString()}`))
  lines.push(escapeCsvCell('Certified Verification: Laboratory In-Charge, Academic Coordinator & Campus Chief'))

  return BOM + lines.join('\r\n')
}

import ExcelJS from 'exceljs'
import type {

  labName?: string

function getColumnLetter(colIndex: number): string {
  let letter = ''
  let temp = colIndex
  while (temp > 0) {
    const rem = (temp - 1) % 26
    letter = String.fromCharCode(65 + rem) + letter
    temp = Math.floor((temp - 1) / 26)
  }
  return letter || 'A'
}

interface PracticalXlsxCol {
  id: string
  header: string
  width: number
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
  numFmt?: string
  isStatus?: boolean
  isAttendance?: boolean
  getValue: (r: any) => any
}

const ALL_PRACTICAL_XLSX_COLS: PracticalXlsxCol[] = [

  { id: 'facility', header: 'Laboratory Facility', width: 22, align: 'left', getValue: (r) => r.labs?.name || r.lab_id || '—' },
  { id: 'class_batch', header: 'Class', width: 14, align: 'left', getValue: (r) => formatClassCode(r.batch_group) },
  {
    id: 'subject',

  { id: 'experiment', header: 'Practical Experiment Title', width: 36, align: 'left', getValue: (r) => r.practical_title || '—' },
  { id: 'teacher', header: 'Subject Teacher', width: 24, align: 'left', getValue: (r) => r.profiles?.full_name || 'Assigned Subject Teacher' },
  {
    id: 'attendance',

    },

/**
 * Generate high-fidelity Excel (.xlsx) workbook for Practical Records with WYSIWYG visible columns
 */
export async function generatePracticalRecordsXlsxBuffer(
  sections: PracticalGroupedSection[],
  meta?: PracticalMeta
): Promise<Buffer> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  workbook.creator = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  workbook.lastModifiedBy = 'LabSync LIMS'
  workbook.created = new Date()
  workbook.modified = new Date()

  const sheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Practical Records', {
    pageSetup: { orientation: 'landscape', fitToPage: true, fitToWidth: 1, fitToHeight: 0 },
    views: [{ state: 'frozen', ySplit: meta?.traceabilityNote ? 7 : 6 }],
  })

  // Resolve active visible columns
  const visibleSet = meta?.visibleColumns && meta.visibleColumns.length > 0 ? new Set(meta.visibleColumns) : null
  const activeCols = visibleSet ? ALL_PRACTICAL_XLSX_COLS.filter((c) => visibleSet.has(c.id)) : ALL_PRACTICAL_XLSX_COLS
  const resolvedCols = activeCols.length > 0 ? activeCols : ALL_PRACTICAL_XLSX_COLS
  const lastCol = getColumnLetter(resolvedCols.length)

  // 1. INSTITUTIONAL TITLE BANNER (Rows 1 - 5)
  // Row 1: School Title
  sheet.mergeCells(`A1:${lastCol}1`)
  const r1 = sheet.getCell('A1')
  r1.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  r1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 14, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  r1.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } // Slate-900
  r1.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(1).height = 28

  // Row 2: Department & Academic Year
  sheet.mergeCells(`A2:${lastCol}2`)
  const r2 = sheet.getCell('A2')
  r2.value = `${INSTITUTION_CONFIG.department} • Academic Year: ${meta?.academicYear || INSTITUTION_CONFIG.academicYear}`
  r2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } }
  r2.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } } // Slate-800
  r2.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(2).height = 20

  // Row 3: Document Title & Metadata
  sheet.mergeCells(`A3:${lastCol}3`)
  const r3 = sheet.getCell('A3')
  r3.value = `OFFICIAL PRACTICAL EXPERIMENT RECORD & ATTENDANCE AUDIT SHEET`
  r3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  r3.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF1F5F9' } } // Slate-100
  r3.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(3).height = 20

  // Row 4: Filter Metadata
  sheet.mergeCells(`A4:${lastCol}4`)
  const r4 = sheet.getCell('A4')
  r4.value = `Facility: ${meta?.labName || 'All Laboratories'}  |  Date Range: ${meta?.dateRange || 'All Time'}  |  Grouping: ${meta?.groupByLabel || 'None'}  |  Exported: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`
  r4.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF475569' } }
  r4.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF8FAFC' } }
  r4.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(4).height = 18

  let currentRowIndex = 5

  // Row 5: Audit Traceability metadata (if available)
  if (meta?.traceabilityNote) {
    sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRowIndex}:${lastCol}${currentRowIndex}`)
    const rTrace = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRowIndex}`)
    rTrace.value = `Audit Traceability: ${meta.traceabilityNote}`
    rTrace.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8.5, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF4338CA' } } // Indigo-700
    rTrace.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFEEF2FF' } } // Indigo-50
    rTrace.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
    sheet.getRow(currentRowIndex).height = 18
    currentRowIndex++
  }

  // 2. TABLE HEADERS
  const headerRow = sheet.getRow(currentRowIndex)
  headerRow.values = resolvedCols.map((c) => c.header)
  headerRow.height = 26
  headerRow.eachCell((cell) => {
    cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
    cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E3A8A' } } // Blue-900 Navy
    cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center', wrapText: true }
    cell.border = {
      top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      bottom: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF334155' } },
      right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF334155' } },
    }
  })
  currentRowIndex++

  // Set column widths
  resolvedCols.forEach((col, idx) => {
    sheet.getColumn(idx + 1).width = col.width
  })

  // 3. DATA ROWS & GROUPING SECTIONS
  for (const section of sections) {
    if (section.groupKey !== 'all') {
      sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRowIndex}:${lastCol}${currentRowIndex}`)
      const groupCell = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRowIndex}`)
      groupCell.value = `📂 SECTION: ${section.groupTitle.toUpperCase()}`
      groupCell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } }
      groupCell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } }
      groupCell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', indent: 1 }
      sheet.getRow(currentRowIndex).height = 22
      currentRowIndex++
    }

    for (const record of section.records) {
      const row = sheet.getRow(currentRowIndex)
      const isSkipped = record.status === 'skipped'
      row.values = resolvedCols.map((col) => col.getValue(record))
      row.height = 20

      row.eachCell((cell, colNumber) => {
        const colDef = resolvedCols[colNumber - 1]
        cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
        cell.border = {
          top: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
          bottom: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
          left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
          right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        }

        cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: colDef.align }
        if (colDef.isStatus) {
          if (isSkipped) {
            cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFB45309' } } // Amber-700
            cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEF3C7' } }
          } else {
            cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF047857' } } // Emerald-700
            cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFD1FAE5' } }
          }
        }
      })
      currentRowIndex++
    }

    if (section.groupKey !== 'all') {
      const subtotalRow = sheet.getRow(currentRowIndex)
      sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRowIndex}:${lastCol}${currentRowIndex}`)
      const subLabel = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRowIndex}`)
      subLabel.value = `SUBTOTAL [${section.groupTitle}]: ${section.subtotal.totalSessions} Sessions (${section.subtotal.conductedSessions} Conducted, ${section.subtotal.skippedSessions} Skipped) | Attendance: ${section.subtotal.totalPresent}/${section.subtotal.totalEnrolled} (${section.subtotal.averageAttendancePct.toFixed(1)}%)`
      subLabel.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } }
      subLabel.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'left', indent: 1 }
      subtotalRow.height = 22
      currentRowIndex++
    }
  }

  // 4. SIGNATURE NOTICE
  currentRowIndex += 2
  const sigColLeft = 'A'
  const sigColRight = lastCol
  const sigLeft = sheet.getCell(`${sigColLeft}${currentRowIndex}`)
  sigLeft.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.signatures.left.title.toUpperCase()
  sigLeft.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sigLeft.alignment = { horizontal: 'left' }
  sigLeft.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sigRight = sheet.getCell(`${sigColRight}${currentRowIndex}`)
  sigRight.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.signatures.right.title.toUpperCase()
  sigRight.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sigRight.alignment = { horizontal: 'right' }
  sigRight.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  return (await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer()) as unknown as Buffer
}

interface IncidentXlsxCol {
  id: string
  header: string
  width: number
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
  isSeverity?: boolean
  isStatus?: boolean
  getValue: (r: IncidentRecordItem) => any
}

const ALL_INCIDENT_XLSX_COLS: IncidentXlsxCol[] = [
  { id: 'date_case', header: 'Date & Case #', width: 18, align: 'center', getValue: (inc) => `${inc.date || inc.created_at?.split('T')[0]} (${inc.id})` },
  { id: 'facility', header: 'Facility Room', width: 22, align: 'left', getValue: (inc) => inc.labs?.name || inc.lab_id || '—' },
  {
    id: 'equipment',
    header: 'Lab Equipment / Workstation',
    width: 28,
    align: 'left',
    getValue: (inc) => `${inc.equipment_name || '—'}${inc.quantity && inc.quantity > 1 ? ` (${inc.quantity} units)` : ''}`,
  },
  { id: 'title_circ', header: 'Circumstances & Description', width: 36, align: 'left', getValue: (inc) => inc.title || '—' },
  { id: 'severity', header: 'Severity', width: 14, align: 'center', isSeverity: true, getValue: (inc) => (inc.severity || '').toUpperCase() },
  {
    id: 'teacher_batch',
    header: 'Faculty & Class',
    width: 24,
    align: 'left',
    getValue: (inc) => `${inc.subject_teacher_name || inc.reported_by || '—'} [${formatClassCode(inc.batch_name)}]`,
  },
  { id: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 16, align: 'center', isStatus: true, getValue: (inc) => (inc.status || '').toUpperCase() },
  {
    id: 'resolution',
    header: 'Action / Resolution Notes',
    width: 38,
    align: 'left',
    getValue: (inc) => inc.resolution_notes || 'Pending Triage / Inspection',
  },
]

/**
 * Generate high-fidelity Excel (.xlsx) workbook for Laboratory Incidents & Damage Register with WYSIWYG visible columns
 */
export async function generateIncidentRecordsXlsxBuffer(
  incidents: IncidentRecordItem[],
  metrics?: IncidentSummaryMetrics,
  filtersMeta?: {
    labName?: string
    dateRange?: string
    traceabilityNote?: string
    visibleColumns?: string[]
  }
): Promise<Buffer> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  workbook.creator = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  workbook.lastModifiedBy = 'LabSync LIMS'

  // Resolve active visible columns
  const visibleSet = filtersMeta?.visibleColumns && filtersMeta.visibleColumns.length > 0 ? new Set(filtersMeta.visibleColumns) : null
  const activeCols = visibleSet ? ALL_INCIDENT_XLSX_COLS.filter((c) => visibleSet.has(c.id)) : ALL_INCIDENT_XLSX_COLS
  const resolvedCols = activeCols.length > 0 ? activeCols : ALL_INCIDENT_XLSX_COLS
  const lastCol = getColumnLetter(resolvedCols.length)

  const sheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Incident Register', {
    pageSetup: { orientation: 'landscape', fitToPage: true, fitToWidth: 1, fitToHeight: 0 },
    views: [{ state: 'frozen', ySplit: filtersMeta?.traceabilityNote ? 6 : 5 }],
  })

  // 1. Institutional Banner (Rows 1 - 4)
  sheet.mergeCells(`A1:${lastCol}1`)
  const r1 = sheet.getCell('A1')
  r1.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  r1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 14, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  r1.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF991B1B' } } // Red-800
  r1.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(1).height = 28

  sheet.mergeCells(`A2:${lastCol}2`)
  const r2 = sheet.getCell('A2')
  r2.value = `OFFICIAL LABORATORY EQUIPMENT DAMAGE & SAFETY INCIDENT REGISTER`
  r2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 11, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  r2.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF7F1D1D' } } // Red-900
  r2.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(2).height = 22

  sheet.mergeCells(`A3:${lastCol}3`)
  const r3 = sheet.getCell('A3')
  r3.value = `Facility: ${filtersMeta?.labName || 'All Facilities'}  |  Date Range: ${filtersMeta?.dateRange || 'All Time'}  |  Exported: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`
  r3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF475569' } }
  r3.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF8FAFC' } }
  r3.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(3).height = 18

  let currentRow = 4

  // Traceability metadata row (if available)
  if (filtersMeta?.traceabilityNote) {
    sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRow}:${lastCol}${currentRow}`)
    const rTrace = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRow}`)
    rTrace.value = `Audit Traceability: ${filtersMeta.traceabilityNote}`
    rTrace.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8.5, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF991B1B' } }
    rTrace.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEE2E2' } }
    rTrace.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
    sheet.getRow(currentRow).height = 18
    currentRow++
  }

  // Summary metrics row (if available)
  if (metrics) {
    sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRow}:${lastCol}${currentRow}`)
    const rMetrics = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRow}`)
    rMetrics.value = `Total: ${metrics.totalIncidents} | Open: ${metrics.openCount} | Resolved: ${metrics.resolvedCount} | Critical: ${metrics.criticalCount} | Moderate: ${metrics.moderateCount} | Minor: ${metrics.minorCount} | Avg Resolution: ${metrics.averageTimeToResolveLabel}`
    rMetrics.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } }
    rMetrics.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF1F5F9' } }
    rMetrics.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
    sheet.getRow(currentRow).height = 20
    currentRow++
  }

  // TABLE HEADERS
  const headerRow = sheet.getRow(currentRow)
  headerRow.values = resolvedCols.map((c) => c.header)
  headerRow.height = 26
  headerRow.eachCell((cell) => {
    cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
    cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF7F1D1D' } } // Red-900
    cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center', wrapText: true }
    cell.border = {
      top: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      bottom: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF475569' } },
      right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF475569' } },
    }
  })
  currentRow++

  // Set column widths
  resolvedCols.forEach((col, idx) => {
    sheet.getColumn(idx + 1).width = col.width
  })

  // DATA ROWS
  for (const inc of incidents) {
    const row = sheet.getRow(currentRow)
    row.values = resolvedCols.map((col) => col.getValue(inc))
    row.height = 20

    row.eachCell((cell, colNumber) => {
      const colDef = resolvedCols[colNumber - 1]
      cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
      cell.border = {
        top: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        bottom: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
      }

      cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: colDef.align }

      // Severity highlight
      if (colDef.isSeverity) {
        if (inc.severity === 'major_critical') {
          cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF991B1B' } }
          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEE2E2' } }
        } else if (inc.severity === 'moderate') {
          cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFB45309' } }
          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEF3C7' } }
        } else {
          cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0369A1' } }
          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFE0F2FE' } }
        }
      } else if (colDef.isStatus) {
        if (inc.status === 'resolved') {
          cell.font = { bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF047857' } }
          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFD1FAE5' } }
        } else if (inc.status === 'escalated_to_hod') {
          cell.font = { bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF991B1B' } }
          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEE2E2' } }
        }
      }
    })
    currentRow++
  }

  // SIGNATURE NOTICE
  currentRow += 2
  const sigColLeft = 'A'
  const sigColRight = lastCol
  const sigLeft = sheet.getCell(`${sigColLeft}${currentRow}`)
  sigLeft.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.signatures.left.title.toUpperCase()
  sigLeft.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sigLeft.alignment = { horizontal: 'left' }
  sigLeft.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sigRight = sheet.getCell(`${sigColRight}${currentRow}`)
  sigRight.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.signatures.right.title.toUpperCase()
  sigRight.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sigRight.alignment = { horizontal: 'right' }
  sigRight.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  return (await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer()) as unknown as Buffer
}

/**
 * Generate high-fidelity Excel (.xlsx) workbook for Lab Maintenance & Servicing Records
 */
export async function generateMaintenanceRecordsXlsxBuffer(
  records: MaintenanceRecordItem[],
  metrics?: MaintenanceSummaryMetrics,
  filtersMeta?: { labName?: string; dateRange?: string }
): Promise<Buffer> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  workbook.creator = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  workbook.lastModifiedBy = 'LabSync LIMS'

  const sheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Maintenance Records', {
    pageSetup: { orientation: 'landscape', fitToPage: true, fitToWidth: 1, fitToHeight: 0 },
    views: [{ state: 'frozen', ySplit: 8 }],
  })

  // 1. Institutional Banner (Rows 1 - 3)
  sheet.mergeCells('A1:N1')
  const r1 = sheet.getCell('A1')
  r1.value = INSTITUTION_CONFIG.name
  r1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 14, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  r1.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } } // Slate-800
  r1.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(1).height = 28

  sheet.mergeCells('A2:N2')
  const r2 = sheet.getCell('A2')
  r2.value = `OFFICIAL LABORATORY PREVENTIVE MAINTENANCE & SERVICING AUDIT REGISTER`
  r2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 11, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  r2.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF312E81' } } // Indigo-900
  r2.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(2).height = 22

  sheet.mergeCells('A3:N3')
  const r3 = sheet.getCell('A3')
  r3.value = `Facility: ${filtersMeta?.labName || 'All Facilities'}  |  Scope: ${filtersMeta?.dateRange || 'All Time'}  |  Exported: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`
  r3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF475569' } }
  r3.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF8FAFC' } }
  r3.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(3).height = 18

  // 2. SUMMARY METRICS BLOCK (Row 4)
  if (metrics) {
    sheet.mergeCells('A4:C4')
    sheet.getCell('A4').value = `Total Servicing Logs: ${metrics.totalLogs}`
    sheet.getCell('A4').font = { bold: true, size: 9 }

    sheet.mergeCells('D4:F4')
    sheet.getCell('D4').value = `Overdue Reminders: ${metrics.overdueCount}`
    sheet.getCell('D4').font = { bold: true, size: 9, color: { argb: 'FFB45309' } }

    sheet.mergeCells('G4:J4')
    sheet.getCell('G4').value = `Servicing Cycles Completed: ${metrics.completedThisPeriodCount}`
    sheet.getCell('G4').font = { bold: true, size: 9, color: { argb: 'FF047857' } }

    sheet.mergeCells('K4:N4')
    sheet.getCell('K4').value = `Assets Maintained: ${metrics.uniqueAssetsCount ?? records.length}`
    sheet.getCell('K4').font = { bold: true, size: 9, color: { argb: 'FF4338CA' } }

    sheet.getRow(4).height = 20
    sheet.getRow(4).eachCell((c) => {
      c.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFF1F5F9' } }
      c.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
    })
  }

  // Row 5: Empty spacer
  sheet.getRow(5).height = 6

  // 3. PHASE HEADERS (Row 6: Section 1 vs Section 2)
  sheet.mergeCells('A6:G6')
  const rPhase1 = sheet.getCell('A6')
  rPhase1.value = 'SECTION 1: PLAN & PREVENTIVE SCHEDULE SPECIFICATION'
  rPhase1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  rPhase1.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } } // Slate-800
  rPhase1.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }

  sheet.mergeCells('H6:N6')
  const rPhase2 = sheet.getCell('H6')
  rPhase2.value = 'SECTION 2: SERVICE EXECUTION & TECHNICAL CERTIFICATION'
  rPhase2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 10, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
  rPhase2.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF065F46' } } // Emerald-800
  rPhase2.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
  sheet.getRow(6).height = 22

  // Row 7: Empty spacer
  sheet.getRow(7).height = 4

  // 4. COLUMN HEADERS (Row 8)
  const headers = [
    'Target Asset / Hardware',
    'Category',
    'Routine Plan Title',
    'Cadence',
    'Target Role',
    'Scheduled Due Date',
    'SOP Checklist Items',
    'Work Performed / Action Taken',
    'Checklist Completed %',
    'Parts Replaced',
    'Service Date',
    'Certified Technician',
    'Next Recommended Due',
    'Remarks / Observations',
  ]
  const headerRow = sheet.getRow(8)
  headerRow.values = headers
  headerRow.height = 26
  headerRow.eachCell((cell, colNumber) => {
    cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9.5, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }
    cell.fill = {
      type: 'pattern',
      pattern: 'solid',
      fgColor: { argb: colNumber <= 7 ? 'FF0F172A' : 'FF064E3B' },
    }
    cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center', wrapText: true }
    cell.border = {
      top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      bottom: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } },
      left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF334155' } },
      right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FF334155' } },
    }
  })

  // 5. DATA ROWS
  let currentRow = 9
  for (const record of records) {
    const planChecklist = Array.isArray(record.plan?.checklist) ? record.plan.checklist : []
    const completedChecklist = Array.isArray(record.checklist_completed) ? (record.checklist_completed as string[]) : []
    const totalSteps = planChecklist.length > 0 ? planChecklist.length : completedChecklist.length
    const checklistSummary = totalSteps > 0
      ? `${completedChecklist.length}/${totalSteps} (${record.checklist_completed_pct}%)`
      : '—'

    const intervalLabel = record.plan?.interval_days
      ? `Every ${record.plan.interval_days} Days`
      : 'As Needed'

    const checklistItemsStr = planChecklist.length > 0
      ? planChecklist.map((step, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${step}`).join('; ')
      : (completedChecklist.length > 0 ? completedChecklist.join('; ') : 'Routine Technical Checklist')

    const row = sheet.getRow(currentRow)
    row.values = [
      record.asset_identifier,
      (record.plan?.category || 'general').toUpperCase(),
      record.plan_title || record.plan?.title || 'Preventive Maintenance',
      intervalLabel,
      (record.plan?.target_role || 'lab_incharge').replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase(),
      record.service_date,
      checklistItemsStr,
      record.work_performed,
      checklistSummary,
      record.parts_replaced || 'None (Serviced / Cleaned)',
      record.service_date,
      record.performed_by_name || 'Laboratory Staff',
      record.next_service_due || '—',
      record.remarks || 'Routine maintenance verified.',
    ]

    row.height = 22
    row.eachCell((cell, colNumber) => {
      cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9 }
      cell.border = {
        top: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        bottom: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        left: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
        right: { style: 'thin', color: { argb: 'FFE2E8F0' } },
      }

      if (colNumber === 9) {
        cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
        cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF047857' } }
      } else if (colNumber === 2 || colNumber === 4 || colNumber === 5 || colNumber === 6 || colNumber === 11 || colNumber === 13) {
        cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' }
      } else {
        cell.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'left' }
      }
    })

    currentRow++
  }

  // 6. 3-TIER SIGNATURE BLOCKS AT BOTTOM
  currentRow += 2
  sheet.mergeCells(`B${currentRow}:D${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`F${currentRow}:H${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`K${currentRow}:M${currentRow}`)

  const sig1 = sheet.getCell(`B${currentRow}`)
  sig1.value = 'LABORATORY IN-CHARGE'
  sig1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sig1.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig1.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sig2 = sheet.getCell(`F${currentRow}`)
  sig2.value = 'ACADEMIC COORDINATOR'
  sig2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sig2.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig2.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sig3 = sheet.getCell(`K${currentRow}`)
  sig3.value = 'CAMPUS CHIEF / PRINCIPAL'
  sig3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sig3.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig3.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  currentRow++
  sheet.mergeCells(`B${currentRow}:D${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`F${currentRow}:H${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`K${currentRow}:M${currentRow}`)

  const sub1 = sheet.getCell(`B${currentRow}`)
  sub1.value = 'Technical Verification & Servicing Audit'
  sub1.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF64748B' } }
  sub1.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }

  const sub2 = sheet.getCell(`F${currentRow}`)
  sub2.value = 'Curricular Oversight & Schedule Verification'
  sub2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF64748B' } }
  sub2.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }

  const sub3 = sheet.getCell(`K${currentRow}`)
  sub3.value = 'Institutional Endorsement & Certification'
  sub3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF64748B' } }
  sub3.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }

  // 7. Column Auto-Widths (14 columns)
  const colWidths = [18, 14, 28, 16, 18, 16, 32, 34, 18, 22, 14, 22, 18, 30]
  sheet.columns.forEach((col, idx) => {
    col.width = colWidths[idx] || 18
  })

  return (await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer()) as unknown as Buffer
}

/**

    const cell = sheet.getCell(`${colLetter}7`)
    cell.value = h.label.toUpperCase()
    cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } }

          cell.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FFFEF2F2' } }
        }
      }

      if (colIdx === 7 && !rec.is_eligible) {
        cell.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFB91C1C' } }
      }
    })

    currentRow++
  })

  // 5. SIGNATURE NOTICE AT BOTTOM
  currentRow += 2
  sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRow}:C${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`D${currentRow}:F${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`G${currentRow}:I${currentRow}`)

  const sig1 = sheet.getCell(`A${currentRow}`)

  sig1.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig1.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sig2 = sheet.getCell(`D${currentRow}`)
  sig2.value = 'LABORATORY IN-CHARGE'
  sig2.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sig2.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig2.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  const sig3 = sheet.getCell(`G${currentRow}`)
  sig3.value = 'CAMPUS CHIEF / PRINCIPAL'
  sig3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 9, bold: true, color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } }
  sig3.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }
  sig3.border = { top: { style: 'medium', color: { argb: 'FF0F172A' } } }

  sheet.mergeCells(`A${currentRow}:C${currentRow}`)
  sheet.mergeCells(`D${currentRow}:F${currentRow}`)
