  sub3.font = { name: 'Arial', size: 8, italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF64748B' } }
  sub3.alignment = { horizontal: 'center' }

  // Set column widths
  headers.forEach((h, idx) => {
    sheet.getColumn(idx + 1).width = h.width
  })

  return (await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer()) as unknown as Buffer
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\imports\string-normalization.ts ===
/**
 * String normalization and fuzzy comparison utilities for student names,
 * abbreviations, and Nepali naming conventions.
 */

/**
 * Normalizes a student name for deduplication and matching:
 * - Strips all non-alphanumeric characters (periods, commas, dashes, quotes)
 * - Collapses repeated whitespace
 * - Standardizes common abbreviations (e.g., 'k.c.' -> 'kc', 'b.k.' -> 'bk')
 * - Converts to lowercase trimmed string
 *
 * Example:
 * normalizeStudentName("  Ram  K.C.  ") === "ram kc"
 * normalizeStudentName("Sita B.K.") === "sita bk"
 * normalizeStudentName("Bikash Shrestha-Magar") === "bikash shresthamagar"
 */
export function normalizeStudentName(name: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!name) return ''

  return name
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim()
    // Remove periods inside abbreviations: k.c. -> kc, b.k. -> bk
    .replace(/([a-z])\./gi, '$1')
    // Remove non-alphanumeric characters except single space
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/gi, '')
    // Collapse multiple spaces into a single space
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

/**
 * Capitalizes a student name nicely into Title Case for presentation.
 * Example: "RAM BAHADUR K.C." -> "Ram Bahadur K.C."
 */
export function formatStudentDisplayName(name: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!name) return ''

  return name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .map((word) => {
      if (!word) return ''
      // If it looks like an acronym/abbreviation with dots (e.g., K.C.), uppercase it
      if (word.includes('.')) {
        return word.toUpperCase()
      }
      return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1).toLowerCase()
    })
    .join(' ')
}

/**
 * Calculates Levenshtein edit distance between two strings
 */
export function levenshteinDistance(a: string, b: string): number {
  const an = a.length
  const bn = b.length
  if (an === 0) return bn
  if (bn === 0) return an

  const matrix = Array.from({ length: bn + 1 }, () => new Array(an + 1).fill(0))

  for (let i = 0; i <= an; i++) matrix[0][i] = i
  for (let j = 0; j <= bn; j++) matrix[j][0] = j

  for (let j = 1; j <= bn; j++) {
    for (let i = 1; i <= an; i++) {
      const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1
      matrix[j][i] = Math.min(
        matrix[j][i - 1] + 1,       // insertion
        matrix[j - 1][i] + 1,       // deletion
        matrix[j - 1][i - 1] + cost // substitution
      )
    }
  }

  return matrix[bn][an]
}

/**
 * Returns string similarity score from 0.0 (completely different) to 1.0 (exact match)
 */
export function calculateNameSimilarity(nameA: string, nameB: string): number {
  const normA = normalizeStudentName(nameA)
  const normB = normalizeStudentName(nameB)

  if (normA === normB) return 1.0
  if (!normA || !normB) return 0.0

  const maxLen = Math.max(normA.length, normB.length)
  if (maxLen === 0) return 1.0

  const distance = levenshteinDistance(normA, normB)
  return Math.max(0, 1.0 - distance / maxLen)
}

/**
 * Checks if two student names can be safely considered the same person (>= 85% match or exact token match)
 */
export function isNameMatch(nameA: string, nameB: string, threshold = 0.85): boolean {
  const normA = normalizeStudentName(nameA)
  const normB = normalizeStudentName(nameB)

  if (normA === normB) return true

  // Check token permutation (e.g., "Sharma Ram" vs "Ram Sharma")
  const tokensA = normA.split(' ').sort().join(' ')
  const tokensB = normB.split(' ').sort().join(' ')
  if (tokensA === tokensB) return true

  return calculateNameSimilarity(normA, normB) >= threshold
}

import type { UnifiedReportFilters, PrintDensityMode } from '../../types/report-spec'

/**
 * Sanitizes input before interpolating into PostgREST .or() filter strings.
 * Strips special PostgREST syntax characters (commas, parentheses, %, *, ")
 * and normalizes whitespace so parenthetical and comma-containing queries do not break syntax.
 */
export function sanitizePostgrestSearch(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!raw) return ''
  return raw.replace(/[,()%"*]/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
}

/**
 * Encodes UnifiedReportFilters into standard URLSearchParams.
 * Omits empty, undefined, or 'all' values for clean URLs.
 */
export function filtersToSearchParams(filters: UnifiedReportFilters): URLSearchParams {
  const params = new URLSearchParams()

  if (filters.academic_year_id && filters.academic_year_id !== 'all') {
    params.set('academic_year_id', filters.academic_year_id)
  }
  if (filters.lab_id && filters.lab_id !== 'all') {
    params.set('lab_id', filters.lab_id)
  }
  if (filters.date_from) {
    params.set('date_from', filters.date_from)
  }
  if (filters.date_to) {
    params.set('date_to', filters.date_to)
  }
  if (filters.date) {
    params.set('date', filters.date)
  }
  if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') {
    params.set('status', filters.status)
  }
  if (filters.search && filters.search.trim()) {
    params.set('search', filters.search.trim())
  }

  // Pagination
  if (filters.page && filters.page > 1) {
    params.set('page', String(filters.page))
  }
  if (filters.pageSize && filters.pageSize !== 10) {
    params.set('pageSize', String(filters.pageSize))
  }

  // Sorting
  if (filters.sortKey) {
    params.set('sortKey', filters.sortKey)
  }
  if (filters.sortDirection) {
    params.set('sortDirection', filters.sortDirection)
  }

  // Practical-specific
  if (filters.batch_group && filters.batch_group !== 'all') {
    params.set('batch_group', filters.batch_group)
  }
  if (filters.subject_name && filters.subject_name !== 'all') {
    params.set('subject_name', filters.subject_name)
  }
  if (filters.teacher_id && filters.teacher_id !== 'all') {
    params.set('teacher_id', filters.teacher_id)
  }
  if (filters.teacher_name && filters.teacher_name.trim()) {
    params.set('teacher_name', filters.teacher_name.trim())
  }
  if (filters.period_label && filters.period_label !== 'all') {
    params.set('period_label', filters.period_label)
  }
  if (filters.remarks && filters.remarks.trim()) {
    params.set('remarks', filters.remarks.trim())
  }
  if (filters.groupBy && filters.groupBy !== 'none') {
    params.set('groupBy', filters.groupBy)
  }

  // Incident-specific
  if (filters.severity && filters.severity !== 'all') {
    params.set('severity', filters.severity)
  }
  if (filters.incident_type && filters.incident_type !== 'all') {
    params.set('incident_type', filters.incident_type)
  }
  if (filters.equipment_name && filters.equipment_name.trim()) {
    params.set('equipment_name', filters.equipment_name.trim())
  }
  if (filters.title && filters.title.trim()) {
    params.set('title', filters.title.trim())
  }

  // Maintenance-specific
  if (filters.asset_identifier && filters.asset_identifier !== 'all') {
    params.set('asset_identifier', filters.asset_identifier)
  }
  if (filters.plan_category && filters.plan_category !== 'all') {
    params.set('plan_category', filters.plan_category)
  }
  if (filters.technician_id && filters.technician_id !== 'all') {
    params.set('technician_id', filters.technician_id)
  }
  if (filters.maintenance_status && filters.maintenance_status !== 'all') {
    params.set('maintenance_status', filters.maintenance_status)
  }

  // Print density preference
  if (filters.density) {
    params.set('density', filters.density)
  }

  // WYSIWYG selection and column projection
  if (filters.cols && filters.cols.trim()) {
    params.set('cols', filters.cols.trim())
  }
  if (filters.selected && filters.selected.trim()) {
    params.set('selected', filters.selected.trim())
  }
  if (filters.selKey && filters.selKey.trim()) {
    params.set('selKey', filters.selKey.trim())
  }
  if (filters.selectedAll) {
    params.set('selectedAll', 'true')
  }

  return params
}

/**
 * Deserializes search parameters into a typed UnifiedReportFilters object.
 * Supports URLSearchParams, plain objects, and Next.js Page searchParams.
 */
export function searchParamsToFilters(
  input?: URLSearchParams | Record<string, string | string[] | undefined> | null
): UnifiedReportFilters {
  const filters: UnifiedReportFilters = {}
  if (!input) return filters

  const getParam = (key: string): string | undefined => {
    if (input instanceof URLSearchParams) {
      const val = input.get(key)
      return val !== null && val !== '' ? val : undefined
    }
    const raw = input[key]
    if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw[0]
    return raw !== '' ? raw : undefined
  }

  const academic_year_id = getParam('academic_year_id') || getParam('year')
  if (academic_year_id) filters.academic_year_id = academic_year_id

  const lab_id = getParam('lab_id')
  if (lab_id) filters.lab_id = lab_id

  const date_from = getParam('date_from')
  if (date_from) filters.date_from = date_from

  const date_to = getParam('date_to')
  if (date_to) filters.date_to = date_to

  const date = getParam('date')
  if (date) filters.date = date

  const status = getParam('status')
  if (status) filters.status = status

  const search = getParam('search')
  if (search) filters.search = search

  // Pagination
  const pageStr = getParam('page')
  if (pageStr) {
    const p = parseInt(pageStr, 10)
    if (!isNaN(p) && p > 0) filters.page = p
  }

  const pageSizeStr = getParam('pageSize')
  if (pageSizeStr) {
    const ps = parseInt(pageSizeStr, 10)
    if (!isNaN(ps) && ps > 0) filters.pageSize = ps
  }

  // Sorting
  const sortKey = getParam('sortKey')
  if (sortKey) filters.sortKey = sortKey

  const sortDirection = getParam('sortDirection')
  if (sortDirection === 'asc' || sortDirection === 'desc') {
    filters.sortDirection = sortDirection
  }

  // Practical-specific
  const batch_group = getParam('batch_group')
  if (batch_group) filters.batch_group = batch_group

  const subject_name = getParam('subject_name')
  if (subject_name) filters.subject_name = subject_name

  const teacher_id = getParam('teacher_id')
  if (teacher_id) filters.teacher_id = teacher_id

  const teacher_name = getParam('teacher_name')
  if (teacher_name) filters.teacher_name = teacher_name

  const period_label = getParam('period_label')
  if (period_label) filters.period_label = period_label

  const remarks = getParam('remarks')
  if (remarks) filters.remarks = remarks

  const groupBy = getParam('groupBy')
  if (groupBy) filters.groupBy = groupBy

  // Incident-specific
  const severity = getParam('severity')
  if (severity) filters.severity = severity

  const incident_type = getParam('incident_type')
  if (incident_type) filters.incident_type = incident_type

  const equipment_name = getParam('equipment_name')
  if (equipment_name) filters.equipment_name = equipment_name

  const title = getParam('title')
  if (title) filters.title = title

  // Maintenance-specific
  const asset_identifier = getParam('asset_identifier')
  if (asset_identifier) filters.asset_identifier = asset_identifier

  const plan_category = getParam('plan_category')
  if (plan_category) filters.plan_category = plan_category

  const technician_id = getParam('technician_id')
  if (technician_id) filters.technician_id = technician_id

  const maintenance_status = getParam('maintenance_status')
  if (maintenance_status) filters.maintenance_status = maintenance_status

  // Density
  const density = getParam('density') as PrintDensityMode | undefined
  if (density === 'compact' || density === 'detailed') {
    filters.density = density
  }

  // WYSIWYG selection and column projection
  const cols = getParam('cols')
  if (cols) filters.cols = cols

  const selected = getParam('selected')
  if (selected) filters.selected = selected

  const selKey = getParam('selKey')
  if (selKey) filters.selKey = selKey

  const selectedAll = getParam('selectedAll')
  if (selectedAll === 'true' || selectedAll === '1') {
    filters.selectedAll = true
  }

  return filters
}

/**
 * Truncates text at a natural word boundary rather than slicing mid-word.
 * E.g. avoids "...capacit[1]" by slicing to the last space and cleaning trailing punctuation.
 */
export function truncateAtWordBoundary(
  text: string | null | undefined,
  maxChars: number
): { text: string; truncated: boolean } {
  if (!text) return { text: '', truncated: false }
  const clean = text.trim()
  if (clean.length <= maxChars) {
    return { text: clean, truncated: false }
  }

  let sliced = clean.slice(0, maxChars)
  const lastSpace = sliced.lastIndexOf(' ')
  // Only back up to word boundary if it doesn't discard more than half the allocated space
  if (lastSpace > maxChars * 0.4) {
    sliced = sliced.slice(0, lastSpace)
  }

  // Remove trailing punctuation before appending ellipsis
  sliced = sliced.replace(/[,;:. \-_]+$/, '')

  return {
    text: sliced,
    truncated: true,
  }
}

export interface ExportScopeDecision {
  mode: 'SELECTED_EXPLICIT' | 'ALL_MATCHING_EXPLICIT' | 'FILTERED_DEFAULT'
  selectedIds?: string[]
  filterSummary: string
  traceabilityNote: string
}

/**
 * Single pure decision function for export/print scope precedence and audit traceability.
 * Precedence:
 * 1. If selectedIds is non-empty and not isAllMatchingSelected: overrides all filters; mode is SELECTED_EXPLICIT.
 * 2. If isAllMatchingSelected is true: executes current filters across all pages, flagged as ALL_MATCHING_EXPLICIT.
 * 3. Default: executes current filters, mode is FILTERED_DEFAULT.
 */
export function resolveExportPrintScope(params: {
  selectedIds?: string[]
  isAllMatchingSelected?: boolean
  totalCount: number
  filters: Record<string, any>
  sortKey?: string
  sortDirection?: string
}): ExportScopeDecision {
  const { selectedIds = [], isAllMatchingSelected = false, totalCount, filters, sortKey, sortDirection } = params

  const parts: string[] = []
  if (filters.lab_id && filters.lab_id !== 'all') parts.push(`Facility: ${filters.lab_id.toUpperCase()}`)
  if (filters.batch_group && filters.batch_group !== 'all') parts.push(`Class: ${filters.batch_group}`)
  if (filters.subject_name && filters.subject_name !== 'all') parts.push(`Subject: ${filters.subject_name}`)
  if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') parts.push(`Status: ${filters.status}`)
  if (filters.severity && filters.severity !== 'all') parts.push(`Severity: ${filters.severity}`)
  if (filters.date_from || filters.date_to) {
    parts.push(`Date: ${filters.date_from || 'Earliest'} to ${filters.date_to || 'Latest'}`)
  }
  if (filters.search && filters.search.trim()) parts.push(`Search: "${filters.search.trim()}"`)

  const filterSummary = parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' | ') : 'All Records (No filter)'
  const sortSummary = sortKey ? `${sortKey} (${sortDirection || 'asc'})` : 'Default'

  if (selectedIds.length > 0 && !isAllMatchingSelected) {
    return {
      mode: 'SELECTED_EXPLICIT',
      selectedIds,
      filterSummary: `Explicit Selection (${selectedIds.length} records)`,
      traceabilityNote: `Generated from: ${selectedIds.length} selected records · Scope: Explicit Selection · Sorted by: ${sortSummary}`,
    }
  }

  if (isAllMatchingSelected) {
    return {
      mode: 'ALL_MATCHING_EXPLICIT',
      filterSummary,
      traceabilityNote: `Generated from: ${totalCount} records (All Matching Selected) · Filters: ${filterSummary} · Sorted by: ${sortSummary}`,
    }
  }

  return {
    mode: 'FILTERED_DEFAULT',
    filterSummary,
    traceabilityNote: `Generated from: ${totalCount} records · Filters: ${filterSummary} · Sorted by: ${sortSummary}`,
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\report-spec\incident-spec.ts ===
import type { RecordReportSpec } from '../../types/report-spec'
import type { IncidentRecordItem } from '../../types/records'

export function formatIncidentCaseNumber(id?: string | null, incidentNumber?: string | null): string {
  if (incidentNumber && incidentNumber.trim()) {
    const trimmed = incidentNumber.trim()
    return trimmed.startsWith('#') ? trimmed : `#${trimmed}`
  }
  if (!id) return '#INC-—'
  const parts = id.split('-')
  if (parts.length >= 3) {
    const suffix = parts[parts.length - 1].toUpperCase()
    return `#INC-${suffix}`
  } else if (parts.length === 2 && parts[0].toLowerCase() === 'inc') {
    return `#INC-${parts[1].toUpperCase()}`
  }
  return `#INC-${id.slice(-4).toUpperCase()}`
}

import type { RecordReportSpec } from '../../types/report-spec'
import type { PracticalRecordItem } from '../../types/records'

/**
 * Sanitizes subject names to defensively strip repeating course code prefixes.
 * E.g. "COMP-12 - COMP-12 — Data Structures & Algorithms Lab" -> "COMP-12 — Data Structures & Algorithms Lab"
 * E.g. "COMP-12 - Data Structures & Algorithms Lab" -> "Data Structures & Algorithms Lab"
 */
export function sanitizeSubjectTitle(subject: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!subject || !subject.trim()) return '—'
  let s = subject.trim()
  // Clean repeating code prefix: "COMP-12 - COMP-12 — ..." -> "COMP-12 — ..."
  s = s.replace(/^([A-Z0-9-]+)\s*[-–—]\s*\1\s*[-–—]\s*/i, '$1 — ')
  return s
}

/**
 * Canonical Class Alias Map
 * Maps canonical institutional classes from the Admin Panel to known historical/legacy variations.
 */
export const CLASS_ALIAS_MAP: Record<string, string[]> = {
  '12 Eng': ['12 Eng', '12C', '12C - Tech Stream Sec A', '12C - Tech Stream', '12-Eng', 'Class 12', '12C-A'],
  '11 Eng': ['11 Eng', '11 Sc', '11 Sc - Science Stream Sec B', '11 Sc - Science Sec B', '11-Eng', 'Class 11', '11Sc-B', '11Sc'],
  '12 Mgt': ['12 Mgt', '12 Mgt - Mgmt Stream Sec A', '12-Mgt'],
  '11 Mgt': ['11 Mgt', '11C - Computer Tech Sec A', '11C', '11-Mgt'],
  '10 Eng': ['10 Eng', 'Class 10', 'Grade 10'],
  '9 Eng': ['9 Eng', 'Class 9', 'Grade 9'],
  '8A': ['8A', 'Grade 8A'],
  '7B': ['7B', 'Grade 7B'],
  '6A': ['6A', 'Grade 6A'],
}

/**
 * Resolves a class name into all known query aliases for backward compatibility.
 */
export function getClassAliases(className: string | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!className || className === 'all') return []
  return CLASS_ALIAS_MAP[className] || [className]
}

/**
 * Shortens institutional class strings to clean codes.
 * E.g. "12 Eng - Computer Engineering" -> "12 Eng", "12C - Tech Stream Sec A" -> "12C-A".
 * Explicit Fallback: Returns raw original string verbatim if unrecognized.
 */
export function formatClassCode(batchGroup: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!batchGroup || !batchGroup.trim()) return '—'
  const clean = batchGroup.trim()

  // 1. Canonical Admin Panel Classes: e.g. "12 Eng - Computer Engineering" -> "12 Eng", "8A - General Science" -> "8A"
  const matchCanonical = clean.match(/^(\d+\s*(?:Eng|Mgt)|[6789][A-Z]|\d+)\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s&-]+$/i)
  if (matchCanonical) {
    return matchCanonical[1].trim()
  }

  // Exact match for already-canonical class names: "12 Eng", "11 Eng", "12 Mgt", "8A", "7B", "6A"
  if (/^(\d+\s*(?:Eng|Mgt)|[6789][A-Z])$/i.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // 2. Grade + Stream + Section: "12C - Tech Stream Sec A" -> "12C-A", "11 Sc - Science Stream Sec B" -> "11Sc-B"
  const matchSec = clean.match(/^(\d+[A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s-]+)?(?:\s+(?:Sec|Section)\s+([A-Za-z0-9]+))$/i)
  if (matchSec) {
    const base = matchSec[1].replace(/\s+/g, '').trim()
    const sec = matchSec[2].trim()
    return `${base}-${sec}`
  }

  // 3. Grade format: "Grade 11 - Sci A" -> "11-SciA", "Grade 12 - Sci B" -> "12-SciB"
  const matchGrade = clean.match(/^Grade\s+(\d+)\s*-\s*([A-Za-z0-9\s-]+)$/i)
  if (matchGrade) {
    return `${matchGrade[1]}-${matchGrade[2].replace(/\s+/g, '')}`
  }

  // 4. Simple class format: "Class 10", "Class 11", "Class 6" -> "Class 10", "Class 11"
  if (/^Class\s+\d+$/i.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // 5. Grade + Stream without section: "12B - Bio-Science Stream" -> "12B", "11 Sc - Science Stream" -> "11Sc"
  const matchStream = clean.match(/^(\d+[A-Za-z\s]*?)\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s-]+(?:Stream)?$/i)
  if (matchStream) {
    return matchStream[1].replace(/\s+/g, '').trim()
  }

  // 6. Short code already: "12C", "11A" -> return as-is
  if (/^\d+[A-Za-z]{1,2}$/.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // EXPLICIT FALLBACK: Return raw original string verbatim, never mangled!
  return clean
}

=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\roster-ingestion\parser.ts ===
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs'
import type { RawRosterRow, ParsedRosterRow } from './types'

/**
 * Normalizes header strings for flexible column matching
 */
function normalizeHeader(header: string): string {
  return header.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
}

function matchColumnType(
  header: string
): 'roll' | 'name' | 'adm' | 'reg' | 'gender' | 'contact' | null {
  const norm = normalizeHeader(header)
  if (['roll', 'rollno', 'rollnumber', 'sn', 'snno', 'symbolno'].includes(norm)) {
    return 'roll'
  }
  if (['name', 'studentname', 'fullname', 'nameofstudent'].includes(norm)) {
    return 'name'
  }
  // Matches RFID No., Card No., Student ID, or Admission No.
  if (
    [
      'rfid',
      'rfidno',
      'rfidnumber',
      'cardno',
      'smartcardno',
      'admissionno',
      'admissionnumber',
      'admno',
      'admnumber',
      'studentid',
      'admid',
      'idno',
    ].includes(norm)
  ) {
    return 'adm'
  }
  if (['registrationno', 'registrationnumber', 'regno', 'regnumber', 'boardreg', 'boardregno'].includes(norm)) {
    return 'reg'
  }
  if (['gender', 'sex'].includes(norm)) {
    return 'gender'
  }
  if (['contact', 'phone', 'phoneno', 'mobile', 'guardianphone', 'contactno'].includes(norm)) {
    return 'contact'
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * Parses CSV text into raw key-value rows
 */
function parseCsvContent(csvText: string): RawRosterRow[] {
  const lines = csvText.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l.length > 0)
  if (lines.length < 2) return []

  // Split lines accounting for quotes
  const parseLine = (line: string): string[] => {
    const result: string[] = []
    let cur = ''
    let inQuotes = false

    for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
      const c = line[i]
      if (c === '"') {
        inQuotes = !inQuotes
      } else if (c === ',' && !inQuotes) {
        result.push(cur.trim())
        cur = ''
      } else {
        cur += c
      }
    }
    result.push(cur.trim())
    return result
  }

  // Find header line
  let headerIndex = -1
  let colMap: {
    roll?: number
    name?: number
    adm?: number
    reg?: number
    gender?: number
    contact?: number
  } = {}

  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(lines.length, 10); i++) {
    const cols = parseLine(lines[i])
    const found: typeof colMap = {}
    cols.forEach((col, idx) => {
      const match = matchColumnType(col)
      if (match === 'roll') found.roll = idx
      if (match === 'name') found.name = idx
      if (match === 'adm') found.adm = idx
      if (match === 'reg') found.reg = idx
      if (match === 'gender') found.gender = idx
      if (match === 'contact') found.contact = idx
    })

    // Student Name is the ONLY required column to identify a valid roster header
    if (found.name !== undefined) {
      headerIndex = i
      colMap = found
      break
    }
  }

  if (headerIndex === -1) {
    throw new Error("Could not find required header ('Full Name' or 'Student Name') in CSV file.")
  }

  const rows: RawRosterRow[] = []
  for (let i = headerIndex + 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const cols = parseLine(lines[i])
    if (cols.length === 0 || cols.every((c) => c === '')) continue

    const rollVal = colMap.roll !== undefined ? cols[colMap.roll] : null
    const nameVal = colMap.name !== undefined ? cols[colMap.name] : null
    const admVal = colMap.adm !== undefined ? cols[colMap.adm] : null
    const regVal = colMap.reg !== undefined ? cols[colMap.reg] : null
    const genderVal = colMap.gender !== undefined ? cols[colMap.gender] : null
    const contactVal = colMap.contact !== undefined ? cols[colMap.contact] : null

    rows.push({
      roll_number: rollVal,
      full_name: nameVal,
      admission_no: admVal,
      registration_no: regVal,
      gender: genderVal,
      contact: contactVal,
    })
  }

  return rows
}

/**
 * Parses Excel workbook (.xlsx / .xls) buffer into raw key-value rows
 */
async function parseExcelBuffer(buffer: Buffer): Promise<RawRosterRow[]> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  await workbook.xlsx.load(buffer as any)

  const worksheet = workbook.worksheets[0]
  if (!worksheet) {
    throw new Error('Spreadsheet contains no worksheets.')
  }

  let headerRowIndex = -1
  let colMap: {
    roll?: number
    name?: number
    adm?: number
    reg?: number
    gender?: number
    contact?: number
  } = {}

  // Scan first 15 rows for header row
  worksheet.eachRow({ includeEmpty: false }, (row, rowNumber) => {
    if (headerRowIndex !== -1 || rowNumber > 15) return

    const found: typeof colMap = {}
    row.eachCell({ includeEmpty: false }, (cell, colNumber) => {
      const text = cell.text?.toString() || ''
      const match = matchColumnType(text)
      if (match === 'roll') found.roll = colNumber
      if (match === 'name') found.name = colNumber
      if (match === 'adm') found.adm = colNumber
      if (match === 'reg') found.reg = colNumber
      if (match === 'gender') found.gender = colNumber
      if (match === 'contact') found.contact = colNumber
    })

    // Student Name is the ONLY mandatory column
    if (found.name !== undefined) {
      headerRowIndex = rowNumber
      colMap = found
    }
  })

  if (headerRowIndex === -1) {
    throw new Error("Could not find required column header ('Student Name' or 'Full Name') in worksheet.")
  }

  const rows: RawRosterRow[] = []
  worksheet.eachRow({ includeEmpty: false }, (row, rowNumber) => {
    if (rowNumber <= headerRowIndex) return

    const rollCell = colMap.roll ? row.getCell(colMap.roll) : null
    const nameCell = colMap.name ? row.getCell(colMap.name) : null
    const admCell = colMap.adm ? row.getCell(colMap.adm) : null
    const regCell = colMap.reg ? row.getCell(colMap.reg) : null
    const genderCell = colMap.gender ? row.getCell(colMap.gender) : null
    const contactCell = colMap.contact ? row.getCell(colMap.contact) : null

    const rollText = rollCell?.text?.trim() || ''
    const nameText = nameCell?.text?.trim() || ''
    const admText = admCell?.text?.trim() || ''
    const regText = regCell?.text?.trim() || ''
    const genderText = genderCell?.text?.trim() || ''
    const contactText = contactCell?.text?.trim() || ''

    // Skip entirely blank rows
    if (!rollText && !nameText && !admText && !regText && !genderText && !contactText) return

    rows.push({
      roll_number: rollText,
      full_name: nameText,
      admission_no: admText,
      registration_no: regText,
      gender: genderText,
      contact: contactText,
    })
  })

  return rows
}

/**
 * Universal multi-format spreadsheet parser (.xlsx, .xls, .csv)
 */
export async function parseRosterFile(
  fileBuffer: Buffer,
  fileName: string
): Promise<ParsedRosterRow[]> {
  const isCsv = fileName.toLowerCase().endsWith('.csv')
  const rawRows: RawRosterRow[] = isCsv
    ? parseCsvContent(fileBuffer.toString('utf8'))
    : await parseExcelBuffer(fileBuffer)

  if (rawRows.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('The uploaded spreadsheet does not contain any student rows.')
  }

  // Convert raw rows to clean structured ParsedRosterRows
  const parsedRows: ParsedRosterRow[] = []

  rawRows.forEach((row, idx) => {
    const rowIndex = idx + 1

    // Extract roll number as integer (defaults to null if absent/not provided)
    let rollNum: number | null = null
    if (typeof row.roll_number === 'number') {
      rollNum = Math.floor(row.roll_number)
    } else if (typeof row.roll_number === 'string' && row.roll_number.trim() !== '') {
      const match = row.roll_number.match(/\d+/)
      rollNum = match ? parseInt(match[0], 10) : null
    }

    const cleanName = (row.full_name || '').trim()
    const cleanAdm = (row.admission_no || '').trim() || null
    const cleanReg = (row.registration_no || '').trim() || null

    // Normalize optional gender
    let cleanGender: 'male' | 'female' | 'other' | null = null
    if (row.gender) {
      const gNorm = row.gender.toString().toLowerCase().trim()
      if (gNorm.startsWith('m')) cleanGender = 'male'
      else if (gNorm.startsWith('f')) cleanGender = 'female'
      else if (gNorm.startsWith('o')) cleanGender = 'other'
    }

    const cleanContact = (row.contact || '').toString().trim() || null

    parsedRows.push({
      row_index: rowIndex,
      roll_number: rollNum,
      full_name: cleanName,
      admission_no: cleanAdm,
      registration_no: cleanReg,
      gender: cleanGender,
      contact: cleanContact,
    })
  })

  return parsedRows
}

/**
 * Roster Ingestion and Cohort Lifecycle Types
 * Strict 4-column LMS specification: Roll Number, Full Name, Admission No, Registration No
 */

export type IngestionMode = 'sync' | 'clean_slate'

export interface RawRosterRow {
  roll_number?: number | string | null
  full_name?: string | null
  admission_no?: string | null
  registration_no?: string | null
  gender?: string | null
  contact?: string | null
}

export interface ParsedRosterRow {
  row_index: number
  roll_number: number | null
  full_name: string
  admission_no: string | null
  registration_no: string | null
  gender?: 'male' | 'female' | 'other' | null
  contact?: string | null
}

export type RowValidationStatus =
  | 'valid_new'          // Clean new student person entity + cohort enrollment
  | 'valid_existing'     // Existing student (matched by strong ID) + new cohort enrollment
  | 'valid_retained'     // Existing student already enrolled with same roll (idempotent retention)
  | 'valid_roll_update'  // Existing student matched, safe roll shift/swap in cohort
  | 'review_needed'      // Advisory review (e.g. name similarity or changed name on same roll)
  | 'warning'            // Advisory warning
  | 'error'              // Blocking fatal error (duplicate roll, collision, identifier mismatch)

export interface ValidationRowPreview {
  row_index: number
  roll_number: number | null
  full_name: string
  admission_no: string | null
  is_auto_admission_no?: boolean
  registration_no: string | null
  gender?: 'male' | 'female' | 'other' | null
  contact?: string | null
  status: RowValidationStatus
  matched_student_id?: string | null
  matched_student_name?: string | null
  old_roll_number?: number | null
  diff_badge?: 'matched_exact' | 'roll_changed' | 'name_typo' | 'brand_new' | 'collision'
  error_message?: string | null
  warning_message?: string | null
  existing_student_id?: string | null
  existing_student_name?: string | null
  existing_admission_no?: string | null
  match_confidence?: 'admission_exact' | 'name_only' | 'none'
  user_override_decision?: 'confirm_match' | 'treat_as_new'
}

export interface UnmatchedExistingStudent {
  id: string
  student_id: string
  roll_number: number
  full_name: string
  admission_no: string | null
  registration_no: string | null
  workstation_label?: string | null
}

export interface RosterBuckets {
  bucket1_auto_confirmed: ValidationRowPreview[]
  bucket2_review_needed: ValidationRowPreview[]
  bucket3_clean_new: ValidationRowPreview[]
  bucket4_unmatched_existing: UnmatchedExistingStudent[]
}

export interface RosterPreflightReport {
  target_academic_year_id: string
  target_academic_year_name: string
  target_class_id: string
  target_class_name: string
  mode: IngestionMode
  clean_slate_eligible: boolean
  existing_cohort_count: number
  existing_attendance_logs_count: number
  total_rows: number
  valid_count: number
  new_students_count: number
  existing_students_count: number
  retained_enrollments_count: number
  updated_enrollments_count: number
  warning_count: number
  error_count: number
  can_commit: boolean
  buckets: RosterBuckets
  rows: ValidationRowPreview[]
}

export interface RosterCommitResult {
  success: boolean
  mode?: IngestionMode
  processed_rows: number
  students_created: number
  students_matched: number
  enrollments_created: number
  enrollments_retained: number
  enrollments_updated?: number
  error?: string
}

export interface IngestionPayloadRow {
  roll_number: number
  full_name: string
  admission_no: string | null
  registration_no: string | null
  gender?: string | null
  contact?: string | null
  student_id?: string | null
  action?: 'sync' | 'replace_occupant' | 'assign_next_roll' | null
}

import type {
  ParsedRosterRow,
  ValidationRowPreview,
  RosterPreflightReport,
  IngestionMode,
  UnmatchedExistingStudent,
} from './types'
import {
  formatStudentDisplayName,
  normalizeStudentName,
  calculateNameSimilarity,
} from '../imports/string-normalization'

export interface DatabaseValidationContext {
  targetAcademicYearId: string
  targetAcademicYearName: string
  targetClassId: string
  targetClassName: string
  // Existing students in institution
  existingStudentsByAdmissionNo: Map<
    string,
    { id: string; full_name: string; admission_no: string | null; registration_no: string | null }
  >
  existingStudentsByRegistrationNo: Map<
    string,
    { id: string; full_name: string; admission_no: string | null; registration_no: string | null }
  >
  allActiveStudents: Array<{ id: string; full_name: string; admission_no: string | null }>
  // Existing enrollments in target cohort (academic_year_id, class_id)
  existingEnrollmentsByStudentId: Map<string, { id: string; roll_number: number }>
  existingEnrollmentsByRoll: Map<
    number,
    { id: string; student_id: string; full_name: string; admission_no: string | null; registration_no: string | null }
  >
  existingCohortStudents?: Array<UnmatchedExistingStudent>
  existingAttendanceLogsCount?: number
  mode?: IngestionMode
}

/**
 * Checks exact normalized name match (including token permutations like "Sharma Aarav" vs "Aarav Sharma")
 * without fuzzy Levenshtein blurring, avoiding silent identity mixups.
 */
export function isExactNormalizedNameMatch(nameA: string, nameB: string): boolean {
  const normA = normalizeStudentName(nameA)
  const normB = normalizeStudentName(nameB)
  if (!normA || !normB) return false
  if (normA === normB) return true
  const tokensA = normA.split(' ').sort().join(' ')
  const tokensB = normB.split(' ').sort().join(' ')
  return tokensA === tokensB
}

/**
 * Formats a clean sequential institutional admission number:
 * Format: ADM-[YEAR]-[SEQ]
 * Example: ADM-2083-0001
 * Completely decoupled from mutable class names or roll numbers.
 */
export function formatSequentialAdmissionNo(
  academicYearId: string,
  seq: number
): string {
  const cleanYear = (academicYearId || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '')
  const padSeq = String(seq).padStart(seq < 1000 ? 4 : 5, '0')
  return `ADM-${cleanYear}-${padSeq}`
}

/**
 * Legacy compatibility alias for formatAutoAdmissionNo
 * Returns format: ADM-[YEAR]-[CLASS]-[ROLL]
 */
export function formatAutoAdmissionNo(
  academicYearId: string,
  classId: string,
  rollNumber: number
): string {
  const cleanYear = (academicYearId || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '')
  const cleanClass = (classId || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '')
  const padRoll = String(rollNumber).padStart(rollNumber < 100 ? 2 : 3, '0')
  return `ADM-${cleanYear}-${cleanClass}-${padRoll}`
}

/**
 * Pre-flight validation pipeline with deterministic reconciliation,
 * automatic provisional roll allocation for sheets missing roll numbers,
 * and support for both 'sync' and 'clean_slate' modes.
 */
export function validateParsedRoster(
  rows: ParsedRosterRow[],
  context: DatabaseValidationContext
): RosterPreflightReport {
  const mode: IngestionMode = context.mode || 'sync'
  const cleanSlateEligible = (context.existingAttendanceLogsCount ?? 0) === 0

  // Intra-file uniqueness tracking sets
  const rollCounts = new Map<number, number>()
  const admCounts = new Map<string, number>()
  const regCounts = new Map<string, number>()

  // 1. Gather all occupied rolls from existing database cohort
  const occupiedRolls = new Set<number>()
  if (context.existingEnrollmentsByRoll) {
    context.existingEnrollmentsByRoll.forEach((_, r) => occupiedRolls.add(r))
  }

  // 2. Tally explicitly provided rolls from uploaded file
  rows.forEach((r) => {
    if (r.roll_number !== null && r.roll_number !== undefined && r.roll_number > 0 && r.roll_number <= 200) {
      rollCounts.set(r.roll_number, (rollCounts.get(r.roll_number) || 0) + 1)
      occupiedRolls.add(r.roll_number)
    }
  })

  // 3. Dynamic provisional roll allocator for rows where roll number was omitted (null/undefined)
  let nextAvailableRoll = 1
  const allocateProvisionalRoll = (): number => {
    while (occupiedRolls.has(nextAvailableRoll) && nextAvailableRoll <= 200) {
      nextAvailableRoll++
    }
    const allocated = nextAvailableRoll <= 200 ? nextAvailableRoll : 200
    occupiedRolls.add(allocated)
    nextAvailableRoll++
    return allocated
  }

  // 4. Pre-process rows with effective non-colliding rolls and clean institutional IDs
  let autoAdmissionSeq = (context.allActiveStudents?.length || 0) + 1
  const effectiveRows = rows.map((r) => {
    const isRollOmitted = r.roll_number === null || r.roll_number === undefined
    const effectiveRoll: number = isRollOmitted ? allocateProvisionalRoll() : (r.roll_number ?? 0)

    const rawAdm = r.admission_no?.trim() || null
    const isAutoAdmission = !rawAdm
    // If rawAdm is provided (e.g. RFID No. '1641052469'), preserve verbatim.
    // If absent, synthesize a clean sequential admission number.
    const effectiveAdm = rawAdm || formatSequentialAdmissionNo(context.targetAcademicYearId, autoAdmissionSeq++)

    return {
      ...r,
      isRollOmitted,
      effectiveRoll,
      effectiveAdm,
      rawAdm,
      isAutoAdmission,
    }
  })

  // Track admission & registration frequency across effective rows
  effectiveRows.forEach((r) => {
    if (r.effectiveAdm) {
      const normAdm = r.effectiveAdm.toLowerCase().trim()
      admCounts.set(normAdm, (admCounts.get(normAdm) || 0) + 1)
    }
    if (r.registration_no) {
      const normReg = r.registration_no.toLowerCase().trim()
      regCounts.set(normReg, (regCounts.get(normReg) || 0) + 1)
    }
  })

  let validCount = 0
  let newStudentsCount = 0
  let existingStudentsCount = 0
  let retainedEnrollmentsCount = 0
  let updatedEnrollmentsCount = 0
  let warningCount = 0
  let errorCount = 0

  const matchedStudentIdsInUpload = new Set<string>()
  const previews: ValidationRowPreview[] = []

  // If clean slate mode requested but attendance history exists, block early
  if (mode === 'clean_slate' && !cleanSlateEligible) {
    errorCount++
  }

  // Pass 2: Row-level validation and identity reconciliation
  effectiveRows.forEach((row) => {
    const rawName = row.full_name.trim()
    const displayName = formatStudentDisplayName(rawName)
    const roll = row.effectiveRoll
    const rawAdm = row.rawAdm
    const isAutoAdmission = row.isAutoAdmission
    const adm = row.effectiveAdm
    const reg = row.registration_no?.trim() || null
    const currentOccupantAtRoll = context.existingEnrollmentsByRoll.get(roll)

    let rowStatus: ValidationRowPreview['status'] = 'valid_new'
    let errorMessage: string | null = null
    let warningMessage: string | null = null
    let matchedStudentId: string | null = null
    let matchedStudentName: string | null = null
    let oldRollNumber: number | null = null
    let diffBadge: ValidationRowPreview['diff_badge'] = 'brand_new'
    let matchConfidence: ValidationRowPreview['match_confidence'] = 'none'
    let existingStudentId: string | null = null
    let existingStudentName: string | null = null
    let existingAdmissionNo: string | null = null

    // A. Syntax & Intra-File Checks
    const rollToCheck = row.isRollOmitted ? row.effectiveRoll : (row.roll_number ?? 0)
    if (!rawName) {
      rowStatus = 'error'
      errorMessage = 'Student full name is required.'
    } else if (rollToCheck <= 0 || rollToCheck > 200) {
      rowStatus = 'error'
      errorMessage = `Roll number (${rollToCheck}) is out of valid range (1–200).`
    } else if (!row.isRollOmitted && row.roll_number !== null && (rollCounts.get(row.roll_number) || 0) > 1) {
      rowStatus = 'error'
      errorMessage = `Duplicate roll number ${row.roll_number} appears multiple times inside the spreadsheet.`
    } else if (rawAdm && (admCounts.get(rawAdm.toLowerCase()) || 0) > 1) {
      rowStatus = 'error'
      errorMessage = `Duplicate admission / RFID number ${rawAdm} appears multiple times inside the spreadsheet.`
    } else if (reg && (regCounts.get(reg.toLowerCase()) || 0) > 1) {
      rowStatus = 'error'
      errorMessage = `Duplicate registration number ${reg} appears multiple times inside the spreadsheet.`
    }

    // B. Reconcile based on mode
    if (rowStatus !== 'error') {
      if (mode === 'clean_slate') {
        if (!cleanSlateEligible) {
          rowStatus = 'error'
          errorMessage = `Cannot perform clean slate replacement: ${context.existingAttendanceLogsCount} attendance record(s) exist for this cohort.`
        } else {
          const studentByAdm = rawAdm ? context.existingStudentsByAdmissionNo.get(rawAdm.toLowerCase()) : null
          const studentByReg = reg ? context.existingStudentsByRegistrationNo.get(reg.toLowerCase()) : null

          if (studentByAdm && studentByReg && studentByAdm.id !== studentByReg.id) {
            rowStatus = 'error'
            errorMessage = `Identity conflict: Admission No '${rawAdm}' and Registration No '${reg}' resolve to two different students.`
          } else {
            const matched = studentByAdm || studentByReg
            if (matched) {
              matchedStudentId = matched.id
              matchedStudentName = matched.full_name
              matchedStudentIdsInUpload.add(matched.id)
              rowStatus = 'valid_existing'
              diffBadge = 'matched_exact'
              matchConfidence = 'admission_exact'
              existingStudentId = matched.id
              existingStudentName = matched.full_name
              existingAdmissionNo = matched.admission_no
            } else {
              rowStatus = 'valid_new'
              diffBadge = 'brand_new'
              matchConfidence = 'none'
            }
          }
        }
      } else {
        // Mode: 'sync'
        // Deterministic Matching Ladder
        const studentByAdm = rawAdm ? context.existingStudentsByAdmissionNo.get(rawAdm.toLowerCase()) : null
        const studentByReg = reg ? context.existingStudentsByRegistrationNo.get(reg.toLowerCase()) : null

        if (studentByAdm && studentByReg && studentByAdm.id !== studentByReg.id) {
          rowStatus = 'error'
          errorMessage = `Identity conflict: Admission / RFID '${rawAdm}' belongs to ${studentByAdm.full_name}, but Registration No '${reg}' belongs to ${studentByReg.full_name}.`
        } else if (studentByAdm || studentByReg) {
          // Strong match by provided identifier (100% confidence)
          const matched = (studentByAdm || studentByReg)!
          matchedStudentId = matched.id
          matchedStudentName = matched.full_name
          matchedStudentIdsInUpload.add(matched.id)
          matchConfidence = 'admission_exact'
          existingStudentId = matched.id
          existingStudentName = matched.full_name
          existingAdmissionNo = matched.admission_no

          const existingEnrollment = context.existingEnrollmentsByStudentId.get(matched.id)

          if (existingEnrollment) {
            if (existingEnrollment.roll_number === roll) {
              rowStatus = 'valid_retained'
              diffBadge = 'matched_exact'
            } else {
              rowStatus = 'valid_roll_update'
              oldRollNumber = existingEnrollment.roll_number
              diffBadge = 'roll_changed'
              warningMessage = `Roll number shifted from ${existingEnrollment.roll_number} → ${roll}.`
            }
          } else {
            if (currentOccupantAtRoll && currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id !== matched.id) {
              rowStatus = 'review_needed'
              diffBadge = 'collision'
              warningMessage = `Roll ${roll} is currently held by "${currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name}". Reassigning to incoming student "${matched.full_name}".`
            } else {
              rowStatus = 'valid_existing'
              diffBadge = 'brand_new'
            }
          }
        } else {
          // When Admission / RFID is omitted (or provided identifier not found):
          // Follow Deterministic In-Class Matching Ladder:

          // Rule 1: Exact Roll + Exact Normalized Name Match
          if (currentOccupantAtRoll && isExactNormalizedNameMatch(rawName, currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name)) {
            matchedStudentId = currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id
            matchedStudentName = currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name
            matchedStudentIdsInUpload.add(currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id)
            rowStatus = 'valid_retained'
            diffBadge = 'matched_exact'
            matchConfidence = 'name_only'
            existingStudentId = currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id
            existingStudentName = currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name
            existingAdmissionNo = currentOccupantAtRoll.admission_no
          } else {
            // Rule 2: Unique In-Class Name Match (Roll Shift)
            const cohortNameMatches = (context.existingCohortStudents || []).filter((s) =>
              isExactNormalizedNameMatch(rawName, s.full_name)
            )

            if (cohortNameMatches.length === 1) {
              const shiftedStudent = cohortNameMatches[0]
              matchedStudentId = shiftedStudent.student_id
              matchedStudentName = shiftedStudent.full_name
              matchedStudentIdsInUpload.add(shiftedStudent.student_id)
              rowStatus = 'valid_roll_update'
              oldRollNumber = shiftedStudent.roll_number
              diffBadge = 'roll_changed'
              matchConfidence = 'name_only'
              existingStudentId = shiftedStudent.student_id
              existingStudentName = shiftedStudent.full_name
              existingAdmissionNo = shiftedStudent.admission_no
              warningMessage = `Roll number shifted from ${shiftedStudent.roll_number} → ${roll}.`
            } else if (cohortNameMatches.length > 1) {
              // DUPLICATE NAME HAZARD: Multiple students in this class have the exact same name
              rowStatus = 'review_needed'
              diffBadge = 'collision'
              matchConfidence = 'name_only'
              warningMessage = `Duplicate Name Hazard: ${cohortNameMatches.length} students named "${displayName}" exist in this cohort. Match via Admission / RFID No. or manual review required.`
            } else if (currentOccupantAtRoll && !row.isRollOmitted) {
              // Rule 3: Same Roll, Different Name Spelling
              rowStatus = 'review_needed'
              diffBadge = 'name_typo'
              matchedStudentId = currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id
              matchedStudentName = currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name
              matchConfidence = 'none'
              existingStudentId = currentOccupantAtRoll.student_id
              existingStudentName = currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name
              existingAdmissionNo = currentOccupantAtRoll.admission_no
              warningMessage = `Roll ${roll} is currently held by "${currentOccupantAtRoll.full_name}". Incoming name is "${displayName}". Manual review required.`
            } else {
              // Rule 4: New Roll & Unrecognized Name
              // Check institution-wide advisory similarity
              const similar = context.allActiveStudents.find(
                (s) => calculateNameSimilarity(rawName, s.full_name) >= 0.85
              )

              if (similar) {
                rowStatus = 'review_needed'
                diffBadge = 'name_typo'
                matchConfidence = 'none'
                existingStudentId = similar.id
                existingStudentName = similar.full_name
                existingAdmissionNo = similar.admission_no
                warningMessage = `Advisory: Similar name to existing student "${similar.full_name}"${
                  similar.admission_no ? ` (${similar.admission_no})` : ''
                }. Confirm if this is a new student.`
              } else {
                rowStatus = 'valid_new'
                diffBadge = 'brand_new'
                matchConfidence = 'none'
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Tally metrics
    if (rowStatus === 'error') {
      errorCount++
    } else {
      validCount++
      if (rowStatus === 'valid_new') newStudentsCount++
      if (rowStatus === 'valid_existing') existingStudentsCount++
      if (rowStatus === 'valid_retained') retainedEnrollmentsCount++
      if (rowStatus === 'valid_roll_update') updatedEnrollmentsCount++
      if (rowStatus === 'review_needed') {
        warningCount++
        newStudentsCount++
      }
    }

    previews.push({
      row_index: row.row_index,
      roll_number: roll,
      full_name: displayName || rawName,
      admission_no: adm,
      is_auto_admission_no: isAutoAdmission,
      registration_no: reg,
      gender: row.gender || null,
      contact: row.contact || null,
      status: rowStatus,
      matched_student_id: matchedStudentId,
      matched_student_name: matchedStudentName,
      old_roll_number: oldRollNumber,
      diff_badge: diffBadge,
      error_message: errorMessage,
      warning_message: warningMessage,
      existing_student_id: existingStudentId || currentOccupantAtRoll?.student_id || null,
      existing_student_name: existingStudentName || currentOccupantAtRoll?.full_name || null,
      existing_admission_no: existingAdmissionNo || currentOccupantAtRoll?.admission_no || null,
      match_confidence: matchConfidence,
    })
  })

  // Group into structural categories
  const bucket1: ValidationRowPreview[] = []
  const bucket2: ValidationRowPreview[] = []
  const bucket3: ValidationRowPreview[] = []

  previews.forEach((p) => {
    if (mode === 'clean_slate') {
      if (p.status === 'error') {
        bucket2.push(p)
      } else {
        bucket1.push(p)
      }
    } else {
      if (p.status === 'valid_retained' || p.status === 'valid_roll_update') {
        bucket1.push(p)
      } else if (p.status === 'review_needed' || p.status === 'warning' || p.status === 'error') {
        bucket2.push(p)
      } else {
        bucket3.push(p)
      }
    }
  })

  // Unmatched currently enrolled students who are absent from the upload
  const bucket4: UnmatchedExistingStudent[] = (context.existingCohortStudents || []).filter(
    (s) => !matchedStudentIdsInUpload.has(s.student_id)
  )

  const canCommit = errorCount === 0 && validCount > 0

  return {
    target_academic_year_id: context.targetAcademicYearId,
    target_academic_year_name: context.targetAcademicYearName,
    target_class_id: context.targetClassId,
    target_class_name: context.targetClassName,
    mode,
    clean_slate_eligible: cleanSlateEligible,
    existing_cohort_count: context.existingCohortStudents?.length ?? 0,
    existing_attendance_logs_count: context.existingAttendanceLogsCount ?? 0,
    total_rows: rows.length,
    valid_count: validCount,
    new_students_count: newStudentsCount,
    existing_students_count: existingStudentsCount,
    retained_enrollments_count: retainedEnrollmentsCount,
    updated_enrollments_count: updatedEnrollmentsCount,
    warning_count: warningCount,
    error_count: errorCount,
    can_commit: canCommit,
    buckets: {
      bucket1_auto_confirmed: bucket1,
      bucket2_review_needed: bucket2,
      bucket3_clean_new: bucket3,
      bucket4_unmatched_existing: bucket4,
    },
    rows: previews,
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\scheduling\conflict-engine.ts ===
import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'

export const SLOT_ORDER = ['t1', 't2', 't3', 't4', 't5', 't6', 't7', 't8', 't9', 't10']

export function getSlotRange(startSlotId?: string | null, span: number = 1): { start: number; end: number } {
  if (!startSlotId) return { start: 0, end: Math.max(0, span - 1) }
  const cleanId = startSlotId.toLowerCase()
  const startIdx = SLOT_ORDER.indexOf(cleanId)
  const safeStart = startIdx === -1 ? 0 : startIdx
  return {
    start: safeStart,
    end: safeStart + Math.max(1, span || 1) - 1,
  }
}

export function slotRangesOverlap(
  r1: { start: number; end: number },
  r2: { start: number; end: number }
): boolean {
  return r1.start <= r2.end && r1.end >= r2.start
}

export function timeRangesOverlap(
  start1?: string | null,
  end1?: string | null,
  start2?: string | null,
  end2?: string | null
): boolean {
  if (!start1 || !end1 || !start2 || !end2) return true
  // Format HH:MM string comparison
  return start1 < end2 && end1 > start2
}

export interface CheckSchedulingConflictParams {
  labId: string
  secondaryLabId?: string | null
  teacherId?: string | null
  teacherName?: string | null
  classSubjectId?: string | null
  classId?: string | null
  batchName?: string | null
  dayKey: string
  slotId?: string | null
  span?: number
  startTime?: string | null
  endTime?: string | null
  excludeScheduleId?: string | null
}

export interface ConflictDetail {
  type: 'room' | 'faculty' | 'cohort' | 'lab_status'
  message: string
  conflictingSchedule?: {
    id: string
    subject_name: string
    batch_name: string
    lab_id: string
    slot_id: string
    start_time: string
    end_time: string
    teacher_name?: string
  }
}

export interface ConflictCheckResult {
  hasConflict: boolean
  conflictType: 'none' | 'room' | 'faculty' | 'cohort' | 'lab_status'
  message: string | null
  conflicts: ConflictDetail[]
}

/**
 * Institutional Triple-Collision Defense Engine
 * Evaluates Room, Faculty, and Cohort double-booking across weekly academic timetable slots.
 */
export async function checkSchedulingConflicts(
  params: CheckSchedulingConflictParams
): Promise<ConflictCheckResult> {
  const pool = getPgPool()
  const conflicts: ConflictDetail[] = []

  // 1. Verify Target Lab Operational Status
  const labRes = await pool.query(
    `SELECT id, name, status, is_active FROM public.labs WHERE id = $1`,
    [params.labId]
  )
  if (labRes.rows.length > 0) {
    const lab = labRes.rows[0]
    if (lab.status === 'Under Maintenance') {
      const msg = `Booking Rejected: "${lab.name || params.labId}" is currently Under Maintenance and cannot be booked for practical sessions.`
      return {
        hasConflict: true,
        conflictType: 'lab_status',
        message: msg,
        conflicts: [{ type: 'lab_status', message: msg }],
      }
    }
    if (!lab.is_active || lab.status === 'Inactive') {
      const msg = `Booking Rejected: "${lab.name || params.labId}" is currently Inactive / Decommissioned.`
      return {
        hasConflict: true,
        conflictType: 'lab_status',
        message: msg,
        conflicts: [{ type: 'lab_status', message: msg }],
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Resolve Target Class ID if classSubjectId is provided
  let targetClassId = params.classId || null
  let resolvedTeacherId = params.teacherId || null

  if (params.classSubjectId) {
    const csRes = await pool.query(
      `SELECT class_id, teacher_id FROM public.class_subjects WHERE id = $1`,
      [params.classSubjectId]
    )
    if (csRes.rows.length > 0) {
      if (!targetClassId) targetClassId = csRes.rows[0].class_id
      if (!resolvedTeacherId) resolvedTeacherId = csRes.rows[0].teacher_id
    }
  }

  // 3. Query all active schedules for the specified day of week
  const schedRes = await pool.query(
    `SELECT 
       s.id,
       s.lab_id,
       s.teacher_id,
       s.subject_name,
       s.batch_name,
       s.slot_id,
       s.span,
       s.start_time,
       s.end_time,
       s.metadata,
       s.class_subject_id,
       cs.class_id
     FROM public.schedules s
     LEFT JOIN public.class_subjects cs ON cs.id = s.class_subject_id
     WHERE LOWER(s.day_key) = LOWER($1)
       AND s.status != 'cancelled'`,
    [params.dayKey]
  )

  const targetRange = getSlotRange(params.slotId, params.span || 1)
  const norm = (s?: string | null) => (s ? s.trim().toLowerCase() : '')

  for (const existing of schedRes.rows) {
    if (params.excludeScheduleId && existing.id === params.excludeScheduleId) {
      continue
    }

    const existRange = getSlotRange(existing.slot_id || 't1', existing.span || 1)
    const slotOverlaps = slotRangesOverlap(targetRange, existRange)
    const timeOverlaps = timeRangesOverlap(
      params.startTime,
      params.endTime,
      existing.start_time,
      existing.end_time
    )

    // Consider overlapping if either slot range or custom time window collides
    const hasOverlap = params.slotId && existing.slot_id ? slotOverlaps : timeOverlaps

    if (!hasOverlap) {
      continue
    }

    const existSecLabId = existing.metadata?.secondary_lab_id || existing.metadata?.secondaryLabKey
    const conflictScheduleSummary = {
      id: existing.id,
      subject_name: existing.subject_name,
      batch_name: existing.batch_name,
      lab_id: existing.lab_id,
      slot_id: existing.slot_id,
      start_time: existing.start_time,
      end_time: existing.end_time,
      teacher_name: existing.metadata?.teacher,
    }

    // A. Room / Facility Collision
    const primaryCollides = existing.lab_id === params.labId || existSecLabId === params.labId
    const secondaryCollides =
      params.secondaryLabId &&
      (existing.lab_id === params.secondaryLabId || existSecLabId === params.secondaryLabId)

    if (primaryCollides || secondaryCollides) {
      const conflictingLab = primaryCollides ? params.labId : params.secondaryLabId
      const msg = `Facility Collision: Laboratory (${conflictingLab}) is already reserved for "${existing.subject_name}" (${existing.batch_name}) during Period ${existing.slot_id?.toUpperCase() || ''}.`
      conflicts.push({
        type: 'room',
        message: msg,
        conflictingSchedule: conflictScheduleSummary,
      })
    }

    // B. Faculty Collision (Assigned Teacher double-booked)
    const sameTeacherId =
      resolvedTeacherId && existing.teacher_id && resolvedTeacherId === existing.teacher_id
    const reqTeacherName = norm(params.teacherName)
    const existTeacherName = norm(existing.metadata?.teacher)
    const sameTeacherName = reqTeacherName && existTeacherName && reqTeacherName === existTeacherName

    if (sameTeacherId || sameTeacherName) {
      const teacherDisplay = params.teacherName || 'The assigned teacher'
      const msg = `Faculty Collision: ${teacherDisplay} is already conducting "${existing.subject_name}" in another laboratory during this period.`
      conflicts.push({
        type: 'faculty',
        message: msg,
        conflictingSchedule: conflictScheduleSummary,
      })
    }

    // C. Cohort Collision (Student batch double-booked)
    const existClassId = existing.class_id
    const sameClassId = targetClassId && existClassId && targetClassId === existClassId
    const normReqBatch = norm(params.batchName)
    const normExistBatch = norm(existing.batch_name)
    const sameBatchName =
      normReqBatch &&
      normExistBatch &&
      (normReqBatch === normExistBatch ||
        (normReqBatch.length > 5 && normExistBatch === normReqBatch))

    if (sameClassId || (sameBatchName && !sameClassId)) {
      const batchDisplay = params.batchName || 'Student cohort'
      const msg = `Class Batch Collision: Student cohort "${batchDisplay}" is already scheduled for "${existing.subject_name}" in another lab during this period.`
      conflicts.push({
        type: 'cohort',
        message: msg,
        conflictingSchedule: conflictScheduleSummary,
      })
    }
  }

  if (conflicts.length > 0) {
    const primaryConflict = conflicts[0]
    return {
      hasConflict: true,
      conflictType: primaryConflict.type,
      message: primaryConflict.message,
      conflicts,
    }
  }

  return {
    hasConflict: false,
    conflictType: 'none',
    message: null,
    conflicts: [],
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\security\terminal-token.ts ===
import crypto from 'node:crypto'

/**
 * Institutional Terminal Token & Session Security Engine.
 * Provides HMAC-SHA256 stateless signing for 1-time handshake tickets
 * and HttpOnly workstation session cookies.
 */

const SECRET_KEY =
  process.env.TERMINAL_SIGNING_SECRET ||
  process.env.SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET ||
  process.env.NEXTAUTH_SECRET ||
  'labsync-lims-default-terminal-signing-salt-2083'

export interface HandshakeTicketPayload {
  stationId: string
  stationCode: string
  machineGuid: string
  iat: number // issued-at timestamp in milliseconds
  exp: number // expiration timestamp in milliseconds
}

export interface TerminalSessionPayload {
  stationId: string
  stationCode: string
  machineGuid: string
  issuedAt: number
}

function base64UrlEncode(str: string): string {
  return Buffer.from(str, 'utf8')
    .toString('base64')
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '')
}

function base64UrlDecode(str: string): string {
  let base64 = str.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')
  while (base64.length % 4) {
    base64 += '='
  }
  return Buffer.from(base64, 'base64').toString('utf8')
}

function sign(payloadStr: string): string {
  return crypto
    .createHmac('sha256', SECRET_KEY)
    .update(payloadStr)
    .digest('base64')
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '')
}

/**
 * Creates a stateless 60-second single-use handshake ticket.
 */
export function createHandshakeTicket(
  workstation: {
    id: string
    label: string
    machine_guid: string
  },
  ttlSeconds: number = 60
): string {
  const now = Date.now()
  const payload: HandshakeTicketPayload = {
    stationId: workstation.id,
    stationCode: workstation.label,
    machineGuid: workstation.machine_guid,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + ttlSeconds * 1000, // configurable single-use window (default 60s)
  }

  const encodedPayload = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify(payload))
  const signature = sign(encodedPayload)
  return `${encodedPayload}.${signature}`
}

// Anti-replay cache: tracks redeemed ticket signatures for their 60s window
const REDEEMED_TICKETS = new Map<string, number>()

function pruneExpiredRedeemedTickets() {
  const now = Date.now()
  for (const [sig, exp] of REDEEMED_TICKETS.entries()) {
    if (now > exp) {
      REDEEMED_TICKETS.delete(sig)
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Verifies a 60-second handshake ticket.
 * Enforces HMAC timing-safe validation, expiration check, and anti-replay defense.
 */
export function verifyHandshakeTicket(ticket: string): {
  valid: boolean
  payload?: HandshakeTicketPayload
  reason?: string
} {
  if (!ticket || !ticket.includes('.')) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Malformed ticket format' }
  }

  const [encodedPayload, receivedSignature] = ticket.split('.')
  if (!encodedPayload || !receivedSignature) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Missing payload or signature' }
  }

  const expectedSignature = sign(encodedPayload)

  // Timing-safe signature comparison
  const receivedBuf = Buffer.from(receivedSignature)
  const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(expectedSignature)

  if (
    receivedBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length ||
    !crypto.timingSafeEqual(receivedBuf, expectedBuf)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Invalid cryptographic signature' }
  }

  // Anti-replay check: ensure ticket has not already been redeemed
  pruneExpiredRedeemedTickets()
  if (REDEEMED_TICKETS.has(receivedSignature)) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Single-use handshake ticket has already been redeemed' }
  }

  try {
    const jsonStr = base64UrlDecode(encodedPayload)
    const payload: HandshakeTicketPayload = JSON.parse(jsonStr)

    // Check expiration
    if (Date.now() > payload.exp) {
      return { valid: false, reason: 'Handshake ticket has expired (valid for 60s)' }
    }

    // Mark ticket as redeemed
    REDEEMED_TICKETS.set(receivedSignature, payload.exp)

    return { valid: true, payload }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return { valid: false, reason: `Failed to decode payload: ${err.message}` }
  }
}

/**
 * Creates a signed HttpOnly session token representing the physical workstation.
 */
export function createTerminalSessionToken(workstation: {
  id: string
  label: string
  machine_guid: string
}): string {
  const payload: TerminalSessionPayload = {
    stationId: workstation.id,
    stationCode: workstation.label,
    machineGuid: workstation.machine_guid,
    issuedAt: Date.now(),
  }

  const encodedPayload = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify(payload))
  const signature = sign(encodedPayload)
  return `${encodedPayload}.${signature}`
}

/**
 * Verifies a workstation session token extracted from HttpOnly cookie.
 */
export function verifyTerminalSessionToken(token: string): {
  valid: boolean
  payload?: TerminalSessionPayload
  reason?: string
} {
  if (!token || !token.includes('.')) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Malformed session token' }
  }

  const [encodedPayload, receivedSignature] = token.split('.')
  if (!encodedPayload || !receivedSignature) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Missing payload or signature' }
  }

  const expectedSignature = sign(encodedPayload)
  const receivedBuf = Buffer.from(receivedSignature)
  const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(expectedSignature)

  if (
    receivedBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length ||
    !crypto.timingSafeEqual(receivedBuf, expectedBuf)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'Invalid session signature' }
  }

  try {
    const jsonStr = base64UrlDecode(encodedPayload)
    const payload: TerminalSessionPayload = JSON.parse(jsonStr)
    return { valid: true, payload }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return { valid: false, reason: `Failed to decode session payload: ${err.message}` }
  }
}

/**
 * Timing-safe string comparison for composite hardware GUIDs.
 */
export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  if (typeof a !== 'string' || typeof b !== 'string') return false
  const bufA = Buffer.from(a)
  const bufB = Buffer.from(b)
  if (bufA.length !== bufB.length) return false
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(bufA, bufB)
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\student-photos\compressor.ts ===
/**
 * Client-side auto-crop & compression pipeline for student avatars.
 * Center-crops to 1:1 square, clamps to 256x256, and outputs lightweight base64 Data URL (~15-25 KB).
 */

export interface CompressedAvatarResult {
  dataUrl: string
  sizeBytes: number
  width: number
  height: number
}

export async function compressStudentAvatar(
  file: File | Blob,
  targetSize = 256,
  quality = 0.75
): Promise<CompressedAvatarResult> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    // If not running in a browser environment, reject safely
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') {
      return reject(new Error('compressStudentAvatar must be executed in a browser environment.'))
    }

    const reader = new FileReader()
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read photo file.'))
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image()
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to decode image.'))
      img.onload = () => {
        try {
          const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
          canvas.width = targetSize
          canvas.height = targetSize
          const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')

          if (!ctx) {
            return reject(new Error('Failed to get 2D canvas context.'))
          }

          // Calculate center square crop coordinates
          const srcWidth = img.naturalWidth || img.width
          const srcHeight = img.naturalHeight || img.height
          const minDim = Math.min(srcWidth, srcHeight)

          const srcX = (srcWidth - minDim) / 2
          const srcY = (srcHeight - minDim) / 2

          // Smooth rendering
          ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true
          ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'

          // Draw center-cropped square resized to targetSize
          ctx.drawImage(img, srcX, srcY, minDim, minDim, 0, 0, targetSize, targetSize)

          // Encode to JPEG data URL
          const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality)
          // Estimate byte size from base64 string
          const base64Length = dataUrl.length - (dataUrl.indexOf(',') + 1)
          const sizeBytes = Math.round((base64Length * 3) / 4)

          resolve({
            dataUrl,
            sizeBytes,
            width: targetSize,
            height: targetSize,
          })
        } catch (err) {
          reject(err)
        }
      }
      img.src = e.target?.result as string
    }

    reader.readAsDataURL(file)
  })
}

/** Alias for general user/staff profile avatar compression */
export const compressAvatar = compressStudentAvatar

=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\student-photos\matcher.ts ===
/**
 * Deterministic Two-Factor Student Photo Matcher.
 * Matches filenames such as "8_kaustuv_thapa.jpg", "08-kaustuv-thapa.png",
 * "14_aarav_sharma.jpeg", "8.jpg", or "ADM-042.jpg" against active class roster records.
 */

export interface StudentPhotoCandidate {
  student_id: string
  full_name: string
  roll_number: number
  admission_no: string | null
  registration_no: string | null
  photo_url?: string | null
}

export type PhotoMatchConfidence = 'high' | 'medium' | 'unmatched'

export interface ParsedPhotoFilename {
  rawFilename: string
  basename: string
  extension: string
  extractedRoll: number | null
  extractedNameCandidate: string | null
}

export interface MatchedStudentPhoto {
  file: File
  filename: string
  previewUrl: string
  dataUrl?: string
  extractedRoll: number | null
  extractedName: string | null
  matchedStudentId: string | null
  matchedStudentName: string | null
  matchedRollNumber: number | null
  confidence: PhotoMatchConfidence
  matchReason: string
}

/**
 * Normalizes a name string for fuzzy token comparison.
 * "kaustuv_thapa" -> ["kaustuv", "thapa"]
 * "Aarav Sharma (Sci)" -> ["aarav", "sharma", "sci"]
 */
export function tokenizeName(name: string): string[] {
  return name
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[._\-+,/()]/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .split(' ')
    .filter(Boolean)
}

/**
 * Checks if two names have significant token overlap or fuzzy similarity.
 */
export function doNamesMatch(nameA: string, nameB: string): boolean {
  const tokensA = tokenizeName(nameA)
  const tokensB = tokenizeName(nameB)
  if (tokensA.length === 0 || tokensB.length === 0) return false

  // Exact token match
  const common = tokensA.filter((t) => tokensB.includes(t))
  if (common.length >= 1 && (common.some((t) => t.length >= 3) || common.length >= Math.min(tokensA.length, tokensB.length))) {
    return true
  }

  // 1-character typo tolerance on tokens of length >= 4 (e.g. kaustuv vs kaustav)
  for (const a of tokensA) {
    for (const b of tokensB) {
      if (a === b) return true
      if (a.length >= 4 && b.length >= 4 && Math.abs(a.length - b.length) <= 1) {
        let diffs = 0
        for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(a.length, b.length); i++) {
          if (a[i] !== b[i]) diffs++
        }
        if (diffs <= 1) return true
      }
    }
  }

  return false
}

/**
 * Parses raw photo filename into roll and name candidates.
 * Supports:
 * - "8_kaustuv_thapa.jpg"
 * - "08-kaustuv-thapa.png"
 * - "14 aarav sharma.jpeg"
 * - "8.jpg", "roll-8.jpg"
 * - "kaustuv_thapa.jpg"
 */
export function parsePhotoFilename(filename: string): ParsedPhotoFilename {
  const lastDot = filename.lastIndexOf('.')
  const basename = lastDot !== -1 ? filename.slice(0, lastDot) : filename
  const extension = lastDot !== -1 ? filename.slice(lastDot + 1).toLowerCase() : ''

  // Pattern 1: Leading roll followed by separator and name: "8_kaustuv_thapa", "08-kaustuv-thapa", "14 aarav sharma"
  const rollAndNameMatch = basename.match(/^(\d+)[\s_\-]+(.+)$/)
  if (rollAndNameMatch) {
    const roll = parseInt(rollAndNameMatch[1], 10)
    const namePart = rollAndNameMatch[2].replace(/[_\-]+/g, ' ').trim()
    return {
      rawFilename: filename,
      basename,
      extension,
      extractedRoll: Number.isFinite(roll) ? roll : null,
      extractedNameCandidate: namePart || null,
    }
  }

  // Pattern 2: Roll number only: "8", "08", "roll-8", "roll_08"
  const rollOnlyMatch = basename.match(/^(?:roll[\s_\-]*)?(\d+)$/i)
  if (rollOnlyMatch) {
    const roll = parseInt(rollOnlyMatch[1], 10)
    return {
      rawFilename: filename,
      basename,
      extension,
      extractedRoll: Number.isFinite(roll) ? roll : null,
      extractedNameCandidate: null,
    }
  }

  // Pattern 3: Name only or alphanumeric ID (e.g. "kaustuv_thapa" or "ADM-042")
  return {
    rawFilename: filename,
    basename,
    extension,
    extractedRoll: null,
    extractedNameCandidate: basename.replace(/[_\-]+/g, ' ').trim(),
  }
}

/**
 * Matches a list of photo files against a class roster using the deterministic 2-factor ladder.
 */
export function matchPhotosToStudents(
  files: File[],
  students: StudentPhotoCandidate[]
): MatchedStudentPhoto[] {
  // Index students by roll number
  const rollMap = new Map<number, StudentPhotoCandidate>()
  // Index students by normalized admission/registration number
  const idMap = new Map<string, StudentPhotoCandidate>()

  for (const s of students) {
    rollMap.set(s.roll_number, s)
    if (s.admission_no) idMap.set(s.admission_no.toLowerCase().trim(), s)
    if (s.registration_no) idMap.set(s.registration_no.toLowerCase().trim(), s)
  }

  return files.map((file) => {
    const parsed = parsePhotoFilename(file.name)
    let previewUrl = ''
    try {
      if (typeof URL !== 'undefined' && typeof URL.createObjectURL === 'function' && typeof Blob !== 'undefined' && file instanceof Blob) {
        previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
      }
    } catch {
      previewUrl = ''
    }

    // Strategy 1: Check exact institutional ID match (e.g. "ADM-2083-014.jpg" or "1641052469.jpg")
    const cleanedBase = parsed.basename.toLowerCase().trim()
    const studentById = idMap.get(cleanedBase)
    if (studentById) {
      return {
        file,
        filename: file.name,
        previewUrl,
        extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
        extractedName: parsed.extractedNameCandidate,
        matchedStudentId: studentById.student_id,
        matchedStudentName: studentById.full_name,
        matchedRollNumber: studentById.roll_number,
        confidence: 'high',
        matchReason: `✓ Matched by Institutional ID (${studentById.admission_no || studentById.registration_no})`,
      }
    }

    // Strategy 2: Two-Factor Roll + Name Ladder
    if (parsed.extractedRoll !== null) {
      const studentByRoll = rollMap.get(parsed.extractedRoll)

      if (studentByRoll) {
        // If we also extracted a candidate name, cross-verify
        if (parsed.extractedNameCandidate) {
          const namesMatch = doNamesMatch(parsed.extractedNameCandidate, studentByRoll.full_name)
          if (namesMatch) {
            return {
              file,
              filename: file.name,
              previewUrl,
              extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
              extractedName: parsed.extractedNameCandidate,
              matchedStudentId: studentByRoll.student_id,
              matchedStudentName: studentByRoll.full_name,
              matchedRollNumber: studentByRoll.roll_number,
              confidence: 'high',
              matchReason: `✓ Matched (Roll ${studentByRoll.roll_number} + Name Verified)`,
            }
          } else {
            // Roll matched, but name differed (possible roll shift or typo)
            return {
              file,
              filename: file.name,
              previewUrl,
              extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
              extractedName: parsed.extractedNameCandidate,
              matchedStudentId: studentByRoll.student_id,
              matchedStudentName: studentByRoll.full_name,
              matchedRollNumber: studentByRoll.roll_number,
              confidence: 'medium',
              matchReason: `Roll ${studentByRoll.roll_number} matched, but name differs ("${parsed.extractedNameCandidate}")`,
            }
          }
        }

        // Just roll number provided (e.g. "8.jpg")
        return {
          file,
          filename: file.name,
          previewUrl,
          extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
          extractedName: null,
          matchedStudentId: studentByRoll.student_id,
          matchedStudentName: studentByRoll.full_name,
          matchedRollNumber: studentByRoll.roll_number,
          confidence: 'high',
          matchReason: `✓ Matched by Roll Number (${studentByRoll.roll_number})`,
        }
      }
    }

    // Strategy 3: Name-only matching if roll was absent or not found in class
    if (parsed.extractedNameCandidate) {
      const candidates = students.filter((s) =>
        doNamesMatch(parsed.extractedNameCandidate!, s.full_name)
      )

      if (candidates.length === 1) {
        const student = candidates[0]
        return {
          file,
          filename: file.name,
          previewUrl,
          extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
          extractedName: parsed.extractedNameCandidate,
          matchedStudentId: student.student_id,
          matchedStudentName: student.full_name,
          matchedRollNumber: student.roll_number,
          confidence: 'medium',
          matchReason: `Matched by Student Name (Current Roll: ${student.roll_number})`,
        }
      }
    }

    // Fallback: Unmatched
    return {
      file,
      filename: file.name,
      previewUrl,
      extractedRoll: parsed.extractedRoll,
      extractedName: parsed.extractedNameCandidate,
      matchedStudentId: null,
      matchedStudentName: null,
      matchedRollNumber: null,
      confidence: 'unmatched',
      matchReason: 'Unmatched file — select student manually from dropdown',
    }
  })
}

export function isSupabaseConfigured(): boolean {
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL
  const key = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY
  return Boolean(
    url &&
    key &&
    !url.includes('dummy-project') &&
    url.startsWith('https://') &&
    key !== 'dummy-anon-key'
  )
