=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\supabase\middleware.ts ===
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server'

export async function updateSession(request: NextRequest) {
  let supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
    request: {
      headers: request.headers,
    },
  })

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL
  const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY

  if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseAnonKey || supabaseUrl.includes('dummy-project')) {
    return supabaseResponse
  }

  try {
    const supabase = createServerClient(
      supabaseUrl,
      supabaseAnonKey,
      {
        cookies: {
          get(name: string) {
            return request.cookies.get(name)?.value
          },
          set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
            request.cookies.set({
              name,
              value,
              ...options,
            })
            supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
              request: {
                headers: request.headers,
              },
            })
            supabaseResponse.cookies.set({
              name,
              value,
              ...options,
            })
          },
          remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
            request.cookies.set({
              name,
              value: '',
              ...options,
            })
            supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
              request: {
                headers: request.headers,
              },
            })
            supabaseResponse.cookies.set({
              name,
              value: '',
              ...options,
            })
          },
        },
      }
    )

    await supabase.auth.getUser()
  } catch (error) {
    // Graceful fallback for unconfigured environments
  }

  return supabaseResponse
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\supabase\server.ts ===
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'
import type { Database } from '@/types/database'

export async function createClient() {
  let cookieStore: any = null
  try {
    cookieStore = await cookies()
  } catch {
    // Called outside request scope (e.g. CLI script or background task)
  }

  return createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore ? cookieStore.getAll() : []
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          if (!cookieStore) return
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            )
          } catch (error) {
            // The `setAll` method was called from a Server Component.
            // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing user sessions.
          }
        },
      },
    }
  )
}

export type ArchiveCategoryType = 'trash' | 'graduated' | 'transferred' | 'academic_years' | 'infrastructure'

export interface ArchivedStudentRecord {
  studentId: string
  enrollmentId: string | null
  fullName: string
  admissionNo: string | null
  registrationNo: string | null
  gender: string | null
  classId: string | null
  className: string | null

=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\lib\types\maintenance-fleet.ts ===
/**
 * Types & Domain Helpers for Fleet Maintenance Operations
 * Used across /maintenance, /computer-lab, and Server Actions.
 */

export type FleetTerminalStatus = 'pending' | 'completed' | 'blocked'

export interface FleetTerminalEntry {
  status: FleetTerminalStatus
  updated_at: string
  technician_name?: string
  technician_id?: string
  notes?: string
}

export type FleetProgressMap = Record<string, FleetTerminalEntry>

export interface FleetProgressSummary {
  total: number
  completed: number
  pending: number
  blocked: number
  percentage: number
}

/**
 * Pure calculation helper: computes summary metrics from machine list and progress map
 */
export function calculateFleetProgressSummary(
  targetMachines: string[],
  progress: FleetProgressMap | null | undefined
): FleetProgressSummary {
  const total = targetMachines.length
  if (total === 0) {
    return {
      total: 0,
      completed: 0,
      pending: 0,
      blocked: 0,
      percentage: 0,
    }
  }

  let completed = 0
  let blocked = 0
  let pending = 0

  const safeMap = progress || {}

  for (const label of targetMachines) {
    const entry = safeMap[label]
    if (!entry || entry.status === 'pending') {
      pending++
    } else if (entry.status === 'completed') {
      completed++
    } else if (entry.status === 'blocked') {
      blocked++
    } else {
      pending++
    }
  }

  const percentage = Math.round((completed / total) * 100)

  return {
    total,
    completed,
    pending,
    blocked,
    percentage,
  }
}

/**
 * Natural sort for workstation labels (PC-01 before PC-10, Teacher Pod at the end or top)
 */
export function sortWorkstationLabels(labels: string[]): string[] {
  return [...labels].sort((a, b) => {
    const aIsTeacher = a.toLowerCase().includes('teacher') || a.toLowerCase().includes('podium')
    const bIsTeacher = b.toLowerCase().includes('teacher') || b.toLowerCase().includes('podium')
    if (aIsTeacher && !bIsTeacher) return 1
    if (!aIsTeacher && bIsTeacher) return -1

    return a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' })
  })
}

/**
 * Group workstation labels by Row (Row A, Row B, etc. or chunks of 10)
 */
export function groupWorkstationLabelsByRow(labels: string[]): Array<{
  rowLabel: string
  items: string[]
}> {
  const sorted = sortWorkstationLabels(labels)
  const map: Map<string, string[]> = new Map()

  for (const label of sorted) {
    // If label starts with PC- and we want to group by chunks or known pattern
    const lower = label.toLowerCase()
    let group = 'Fleet'
    if (lower.includes('teacher') || lower.includes('podium')) {
      group = 'Teacher Pod'
    } else {
      // Check if standard PC-XX number
      const numMatch = label.match(/\d+/)
      if (numMatch) {
        const num = parseInt(numMatch[0], 10)
        if (num <= 10) group = 'Row A (01-10)'
        else if (num <= 20) group = 'Row B (11-20)'
        else if (num <= 30) group = 'Row C (21-30)'
        else if (num <= 40) group = 'Row D (31-40)'
        else if (num <= 50) group = 'Row E (41-50)'
        else group = 'Additional Stations'
      }
    }

    if (!map.has(group)) {
      map.set(group, [])
    }
    map.get(group)!.push(label)
  }

  return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([rowLabel, items]) => ({
    rowLabel,
    items,
  }))
}

  | 'dual_islands'
  | 'triple_islands'
  | 'u_perimeter'

export interface WorkstationPositionUpdate {
  id: string
  label: string
  position_id?: string | null
  pos_x: number
  pos_y: number
  row_label: string | null
}

export interface LayoutPresetInfo {
  id: LayoutPresetType
  title: string
  description: string
  capacityHint: string
  recommendedFor: string
}

export const LAYOUT_PRESETS: LayoutPresetInfo[] = [
  {
    id: 'official_dual_wing',
    title: 'Default Lab Layout (48 PCs)',
    description: 'Standard institutional laboratory setup: Left Wing (Rows A–F) and Right Wing (Rows G–L) with central aisle.',
    capacityHint: '48 PCs (2 Wings · 6 Rows · 4 Cols)',
    recommendedFor: 'Computer Lab 01 standard configuration',
  },
]

/**
 * Snaps a coordinate percentage to the nearest grid increment (e.g. 1%, 2%, 4%).
 */
export function snapCoordinate(val: number, snapGrid: number = 2): number {
  if (snapGrid <= 0) return Math.round(val * 10) / 10
  const snapped = Math.round(val / snapGrid) * snapGrid
  return Math.max(2, Math.min(98, Math.round(snapped * 10) / 10))
}

/**
 * Sort workstations naturally by label (PC-01, PC-02, ..., PC-10, Teacher-01 at front)
 */
export function sortWorkstationsByLabel<T extends { label: string }>(items: T[]): {
  teacherNodes: T[]
  studentNodes: T[]
} {
  const teacherNodes: T[] = []
  const studentNodes: T[] = []

  for (const item of items) {
    const lbl = item.label.toLowerCase()
    if (lbl.includes('teacher') || lbl.includes('podium') || lbl.includes('instructor')) {
      teacherNodes.push(item)
    } else {
      studentNodes.push(item)
    }
  }

  // Natural numeric sort for student PCs (PC-01, PC-2, PC-10)
  studentNodes.sort((a, b) => {
    const numA = parseInt(a.label.replace(/\D/g, '') || '0', 10)
    const numB = parseInt(b.label.replace(/\D/g, '') || '0', 10)
    if (numA !== numB) return numA - numB
    return a.label.localeCompare(b.label)
  })

  return { teacherNodes, studentNodes }
}

/**
 * Mathematically generates new workstation coordinates based on the selected layout preset.
 */
export function calculateAutoArrangement<
  T extends { id: string; label: string; row_label?: string | null; position_id?: string | null }
>(
  workstations: T[],
  preset: LayoutPresetType = 'official_dual_wing',
  snapGrid: number = 2
): WorkstationPositionUpdate[] {
  const { teacherNodes, studentNodes } = sortWorkstationsByLabel(workstations)
  const results: WorkstationPositionUpdate[] = []

  // 1. Position Teacher Podium(s)
  teacherNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
    const posX = snapCoordinate(50 + (idx - (teacherNodes.length - 1) / 2) * 15, snapGrid)
    const posY = snapCoordinate(12, snapGrid)
    results.push({
      id: node.id,
      label: node.label,
      pos_x: posX,
      pos_y: posY,
      row_label: 'Teacher Podium',
      position_id: 'PODIUM',
    })
  })

  const N = studentNodes.length
  if (N === 0) return results

  if (preset === 'official_dual_wing') {
    const slots = generateRoomGridSlots(
      {
        sides: 2,
        rows: 6,
        columns: 4,
        rowNaming: 'letters',
        terminalNaming: 'preserve',
      },
      snapGrid
    )

    // Map canonical slots by slot index (1 to 48) and positionId (e.g. A1, C2)
    const slotByNumber = new Map<number, (typeof slots)[0]>()
    const slotByPosId = new Map<string, (typeof slots)[0]>()
    slots.forEach((s) => {
      slotByNumber.set(s.slotIndex + 1, s)
      slotByPosId.set(s.positionId.toUpperCase(), s)
    })

    const unassignedNodes: typeof studentNodes = []
    const occupiedSlotIndices = new Set<number>()

    // Place each PC directly at its corresponding canonical desk number (PC-N -> Slot N)
    // Missing/deleted PCs naturally leave their canonical slot completely empty!
    studentNodes.forEach((node) => {
      const numMatch = node.label.match(/\d+/)
      const pcNum = numMatch ? parseInt(numMatch[0], 10) : null

      let matchedSlot: (typeof slots)[0] | undefined

      if (pcNum && slotByNumber.has(pcNum) && !occupiedSlotIndices.has(pcNum)) {
        matchedSlot = slotByNumber.get(pcNum)
      } else if (
        node.position_id &&
        slotByPosId.has(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase()) &&
        !occupiedSlotIndices.has(slotByPosId.get(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase())!.slotIndex + 1)
      ) {
        matchedSlot = slotByPosId.get(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase())
      }

      if (matchedSlot) {
        occupiedSlotIndices.add(matchedSlot.slotIndex + 1)
        results.push({
          id: node.id,
          label: node.label,
          pos_x: matchedSlot.pos_x,
          pos_y: matchedSlot.pos_y,
          row_label: matchedSlot.rowLabel,
          position_id: matchedSlot.positionId,
        })
      } else {
        unassignedNodes.push(node)
      }
    })

    // For extra or out-of-range PCs (> 48 or unnumbered, e.g. PC-490):
    // NEVER put them into empty canonical 1..48 slots! They belong in extension positions aligned with columns.
    unassignedNodes.forEach((node, extraIdx) => {
      const extCol = extraIdx % 4
      const extSide = Math.floor(extraIdx / 4) % 2 // 0: left wing, 1: right wing
      const extRowIdx = Math.floor(extraIdx / 8)
      const xStart = extSide === 0 ? 6 : 58
      const xStep = 12
      const extX = snapCoordinate(xStart + extCol * xStep, snapGrid)
      const extY = snapCoordinate(100 + extRowIdx * 12, snapGrid)
      const rowName = `Row ${String.fromCharCode(77 + extRowIdx)} (Extension)`
      const posId = `EXT-${extraIdx + 1}`

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        pos_x: extX,
        pos_y: extY,
        row_label: rowName,
        position_id: posId,
      })
    })

    return results
  }

  if (preset.startsWith('rows_')) {
    // Dynamic row preset parser: rows_2x8, rows_3x8, rows_4x8, rows_5x10, rows_6x8
    const match = preset.match(/^rows_(\d+)x(\d+)$/)
    const rowCount = match ? parseInt(match[1], 10) : 4
    const itemsPerRow = Math.ceil(N / rowCount)
    const rowYStart = rowCount > 4 ? 26 : 30
    const rowYEnd = rowCount > 4 ? 90 : 86
    const rowYStep = rowCount > 1 ? (rowYEnd - rowYStart) / (rowCount - 1) : 0
    const rowNames = Array.from({ length: rowCount }, (_, i) => `Row ${String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}`)

    studentNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      const rowIndex = Math.min(rowCount - 1, Math.floor(idx / itemsPerRow))
      const colIndex = idx % itemsPerRow
      const countInThisRow = Math.min(itemsPerRow, N - rowIndex * itemsPerRow)

      const xMargin = rowCount >= 5 ? 8 : 12
      const xSpan = 100 - xMargin * 2
      const xStep = countInThisRow > 1 ? xSpan / (countInThisRow - 1) : 0
      const posX = countInThisRow > 1 ? xMargin + colIndex * xStep : 50
      const posY = rowCount > 1 ? rowYStart + rowIndex * rowYStep : 58

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        pos_x: snapCoordinate(posX, snapGrid),
        pos_y: snapCoordinate(posY, snapGrid),
        row_label: rowNames[rowIndex] || `Row ${rowIndex + 1}`,
      })
    })
  } else if (preset === 'dual_islands') {
    // Dual Islands: Left Pod and Right Pod with central walking aisle
    const rowCount = Math.max(3, Math.ceil(N / 8))
    const halfCount = Math.ceil(N / 2)
    const rowYStart = 28
    const rowYEnd = 88
    const rowYStep = (rowYEnd - rowYStart) / Math.max(1, rowCount - 1)

    studentNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      const isLeft = idx < halfCount
      const islandIndex = isLeft ? idx : idx - halfCount
      const islandTotal = isLeft ? halfCount : N - halfCount
      const colsPerIsland = Math.ceil(islandTotal / rowCount)

      const rowIndex = Math.min(rowCount - 1, Math.floor(islandIndex / colsPerIsland))
      const colIndex = islandIndex % colsPerIsland

      const xStart = isLeft ? 8 : 62
      const xEnd = isLeft ? 38 : 92
      const xStep = colsPerIsland > 1 ? (xEnd - xStart) / (colsPerIsland - 1) : 0
      const posX = colsPerIsland > 1 ? xStart + colIndex * xStep : (xStart + xEnd) / 2
      const posY = rowYStart + rowIndex * rowYStep

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        pos_x: snapCoordinate(posX, snapGrid),
        pos_y: snapCoordinate(posY, snapGrid),
        row_label: `${isLeft ? 'Left Pod' : 'Right Pod'} (Row ${rowIndex + 1})`,
      })
    })
  } else if (preset === 'triple_islands') {
    // Triple Islands: Left, Center, Right pods with 2 walking corridors
    const rowCount = Math.max(3, Math.ceil(N / 12))
    const thirdCount = Math.ceil(N / 3)
    const rowYStart = 28
    const rowYEnd = 88
    const rowYStep = (rowYEnd - rowYStart) / Math.max(1, rowCount - 1)

    studentNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      const podIndex = Math.min(2, Math.floor(idx / thirdCount))
      const localIdx = idx - podIndex * thirdCount
      const podTotal = podIndex === 2 ? N - 2 * thirdCount : thirdCount
      const colsPerPod = Math.ceil(podTotal / rowCount)

      const rowIndex = Math.min(rowCount - 1, Math.floor(localIdx / colsPerPod))
      const colIndex = localIdx % colsPerPod

      const xSpans = [
        { start: 8, end: 28, name: 'Left Pod' },
        { start: 40, end: 60, name: 'Center Pod' },
        { start: 72, end: 92, name: 'Right Pod' },
      ]
      const { start: xStart, end: xEnd, name: podName } = xSpans[podIndex]
      const xStep = colsPerPod > 1 ? (xEnd - xStart) / (colsPerPod - 1) : 0
      const posX = colsPerPod > 1 ? xStart + colIndex * xStep : (xStart + xEnd) / 2
      const posY = rowYStart + rowIndex * rowYStep

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        pos_x: snapCoordinate(posX, snapGrid),
        pos_y: snapCoordinate(posY, snapGrid),
        row_label: `${podName} (Row ${rowIndex + 1})`,
      })
    })
  } else if (preset === 'u_perimeter') {
    // U-Shape Perimeter (Left wall, Back wall, Right wall)
    const leftCount = Math.floor(N * 0.35)
    const rightCount = Math.floor(N * 0.35)
    const backCount = N - leftCount - rightCount

    studentNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
      let posX = 50
      let posY = 50
      let rowLabel = 'Perimeter'

      if (idx < leftCount) {
        const step = (86 - 28) / Math.max(1, leftCount - 1)
        posX = 12
        posY = 28 + idx * step
        rowLabel = 'West Wall'
      } else if (idx < leftCount + backCount) {
        const backIdx = idx - leftCount
        const step = (88 - 12) / Math.max(1, backCount + 1)
        posX = 12 + (backIdx + 1) * step
        posY = 88
        rowLabel = 'Rear Wall'
      } else {
        const rightIdx = idx - leftCount - backCount
        const step = (86 - 28) / Math.max(1, rightCount - 1)
        posX = 88
        posY = 86 - rightIdx * step
        rowLabel = 'East Wall'
      }

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        pos_x: snapCoordinate(posX, snapGrid),
        pos_y: snapCoordinate(posY, snapGrid),
        row_label: rowLabel,
      })
    })
  }

  return results
}

export interface CustomRoomGridConfig {
  sides: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4
  rows: number
  columns: number
  rowNaming: 'letters' | 'numbers'
  terminalNaming: 'alphanumeric' | 'sequential_pc' | 'side_prefix' | 'preserve'
  customRowNames?: Record<number, string>
  positions?: WorkstationPositionUpdate[]
}

export interface LabLayoutTemplate {
  id: string
  lab_id: string
  name: string
  description?: string | null
  config: CustomRoomGridConfig
  created_at?: string
  updated_at?: string
}

export interface CustomRoomGridSlot {
  slotIndex: number
  sideIndex: number // 0 to sides - 1
  rowIndex: number
  globalRowIndex: number
  colIndex: number
  rowLabel: string
  positionId: string // e.g. A1, A2, A3, A4, G1, G2, G3, G4...
  suggestedHardwareLabel: string // e.g. PC-01, PC-02...
  suggestedLabel: string // backwards compatibility or positionId
  pos_x: number
  pos_y: number
}

function getSideLayoutSpec(
  sideIndex: number,
  totalSides: number
): { xStart: number; xEnd: number; sideLabel: string; sidePrefix: string } {
  if (totalSides === 1) {
    return { xStart: 10, xEnd: 90, sideLabel: '', sidePrefix: '' }
  }
  if (totalSides === 2) {
    // 2 Sides (Left & Right Wings) optimized for spacious modular cubicles
    // Left Wing: 6% to 42% (12% step for 4 cols: 6%, 18%, 30%, 42%)
    // Central Walking Aisle: 42% to 58% (16% wide unobstructed corridor aligned with Teacher Podium at 50%)
    // Right Wing: 58% to 94% (12% step for 4 cols: 58%, 70%, 82%, 94%)
    if (sideIndex === 0) return { xStart: 6, xEnd: 42, sideLabel: ' (Left)', sidePrefix: 'L-' }
    return { xStart: 58, xEnd: 94, sideLabel: ' (Right)', sidePrefix: 'R-' }
  }
  if (totalSides === 3) {
    // 3 Sections (Triple Pods: Left, Center, Right) with two 12% wide walking aisles
    // Pod width = 20%, Aisles = 12% (10% even spacing between PCs)
    if (sideIndex === 0) return { xStart: 8, xEnd: 28, sideLabel: ' (Left)', sidePrefix: 'L-' }
    if (sideIndex === 1) return { xStart: 40, xEnd: 60, sideLabel: ' (Center)', sidePrefix: 'C-' }
    return { xStart: 72, xEnd: 92, sideLabel: ' (Right)', sidePrefix: 'R-' }
  }
  // totalSides === 4
  // 4 Island Pods (width 14% each) separated by 3 aisles (10% wide each)
  const spans = [
    { xStart: 7, xEnd: 21, sideLabel: ' (Pod 1)', sidePrefix: 'P1-' },
    { xStart: 31, xEnd: 45, sideLabel: ' (Pod 2)', sidePrefix: 'P2-' },
    { xStart: 55, xEnd: 69, sideLabel: ' (Pod 3)', sidePrefix: 'P3-' },
    { xStart: 79, xEnd: 93, sideLabel: ' (Pod 4)', sidePrefix: 'P4-' },
  ]
  return spans[sideIndex] || spans[0]
}

/**
 * Generates coordinate slots and systematic labels for a custom multi-row, multi-column, 1 to 4 sided room.
 * Each side has its own distinct set of rows (e.g. Left has Rows A..F with cols 1..4, Right has Rows G..L with cols 1..4).
 */
export function generateRoomGridSlots(
  config: CustomRoomGridConfig,
  snapGrid: number = 2
): CustomRoomGridSlot[] {
  const slots: CustomRoomGridSlot[] = []
  const { sides = 2, rows, columns, rowNaming, terminalNaming, customRowNames } = config

  const clampedSides = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, sides))
  const clampedRows = Math.max(1, Math.min(16, rows))
  const clampedCols = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, columns))

  const yStart = 28
  const yEnd = 88
  const yStep = clampedRows > 1 ? (yEnd - yStart) / (clampedRows - 1) : 0

  let sequentialPcIndex = 1

  // Traverse side by side so Left Pod is populated first (PC-01..PC-24, Rows A..F),
  // then Right Pod (PC-25..PC-48, Rows G..L)
  for (let s = 0; s < clampedSides; s++) {
    const { xStart, xEnd, sideLabel, sidePrefix } = getSideLayoutSpec(s, clampedSides)
    const xStep = clampedCols > 1 ? (xEnd - xStart) / (clampedCols - 1) : 0

    for (let r = 0; r < clampedRows; r++) {
      // Global row index across sections ensures each side has completely distinct row letters:
      // Side 0 (Left): r=0..5 -> globalRowIndex=0..5 -> 'A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F'
      // Side 1 (Right): r=0..5 -> globalRowIndex=6..11 -> 'G', 'H', 'I', 'J', 'K', 'L'
      const globalRowIndex = s * clampedRows + r
      const defaultRowChar = String.fromCharCode(65 + (globalRowIndex % 26))
      const baseRowName =
        customRowNames?.[globalRowIndex]?.trim() ||
        (rowNaming === 'letters' ? `Row ${defaultRowChar}` : `Row ${globalRowIndex + 1}`)
      const rowChar = rowNaming === 'letters' ? defaultRowChar : `R${globalRowIndex + 1}`
      const posY = snapCoordinate(clampedRows > 1 ? yStart + r * yStep : 58, snapGrid)

      for (let c = 0; c < clampedCols; c++) {
        const posX = snapCoordinate(
          clampedCols > 1 ? xStart + c * xStep : (xStart + xEnd) / 2,
          snapGrid
        )

        // Terminal place / position ID: strictly 1 to columns (e.g. 1 to 4)!
        // Left side row 0: A1, A2, A3, A4
        // Right side row 0: G1, G2, G3, G4
        const positionId = `${rowChar}${c + 1}`
        const hardwarePcLabel = `PC-${String(sequentialPcIndex).padStart(2, '0')}`

        let displayLabel = ''
        if (terminalNaming === 'alphanumeric') {
          displayLabel = positionId
        } else if (terminalNaming === 'side_prefix') {
          displayLabel = `${sidePrefix}${rowChar}${c + 1}`
        } else if (terminalNaming === 'sequential_pc') {
          displayLabel = hardwarePcLabel
        } else {
          displayLabel = hardwarePcLabel
        }

        slots.push({
          slotIndex: slots.length,
          sideIndex: s,
          rowIndex: r,
          globalRowIndex,
          colIndex: c,
          rowLabel: `${baseRowName}${sideLabel}`,
          positionId,
          suggestedHardwareLabel: hardwarePcLabel,
          suggestedLabel: displayLabel,
          pos_x: posX,
          pos_y: posY,
        })
        sequentialPcIndex++
      }
    }
  }

  return slots
}

/**
 * Calculates workstation placement, positioning, and preserves physical hardware asset names.
 */
export function calculateCustomRoomLayout<
  T extends { id: string; label: string; row_label?: string | null; position_id?: string | null }
>(
  workstations: T[],
  config: CustomRoomGridConfig,
  snapGrid: number = 2,
  applySuggestedLabels: boolean = true
): WorkstationPositionUpdate[] {
  const { teacherNodes, studentNodes } = sortWorkstationsByLabel(workstations)
  const results: WorkstationPositionUpdate[] = []

  // 1. Teacher Podium(s)
  teacherNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
    const posX = snapCoordinate(50 + (idx - (teacherNodes.length - 1) / 2) * 15, snapGrid)
    const posY = snapCoordinate(12, snapGrid)
    results.push({
      id: node.id,
      label: node.label.toLowerCase().includes('teacher') ? node.label : 'Teacher-01',
      position_id: 'PODIUM',
      pos_x: posX,
      pos_y: posY,
      row_label: 'Teacher Podium',
    })
  })

  // 2. If config.positions contains saved explicit coordinates, respect them directly
  if (config.positions && Array.isArray(config.positions) && config.positions.length > 0) {
    const posById = new Map(config.positions.map((p) => [p.id, p]))
    const posByLabel = new Map(config.positions.map((p) => [p.label, p]))

    studentNodes.forEach((node) => {
      const match = posById.get(node.id) || posByLabel.get(node.label)
      if (match) {
        results.push({
          id: node.id,
          label: match.label || node.label,
          position_id: match.position_id || node.position_id || null,
          pos_x: match.pos_x,
          pos_y: match.pos_y,
          row_label: match.row_label || node.row_label || null,
        })
      }
    })

    if (results.length === workstations.length) {
      return results
    }
  }

  // 3. Generate Grid Slots
  const slots = generateRoomGridSlots(config, snapGrid)

  // Map slots by slot index (1 to N) and positionId (e.g. A1, C2)
  const slotByNumber = new Map<number, (typeof slots)[0]>()
  const slotByPosId = new Map<string, (typeof slots)[0]>()
  slots.forEach((s) => {
    slotByNumber.set(s.slotIndex + 1, s)
    slotByPosId.set(s.positionId.toUpperCase(), s)
  })

  const occupiedSlotIndices = new Set<number>()
  const unassignedNodes: typeof studentNodes = []
  const extensionNodes: typeof studentNodes = []

  // 4. Match student nodes with PC numbers or position_id to slots (preserving gaps!)
  studentNodes.forEach((node) => {
    const numMatch = node.label.match(/\d+/)
    const pcNum = numMatch ? parseInt(numMatch[0], 10) : null

    // If terminal number exceeds configured room slots, treat as extension terminal
    if (pcNum && pcNum > slots.length) {
      extensionNodes.push(node)
      return
    }

    let matchedSlot: (typeof slots)[0] | undefined

    if (config.terminalNaming === 'preserve' || !applySuggestedLabels) {
      if (pcNum && slotByNumber.has(pcNum) && !occupiedSlotIndices.has(pcNum)) {
        matchedSlot = slotByNumber.get(pcNum)
      } else if (
        node.position_id &&
        slotByPosId.has(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase()) &&
        !occupiedSlotIndices.has(slotByPosId.get(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase())!.slotIndex + 1)
      ) {
        matchedSlot = slotByPosId.get(String(node.position_id).toUpperCase())
      }
    }

    if (matchedSlot) {
      occupiedSlotIndices.add(matchedSlot.slotIndex + 1)
      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        position_id: matchedSlot.positionId,
        pos_x: matchedSlot.pos_x,
        pos_y: matchedSlot.pos_y,
        row_label: matchedSlot.rowLabel,
      })
    } else {
      unassignedNodes.push(node)
    }
  })

  // 5. Assign unassigned nodes to remaining available slots
  const availableSlots = slots.filter((s) => !occupiedSlotIndices.has(s.slotIndex + 1))
  let availIdx = 0

  unassignedNodes.forEach((node) => {
    if (availIdx < availableSlots.length) {
      const slot = availableSlots[availIdx++]
      occupiedSlotIndices.add(slot.slotIndex + 1)
      let machineLabel = node.label
      const isCoordinateLabel = /^[A-Z]\d+$/i.test(node.label) || /^[LR]-\w+$/i.test(node.label)
      if (isCoordinateLabel || config.terminalNaming === 'sequential_pc') {
        machineLabel = slot.suggestedHardwareLabel
      } else if (applySuggestedLabels && !node.label.startsWith('PC-')) {
        machineLabel = slot.suggestedHardwareLabel
      }

      results.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: machineLabel,
        position_id: slot.positionId,
        pos_x: slot.pos_x,
        pos_y: slot.pos_y,
        row_label: slot.rowLabel,
      })
    } else {
      extensionNodes.push(node)
    }
  })

  // 6. Out-of-range or extension nodes placed into extension positions
  extensionNodes.forEach((node, extraIdx) => {
    const extCol = extraIdx % 4
    const extSide = Math.floor(extraIdx / 4) % 2
    const extRowIdx = Math.floor(extraIdx / 8)
    const xStart = extSide === 0 ? 6 : 58
    const xStep = 12
    const extX = snapCoordinate(xStart + extCol * xStep, snapGrid)
    const extY = snapCoordinate(100 + extRowIdx * 12, snapGrid)
    const rowName = `Row ${String.fromCharCode(77 + extRowIdx)} (Extension)`
    const posId = `EXT-${extraIdx + 1}`

    results.push({
      id: node.id,
      label: node.label,
      pos_x: extX,
      pos_y: extY,
      row_label: rowName,
      position_id: posId,
    })
  })

  return results
}

export interface LabDeskRowStructure {
  id: string
  sideIndex: number
  rowIndex: number
  globalRowIndex: number
  rowName: string
  sideName: string
  xStart: number
  xEnd: number
  y: number
  width: number
}

export interface LabDeskFloorStructure {
  sidesCount: number
  rows: LabDeskRowStructure[]
  wings: Array<{
    sideIndex: number
    title: string
    subtitle: string
    xStart: number
    xEnd: number
  }>
  centralAisle?: {
    xStart: number
    xEnd: number
    xCenter: number
  } | null
}

/**
 * Computes realistic architectural desk table surfaces and wing layouts for top-view rendering.
 */
export function getLabDeskFloorStructure(
  config?: CustomRoomGridConfig
): LabDeskFloorStructure {
  const sides = config?.sides || 2
  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.min(16, config?.rows || 6))
  const cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, config?.columns || 4))

  const yStart = 28
  const yEnd = 88
  const yStep = rows > 1 ? (yEnd - yStart) / (rows - 1) : 0

  const rowStructures: LabDeskRowStructure[] = []
  const wings: LabDeskFloorStructure['wings'] = []

  for (let s = 0; s < sides; s++) {
    const { xStart, xEnd } = getSideLayoutSpec(s, sides)
    // Add margin around workstations so desk looks like a real physical table surface
    const deskXStart = Math.max(2, xStart - 3.5)
    const deskXEnd = Math.min(98, xEnd + 3.5)
    const deskWidth = deskXEnd - deskXStart

    const sideName =
      sides === 2
        ? s === 0 ? 'Left Pod • Wing A' : 'Right Pod • Wing B'
        : sides === 3
        ? s === 0 ? 'Left Pod' : s === 1 ? 'Center Pod' : 'Right Pod'
        : sides === 4
        ? `Pod ${s + 1}`
        : 'Main Hall'

    const startLetter = String.fromCharCode(65 + ((s * rows) % 26))
    const endLetter = String.fromCharCode(65 + ((s * rows + rows - 1) % 26))

    wings.push({
      sideIndex: s,
      title: sideName.toUpperCase(),
      subtitle: `Rows ${startLetter}–${endLetter} (${rows * cols} PCs)`,
      xStart: deskXStart,
      xEnd: deskXEnd,
    })

    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const globalRowIndex = s * rows + r
      const defaultRowChar = String.fromCharCode(65 + (globalRowIndex % 26))
      const rowName =
        config?.customRowNames?.[globalRowIndex]?.trim() ||
        (config?.rowNaming === 'numbers' ? `Row ${globalRowIndex + 1}` : `Row ${defaultRowChar}`)
      const posY = rows > 1 ? yStart + r * yStep : 58

      rowStructures.push({
        id: `desk_s${s}_r${r}`,
        sideIndex: s,
        rowIndex: r,
        globalRowIndex,
        rowName,
        sideName,
        xStart: deskXStart,
        xEnd: deskXEnd,
        y: posY,
        width: deskWidth,
      })
    }
  }

  const centralAisle =
    sides === 2
      ? {
          xStart: 45.5,
          xEnd: 54.5,
          xCenter: 50,
        }
      : null

  return {
    sidesCount: sides,
    rows: rowStructures,
    wings,
    centralAisle,
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\types\audit.ts ===
export type AuditAction = 'CREATE' | 'UPDATE' | 'DELETE' | 'RESET' | 'OVERRIDE' | 'PROMOTION_BATCH' | 'PROMOTION_ROLLBACK'

export type AuditEntityType =
  | 'lab'
  | 'schedule'
  | 'user_role'
  | 'incident_category'
  | 'curriculum'
  | 'policy'
  | 'infrastructure'
  | 'student'
  | 'roster'
  | 'cohort_promotion'
  | 'workstation'
  | 'workstation_issue'
  | 'seat_plan'
  | 'attendance'
  | 'academic_year'

export interface AuditStructuredDiff {
  before?: Record<string, any> | null
  after?: Record<string, any> | null
  bulk_overflow?: boolean
  summary?: string
  count?: number
}

export interface AuditLogItem {
  id: string
  created_at: string
  actor_id: string | null
  actor_name: string
  actor_role: string
  action: AuditAction
  entity_type: AuditEntityType
  entity_id: string | null
  entity_label: string
  changes: AuditStructuredDiff | null
  metadata: Record<string, any>
}

export interface AuditLogFilterParams {
  entityType?: string
  action?: string
  search?: string
  page?: number
  limit?: number
}

export interface AuditLogResponse {
  logs: AuditLogItem[]
  totalCount: number
  page: number
  totalPages: number
  hasMore: boolean
}

export interface LogAuditEventParams {
  action: AuditAction
  entityType: AuditEntityType
  entityId?: string | null
  entityLabel: string
  before?: Record<string, any> | null
  after?: Record<string, any> | null
  metadata?: Record<string, any>
}

=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\types\report-spec.ts ===
/**
 * Unified Record Report Specification Architecture
 * Single source of truth for UI Tables, Print Registers, XLSX Workbooks, and CSV Exports.
 */

export type DataClassification = 'structured' | 'narrative' | 'metric' | 'badge'
export type TextAlignment = 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
export type PrintDensityMode = 'detailed' | 'compact'

export interface ColumnSpec<T> {
  id: string
  key: keyof T | string
  label: string
  classification: DataClassification
  align: TextAlignment

  // Content length expectations
  typicalLength: number
  maxLength: number

  // Database mapping & Sorting metadata
  sortable?: boolean
  dbColumn?: string
  foreignTable?: string

  // Column-level filter configuration
  filterType?: 'text' | 'select' | 'none'
  filterOptions?: Array<{ label: string; value: string }>

  // Surface 1: Interactive On-Screen UI Table
  ui: {
    widthClass: string
    truncateChars?: number
  }

  // Surface 2: Certified Print Register
  print: {
    detailed: {
      widthPercent: number
      maxChars: number
    }
    compact: {
      widthPercent: number
      maxChars: number
    }
  }

  // Surfaces 3 & 4: Spreadsheet Exports
  export: {
    xlsxWidth: number
    csvHeader: string
    alwaysFullText: true
  }

  // Canonical Formatter & Accessor
  accessor: (record: T) => {
    raw: any
    displayText: string
    subText?: string | null
    footnoteText?: string | null
  }
}

export interface RecordReportSpec<T> {
  reportId: 'practical_logs' | 'lab_incidents' | 'maintenance_logs'
  title: string
  subtitle: string
  badgeCode: string
  columns: ColumnSpec<T>[]
}

/**
 * Unified Filter Interface consumed across UI, Print, and Exports
 */
export interface UnifiedReportFilters {
  // Coarse scope filters
  academic_year_id?: string
  lab_id?: string
  date_from?: string
  date_to?: string
  date?: string
  status?: string
  search?: string

  // Server-side Pagination
  page?: number
  pageSize?: number

  // Server-side Sorting
  sortKey?: string
  sortDirection?: 'asc' | 'desc'

  // Practical-specific
  batch_group?: string
  subject_name?: string
  teacher_id?: string
  teacher_name?: string
  period_label?: string
  remarks?: string
  groupBy?: string

  // Incident-specific
  severity?: string
  incident_type?: string
  equipment_name?: string
  title?: string

  // Maintenance-specific
  asset_identifier?: string
  plan_category?: string
  technician_id?: string
  maintenance_status?: string

  // Print density preference
  density?: PrintDensityMode

  // WYSIWYG selection and column projection
  cols?: string
  selected?: string
  selKey?: string
  selectedAll?: boolean
}

/**
 * Footnote entry for printed audit register annex
 */
export interface FootnoteEntry {
  index: number
  rowIdentifier: string
  fieldName: string
  fullText: string
}

=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\middleware.ts ===
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server'

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({
    request: {
      headers: request.headers,
    },
  })

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL
  const supabaseAnonKey =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY ||
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY

  // If Supabase is not configured, allow pass-through for development
  if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseAnonKey || supabaseUrl.includes('dummy-project')) {
    return response
  }

  try {
    const supabase = createServerClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll()
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
          response = NextResponse.next({
            request: {
              headers: request.headers,
            },
          })
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            response.cookies.set({
              name,
              value,
              ...options,
            })
          )
        },
      },
    })

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

    const { pathname } = request.nextUrl

    // 1. If user is authenticated:
    if (user) {
      // Fast path for Server Action RPCs: authenticated actions handle their own granular RBAC server-side
      if (request.headers.has('next-action')) {
        return response
      }

      // Query user profile to verify active status and role on page navigations
      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('role, is_active')
        .eq('id', user.id)
        .single()

      const isActive = (profile as any)?.is_active ?? true
      const role = (profile as any)?.role || 'teacher'

      // Check if user is deactivated
      if (!isActive) {
        if (request.headers.has('next-action')) {
          return new NextResponse(
            JSON.stringify({ error: 'Account deactivated' }),
            { status: 403, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }
          )
        }
        const redirectUrl = new URL('/login?error=account_deactivated', request.url)
        return NextResponse.redirect(redirectUrl)
      }

      // Check Super Admin route protection
      if (pathname.startsWith('/admin')) {
        if (role !== 'super_admin') {
          // Unauthorized: redirect to dashboard root
          if (request.headers.has('next-action')) {
            return new NextResponse(
              JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized admin access' }),
              { status: 403, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }
            )
          }
          const redirectUrl = new URL('/?error=unauthorized_admin_access', request.url)
          return NextResponse.redirect(redirectUrl)
        }
      }

      // If user is already authenticated and visits /login, redirect to dashboard
      if (pathname === '/login') {
        const redirectUrl = new URL('/', request.url)
        return NextResponse.redirect(redirectUrl)
      }
    } else {
      // 2. If user is unauthenticated:
      // Protect internal pages
      const isProtected =
        pathname === '/' ||
        pathname.startsWith('/admin') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/incidents') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/records') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/schedules') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/logs') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/maintenance') ||
        pathname.startsWith('/print')

      if (isProtected) {
        // Do not redirect Server Action RPC requests to HTML login page (avoids Flight parsing errors)
        if (request.headers.has('next-action')) {
          return new NextResponse(
            JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized. Please sign in.' }),
            { status: 401, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }
          )
        }

        const redirectUrl = new URL(
          `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`,
          request.url
        )
        return NextResponse.redirect(redirectUrl)
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // Fail-open gracefully on transient edge network errors
    console.warn('Middleware auth verification error:', error)
  }

  return response
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    /*
     * Match all request paths except for:
     * - _next/static (static files)
     * - _next/image (image optimization files)
     * - favicon.ico (favicon file)
     * - images / icons
     */
    '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp)$).*)',
  ],
}
=== FILE: D:\Projects\LMR\PRD.md ===
# Product Requirements Document (PRD)
## Project Name: LabSync — Institutional Laboratory Management System (LIMS / LMR)

- **Target Persona**: Lab In-Charge, Department Heads (HOD), Subject Instructors, and Institutional Super Admins at academic colleges and higher secondary institutions (Classes 11 & 12, undergraduate science & engineering laboratories in Nepal).
- **Primary Tech Stack**: Next.js 16+ (App Router, React 19, Server Components & Server Actions), TypeScript (Strict Mode), Tailwind CSS, Radix UI Primitives, Supabase PostgreSQL, ExcelJS.
- **Calendar Support**: Dual Gregorian (AD) and Bikram Sambat (BS) Nepali Calendar Engine (`nepali-date-converter`, `lib/nepali-calendar.ts`).
- **Design Theme**: Deep Obsidian & Midnight Slate (Dark: Canvas `#0B0F19`, Surface `#101726`) / Pearl Slate (Light: `#F8FAFC`) with specular milled chamfer highlights, layered `.glass-card` surfaces, and tactile micro-physics.

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Academic science and technology laboratories (Computer, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, and Electronics) frequently struggle with manual paper logbooks, session slot collisions, untracked equipment damage, ad-hoc maintenance neglect, and cumbersome attendance tallying during institutional handovers.

**LabSync LIMS** digitizes and modernizes laboratory operations through a single, cohesive institutional platform:
1. **Academic Practical Operations**: Real-time Sunday–Friday timetable routines, Class 11/12 batch attendance tallying, syllabus progress tracking, and dual sign-off (Subject Teacher + Lab In-Charge audit verification).
2. **Interactive CAD Floor Plan & Workstation Cockpit**: Clamped pan/zoom CAD floor viewport, 34-node minimap radar, chamfered living LCD nodes with status LEDs, high-speed roll-call accelerators (`Jump-to-Roll`, `Mark All Present`, `Invert`), and quick station popovers.
3. **Laboratory Infrastructure & Maintenance**: Interactive visual matrix inspections, recurring maintenance SOP routines (thermal paste, deep PC cleaning, BIOS updates, glassware checks), automated due-date reminders, and digital service certification.
4. **Institutional Reporting & Board Exam Intelligence**: Canonical PostgreSQL queries (zero shadow databases) computing NEB 75% practical attendance compliance, syllabus completion quotas, faculty dual sign-off rate, 36 hr/wk lab room occupancy, and workstation MTTR/uptime hotspots.
5. **Triple-Collision Defense & Audit Ledger**: Pre-flight collision engine preventing Room, Faculty, and Student Cohort double-booking across weekly timetable and ad-hoc practical slots, coupled with an immutable structured JSONB diff audit ledger.
6. **Multi-Year Academic Lifecycle & Cohort Promotion**: Multi-year student roll management, atomic cohort promotion from Grade 11 to Grade 12 with repeater and transfer exceptions, and database status check constraint enforcement.
7. **Institutional Handover & Multi-Format Exports**: Official NEB Board Exam styled `.xlsx` workbooks with three-tier signature blocks, raw CSV exports, and high-fidelity A4 landscape print sheets (`@media print` protected).

---

## 2. User Roles & Permissions (RBAC)

Role-based access control is strictly enforced at the server action and UI component layers (`lib/permissions.ts`, `hooks/use-user-scope.ts`):

| Role | Access Level | Primary Focus & Permissions |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Super Admin** (`super_admin`) | Full Root Access | User approvals & credential management, role assignments, system reset, timetable configuration, maintenance routine presets, academic year lifecycle & cohort promotions, institution settings, guarded by the **Precaution Active** safety lock. |
| **Lab In-Charge** (`lab_incharge`) | Laboratory Operations | Full Maintenance Workbench (`/maintenance`), CAD floor plan, workstation matrix inspections, incident triage & repair settlements, attendance verification, routine execution, and daily print sheets. |
| **Head of Dept** (`hod`) | Department Oversight | Department-wide practical audits, incident escalations, syllabus quota/pace review, board exam compliance analytics, and certified academic reports. |
| **Teacher / Faculty** (`teacher`) | Instructor Access | 1-click practical logging (`/logs/new`), Live Lab Cockpit roll-call, viewing assigned timetable slots, student attendance roll-call, and reporting equipment breakages. |

> **Note on Data Model Integrity**: Legacy "department" strings have been completely purged from the user model. Faculty and administrators are scoped exclusively via **Role Badges** and **Assigned Laboratory / Facility Scope**.

---

## 3. Database Schema Specification (Supabase PostgreSQL)

```sql
-- ENUMS
create type user_role as enum ('super_admin', 'lab_incharge', 'hod', 'teacher');
create type approval_status as enum ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected');
create type lab_type as enum ('computer_lab', 'physics_lab', 'chemistry_lab', 'biology_lab', 'electronics_lab');
create type session_status as enum ('scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled', 'skipped');
create type incident_severity as enum ('minor', 'moderate', 'major_critical');
create type incident_status as enum ('reported', 'investigating', 'resolved', 'escalated_to_hod');
create type maintenance_cadence as enum ('daily', 'weekly', 'biweekly', 'monthly', 'quarterly', 'semester', 'yearly');
create type maintenance_status as enum ('pending', 'overdue', 'completed', 'snoozed');

-- 1. PROFILES TABLE (Zero department clutter; Scoped RBAC)
create table profiles (
  id uuid references auth.users on delete cascade primary key,
  full_name text not null,
  email text not null unique,
  role user_role default 'teacher',
  approval_status approval_status default 'pending',
  permissions jsonb default '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 2. LAB ROOMS & FACILITIES (Status Lifecycle)
create table labs (
  id text primary key,
  name text not null,
  type lab_type not null,
  capacity int not null default 36,
  status text not null default 'Operational' check (status in ('Operational', 'Under Maintenance', 'Inactive')),
  is_active boolean default true,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 3. ACADEMIC YEARS
create table academic_years (
  id text primary key, -- e.g. '2083'
  name text not null, -- e.g. '2083/84'
  start_date text not null,
  end_date text not null,
  is_current boolean default false,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 4. CLASSES & CURRICULAR MAPPING
create table classes (
  id text primary key, -- e.g. '11sci', '12eng'
  name text not null,
  grade_level int not null check (grade_level in (11, 12)),
  stream text not null,
  is_active boolean default true,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

create table class_subjects (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  class_id text references classes(id) on delete cascade not null,
  subject_code text not null,
  subject_name text not null,
  weekly_quota int default 2,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 5. STUDENTS & ANNUAL ENROLLMENTS
create table students (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  admission_no text unique,
  registration_no text unique,
  full_name text not null,
  gender text check (gender in ('M', 'F', 'O')),
  is_active boolean default true,
  archived_at timestamp with time zone,
  archive_reason text,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

create table student_enrollments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  academic_year_id text references academic_years(id) on delete cascade not null,
  class_id text references classes(id) on delete cascade not null,
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade not null,
  roll_number int not null,
  section text not null default 'A',
  status text not null default 'active' check (status in ('active', 'transferred', 'graduated', 'archived', 'completed')),
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now(),
  unique (academic_year_id, class_id, roll_number),
  unique (academic_year_id, class_id, student_id)
);

-- 6. WORKSTATIONS & CAD FLOOR ELEMENTS
create table workstations (
  id text primary key, -- e.g. 'PC-01'
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  label text not null,
  pos_x float not null default 0,
  pos_y float not null default 0,
  device_type text not null default 'pc',
  row_label text,
  is_active boolean default true,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

create table floor_elements (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  element_type text not null, -- 'door', 'window', 'pillar', 'board', 'podium', 'bench'
  label text,
  pos_x float not null default 0,
  pos_y float not null default 0,
  width float not null default 80,
  height float not null default 40,
  rotation float not null default 0
);

create table workstation_seat_plans (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  academic_year_id text references academic_years(id) on delete cascade not null,
  class_id text references classes(id) on delete cascade not null,
  workstation_id text references workstations(id) on delete cascade not null,
  enrollment_id uuid references student_enrollments(id) on delete cascade not null,
  notes text,
  assigned_by uuid references profiles(id),
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now(),
  unique (lab_id, academic_year_id, class_id, workstation_id),
  unique (lab_id, academic_year_id, class_id, enrollment_id)
);

create table workstation_issues (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  workstation_id text references workstations(id) on delete cascade not null,
  reported_by uuid references profiles(id),
  title text not null,
  description text,
  severity text not null check (severity in ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical')),
  status text not null default 'open' check (status in ('open', 'in_progress', 'resolved')),
  resolved_at timestamp with time zone,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 7. MASTER ROUTINES & TIMETABLE
create table schedules (
  id text primary key,
  day_of_week int not null, -- 0=Sunday to 5=Friday
  time_slot text not null,
  slot_id text not null,
  span int default 1,
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  teacher_id uuid references profiles(id) on delete set null,
  subject_code text not null,
  subject_title text not null,
  class_grade text not null,
  default_students int default 36,
  status text default 'active',
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 8. PRACTICAL LOG ENTRIES (Academic Session Records)
create table practical_logs (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  schedule_id text references schedules(id) on delete set null,
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  teacher_id uuid references profiles(id) on delete set null,
  date text not null, -- YYYY-MM-DD
  period_label text,
  subject_name text not null,
  batch_group text,
  practical_title text,
  topic_learned text,
  total_students int not null default 0,
  present_students int not null default 0,
  absent_students int not null default 0,
  absent_rolls int[] default '{}',
  status session_status default 'completed',
  skip_reason text,
  remarks text,
  logged_by uuid references profiles(id),
  academic_year_id text references academic_years(id),
  class_subject_id uuid references class_subjects(id),
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 9. PREVENTIVE MAINTENANCE PLANS & SERVICE LOGS
create table maintenance_plans (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  title text not null,
  description text,
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  cadence maintenance_cadence not null default 'monthly',
  checklist text[] default '{}',
  affected_machines text[] default '{}',
  category text not null default 'hardware',
  is_active boolean default true,
  created_by uuid references profiles(id),
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

create table maintenance_logs (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  plan_id uuid references maintenance_plans(id) on delete set null,
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  performed_by uuid references profiles(id) on delete set null,
  performed_at timestamp with time zone default now(),
  asset_identifier text,
  work_performed text,
  observations text,
  next_recommended_date text,
  status text not null default 'completed',
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 10. EQUIPMENT BREAKAGE & INCIDENT REGISTER
create table lab_incidents (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  title text not null,
  lab_id text references labs(id) on delete cascade not null,
  reported_by uuid references profiles(id) on delete set null,
  equipment_name text not null,
  incident_type text not null,
  severity incident_severity default 'minor',
  status incident_status default 'reported',
  escalated_to_hod boolean default false,
  resolution_notes text,
  resolved_at timestamp with time zone,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);

-- 11. IMMUTABLE AUDIT LEDGER
create table audit_ledger (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  entity_type text not null,
  entity_id text not null,
  action text not null,
  actor_id uuid references profiles(id),
  before_state jsonb,
  after_state jsonb,
  metadata jsonb default '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at timestamp with time zone default now()
);
```

---

## 4. Core Feature Specifications & Module Breakdown

### Module 1: Authentication, Access Approvals & Security Shell
- **Email/Password & OTP Flow**: Supabase Auth client/server integration (`@supabase/ssr`).
- **Account Approval Pipeline**: New accounts start in `pending` approval status. Super Admins approve or reject registrations from the Admin Console.
- **Top Navigation & Global Command Palette**:
  - Prominent search pill (`Search sessions, rooms, faculty... Ctrl+K` / `⌘K`).
  - Hydration-safe state synchronization powered by React 19's `useSyncExternalStore`.
  - Live clock indicator rendering Nepal Standard Time (NPT) with active period tracking.
- **User Profile Menu**: Renders role badge, assigned facility scope, theme toggle, and secure sign-out.

### Module 2: Live Operational Command Center (`/`)
- **Live Session Cockpit**:
  - Two-state dynamic dispatcher (Active Session Running vs. Standby / Next Upcoming).
  - Concentric radar pulse pip (`animate-ping`) for real-time in-session telemetry.
  - Multi-lab horizontal switcher for admins and coordinators to inspect concurrent sessions across rooms.
  - 1-click **Log Attendance** and **Skip Session** triggers.
- **High-Density KPI Row (Zero Card-in-Card Nesting)**:
  - 3 standalone `.glass-card` elements in a `grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3` at ~76px total height.
  - Row 1: Tinted icon badge + title (left) | status pill (right).
  - Row 2: Bold metric (`text-xl font-extrabold font-heading`) on left | micro-context / progress bar on right.
- **Quick Operations Strip**:
  - Synchronized ~76px height in right column (`Print Report` + `New Log` + `Daily Sheet ↗`).
- **Three-Tier Semantic Attendance System**:
  - `>= 85%`: Success (Emerald text + emerald badge `Optimal`)
  - `75% - 84%`: Nominal (Neutral slate text + neutral badge `Normal` — zero false warning)
  - `< 75%`: Danger (Rose text + rose badge `Low Rate`)
  - `0%` / Pending: Neutral dash `—` + neutral badge `Awaiting`

### Module 3: Interactive CAD Floor Plan & Workstation Cockpit (`/computer-lab`)
- **CAD Pan/Zoom Interactive Viewport**:
  - Smooth clamping ($0.5\times$ to $1.6\times$) with cursor-centered focal scaling.
  - Multi-touch pinch zoom, middle-click and `Space + Drag` panning, and focus auto-centering.
- **Floating HUD Canvas Controls**:
  - Zoom in/out, 1:1 reset, fit-to-screen, pan mode toggle, radar minimap toggle. Strictly zero literal `+` in labels.
- **Collapsible Minimap Radar**:
  - Real-time 34-node room preview with attendance/status micro-dots and interactive draggable/clickable bounding viewport box.
- **Living LCD Workstation Nodes**:
  - Chamfered extruded bezels (`inset 0 1px 0 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.08)`), power status LEDs, glowing roll-call rings, and tactile spring click response (`active:scale-95`).
- **High-Speed Roll-Call Accelerators**:
  - Jump-to-Roll input with instant viewport pan & focal halo beacon.
  - "Mark All Present", "Invert Attendance", and live 3-tier attendance indicator.
- **Quick Station Popover**:
  - Floating 1-tap popover for attendance toggle, Tier 2 seat partner swap, and direct fault report ticket creation.
- **Official A4 Wall Seating Chart (`/print/seating-chart`)**:
  - Laminated printable room chart grouped by rows A–D with teacher podium.

### Module 4: Dual Calendar & Nepali Date Engine
- **Bilingual Gregorian (AD) & Bikram Sambat (BS)**:
  - Integrated Nepali calendar converter (`nepali-date-converter`, `lib/nepali-calendar.ts`).
  - Dual Calendar Picker component (`components/admin/dual-calendar-picker.tsx`) showing simultaneous BS year/month/day and AD equivalent.
- **Academic Holiday & Recess Detection**:
  - Auto-identifies gazetted national and institutional holidays.
  - Whole-day suspension banners automatically replace routine grids on recess dates.

### Module 5: Weekly Routine & Timetable Grid (`/schedules`)
- **Sunday–Friday Academic Week Schedule**:
  - Full support for standard Nepali school/college 6-day academic operating cycle.
- **Live "Now" Timeline Beacon**:
  - Highlights ongoing period slot with a pulsating `LIVE` badge and ambient column tint.
- **2-Line Slot Typography Hierarchy**:
  - Line 1: Subject Code & Class Grade/Batch in bold.
  - Line 2: Teacher Name and Micro-Time Slot Badge (zero horizontal truncation).
- **Consecutive Period Merging**:
  - Seamless consolidation of two continuous periods into a single extended practical block.

### Module 6: Triple-Collision Defense Engine (`lib/scheduling/conflict-engine.ts`)
- Pre-flight validation executed before creating schedules or ad-hoc practical logs.
- Guarding 3 critical conflict dimensions:
  1. **Room Collision**: Prevents double-booking the same lab at overlapping times on the same day.
  2. **Faculty Collision**: Prevents scheduling the same instructor in multiple rooms at the same time.
  3. **Student Cohort Collision**: Prevents double-booking a class/batch across concurrent practicals.

### Module 7: Canonical Institutional Reporting & Operational Intelligence (`/analytics`)
- **Zero Shadow Databases**: All metrics derived directly on-the-fly from live PostgreSQL operational tables.
- **NEB 75% Board Exam Practical Compliance**:
  - Class-wise student practical attendance tally, total session hours, present hours, percentage rate, and compliance flag (`Eligible` vs. `At Risk`).
- **Syllabus Practical Quota & Skipped Sessions**:
  - Track term syllabus experiment quotas per subject, completed sessions, and categorize reasons for skipped lab slots.
- **Faculty Practical Operations & Dual Sign-Off Audit**:
  - Teacher session logging volume, dual sign-off verification rate by Lab In-Charge, and pending verification backlog.
- **Laboratory Room Occupancy Index**:
  - Standard 36-period academic weekly capacity, booked periods, occupancy percentage, and total student footfall.
- **Workstation Reliability & MTTR Hotspots**:
  - Workstation uptime %, Mean Time To Resolution (MTTR in hours), and repeat fault hotspots.
- **Workstation Technical Biography Drawer**:
  - Interactive slide-out lifecycle timeline showing historical faults, preventive maintenance services, and past student seating history for any PC.

### Module 8: Multi-Year Academic Lifecycle & Cohort Promotion
- **Academic Year Management**: Multi-cycle support (`2082/83`, `2083/84`) with active operational cycle switching.
- **Atomic Cohort Promotion (`promoteCohortAction`)**:
  - Mass promotion from Grade 11 to Grade 12.
  - Exception handling for repeaters (retained in same grade) and transfers/dropouts (archived).
  - Enforced check constraint: `status IN ('active', 'transferred', 'graduated', 'archived', 'completed')`.

### Module 9: Preventive Maintenance Workbench (`/maintenance`)
- **2-Tab Architecture**:
  - **Tab 1: Routine Tasks & Schedules**: Active maintenance routines, cadence, last serviced date & technician, next due status badges (`Overdue`, `Due Today`, `Scheduled`), and 1-click **Record Service** or **Postpone**.
  - **Tab 2: Certified Service History & Logbook**: Searchable historical logbook with machine checklists, observations, and printable log sheet.
- **Interactive 40-Workstation Matrix (`WorkstationMatrixSelector`)**:
  - Visual grid of PC desks (`PC-01` to `PC-40`).
  - 3-State cycling on desk click: Pending &rarr; Cleaned & Serviced &rarr; Flagged Faulty.
  - Batch Action Toolbar: `Select All`, `Invert`, `Flag Faults`, `Reset`.
- **Institutional Digital Sign-Off Certificate Stamp**:
  - Institutional seal stamp rendered on maintenance log completion.
- **Zero Pricing Clutter**: Strictly focused on technical compliance (cleaning, thermal paste, BIOS, OS updates, cable routing, glassware safety) without budgetary noise.

### Module 10: Equipment Breakage & Incident Register (`/incidents` & `/records/incidents`)
- **Breakage Intake**: Equipment name, damage type, severity (`minor`, `moderate`, `major_critical`), and description notes.
- **HOD Escalation Trigger**: High-severity incidents automatically flag for Department Head / Coordinator intervention.
- **Settlement & Repair Drawer**: Lab In-Charge records repairs, replacements, student compensation status, or decommissioning.

### Module 11: Multi-Format Board Exports & Real-Time Reminders
- **ExcelJS Export Engine (`lib/exports/xlsx.ts`)**:
  - Official NEB Practical Attendance & Board Exam Compliance Register with institutional header styling, alternating row striping, auto-width columns, and 3-tier signature blocks ("Subject Teacher", "Lab In-Charge", "HOD / Principal").
- **CSV Dataset Export (`lib/exports/csv.ts`)**: Clean raw comma-separated values for school data systems.
- **Real-Time Reminders Queue (`getInstitutionalReminders`)**:
  - Derived alert notifications for critical workstation faults, overdue preventive maintenance, and unverified practical sessions.

### Module 12: High-Fidelity A4 Landscape Print Engine (`/print/*`)
- **Available Print Sheets**:
  - `/print/daily-log`: Daily institutional laboratory sign-off register.
  - `/print/records`: Filtered historical practical session register.
  - `/print/incidents`: Institutional equipment damage and hazard register.
  - `/print/maintenance`: Technical maintenance logbook and servicing certificate.
  - `/print/seating-chart`: Laminated physical room seating chart.
- **Print Shield Architecture**: `@media print` CSS rules automatically strip sidebars, command palettes, theme toggles, and backgrounds, enforcing a pure white background with crisp black typography and dual signature blocks.

### Module 13: Institutional Admin Console & Safety Lock (`/admin`)
- **Tab 1: User Access & Approvals**: Manage registrations, assign roles, reset credentials.
- **Tab 2: Master Timetable Routine Manager**: Edit weekly schedule periods, map subject codes to faculty.
- **Tab 3: Maintenance Routines Manager**: Edit checklists, toggle active/paused routines, and 1-click install recommended SOP presets.
- **Tab 4: Institution Settings**: Configure institution name, shift structures, and academic terms.
- **Precaution Active Safety Switch**: A mandatory toggle guard that prevents accidental edits or deletions in administrative tools.

---

## 5. Visual Design System & Interaction Standards

### Color Tokens & Palette Hierarchy
- **Dark Theme (Deep Obsidian & Midnight Slate)**:
  - Canvas: `#0B0F19`
  - Surface-1: `#101726`
  - Surface-2: `#151E32`
  - Surface-3: `#1A253D`
  - Hairline Borders: `border-white/[0.08]`
  - Milled Chamfer Highlight: `inset 0 1px 0 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06)`
- **Light Theme (Pearl Slate)**:
  - Canvas: `#F8FAFC`
  - Surface: `#FFFFFF`
  - Hairline Borders: `border-zinc-200/80`
- **Typography Stack**:
  - Heading: `Plus Jakarta Sans` (`font-heading`)
  - Body & UI: `Inter` (`font-sans`)
  - Bilingual Devanagari Fallback: `Noto Sans Devanagari` (`--font-devanagari`)
  - Monospace / Numerics: `JetBrains Mono` (`font-mono`) with global `tabular-nums`
- **Tactile Physics**:
  - Buttons compress `2–3%` on click (`active:scale-[0.97]` with `motion-reduce:active:scale-100`).
  - Cards elevate on hover (`hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-xs transition-all duration-150 ease-out`).

---

## 6. Critical Developer Guardrails & Invariants

1. **NO Literal `+` in Button Labels**: Always pair icons with natural text (`<Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> <span>Record Service</span>`), never `<span>+ Record Service</span>`.
2. **Standardized Academic Terminology**:
   - Use **"Attendance"** — never "Turnout".
   - Use **"Timetable"** or **"Schedule Grid"** — never "Matrix".
   - Use **"Logbook"** — never "Ledger".
   - Use **"Equipment Breakage"** — never "Apparatus Breakage".
3. **Safety Lock**: The Super Admin safety switch is named **"Precaution Active"**; retain this label and functionality.
4. **Zero Pricing/Cost Clutter**: Maintenance logs and plans track technical compliance only. No financial fields.
5. **Zero Department Regressions**: Never re-introduce department strings to profiles or tables. Use Role Badges and Facility Scopes.
6. **Zero Shadow Databases**: All analytics and reports must query canonical PostgreSQL tables directly.
7. **Triple-Collision Validation**: Every schedule slot and live booking must pass Room, Faculty, and Cohort collision checks.
8. **Strict TypeScript Standards**: All code must pass `npx tsc --noEmit` with **0 errors**.
9. **Next.js `'use server'` Rules**: Server action files must only export async functions. Domain constants, types, and synchronous validators belong in `lib/`.
10. **Derived Workstation Status**: Workstation operational state is derived from `workstation_issues`, not a static column.
